FILE /home/outsider/Projects/Hermes_agent/LifeOS_plugin/lifeos_hook_bridge/memory_atlas_history.py
    1: # ABOUTME: Retains Atlas observation provenance while archiving exact prior graph bytes.
    2: # ABOUTME: Bounds live history and supplies current-owner reads of verified archive rows.
    3: from contextlib import closing
    4: import hashlib
    5: import json
    6: import os
    7: from pathlib import Path
    8: import re
    9: import sqlite3
   10: 
   11: from .memory_access import MemoryUnavailable, _now
   12: from .memory_atlas import DATABASE, SNAPSHOT
   13: from .memory_atlas_insight import _request, _admit_output
   14: 
   15: 
   16: RUN_TRIGGER = 64
   17: RUN_RETAIN = 32
   18: EVENT_TRIGGER = 128
   19: EVENT_RETAIN = 64
   20: WINDOWS = ((RUN_RETAIN, EVENT_RETAIN), (16, 50), (0, 0))
   21: ARCHIVE_FILES = frozenset({'atlas.db', 'snapshot.json', 'manifest.json'})
   22: ARCHIVE_PATTERN = r'atlas/history/([0-9a-f]{64})/(atlas\.db|snapshot\.json|manifest\.json)'
   23: 
   24: 
   25: def archive_paths(identifier):
   26:     if not isinstance(identifier, str) or re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{64}', identifier) is None:
   27:         raise ValueError('Atlas history requires one exact archive identifier')
   28:     return ['atlas/history/' + identifier + '/' + name for name in sorted(ARCHIVE_FILES)]
   29: 
   30: 
   31: def publication_path(memory, relative):
   32:     if re.fullmatch(ARCHIVE_PATTERN, relative) is None:
   33:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas archives require fixed digest destinations')
   34:     base = memory.root.parent / '.local/state/lifeos/atlas'
   35:     path = base / Path(relative).relative_to('atlas')
   36:     for selected in (base/'history', path.parent, path):
   37:         if selected.is_symlink() or selected.resolve() != selected:
   38:             raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas archive destinations require private physical owner files')
   39:         if selected.exists():
   40:             info = selected.stat()
   41:             regular = selected.is_file() and info.st_nlink == 1 if selected == path else selected.is_dir()
   42:             if not regular or info.st_uid != os.getuid() or info.st_mode & 0o077:
   43:                 raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas archive destinations require private physical owner files')
   44:     return path
   45: 
   46: 
   47: def _history(snapshot):
   48:     empty = {'version': 1, 'archived_collectors': {}, 'archived_events': 0}
   49:     if snapshot is None:
   50:         return empty
   51:     decoded = json.loads(snapshot)
   52:     if not isinstance(decoded, dict):
   53:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas history requires its native snapshot object')
   54:     value = decoded.get('history', empty)
   55:     if (not isinstance(value, dict) or set(value) != set(empty) or type(value['version']) is not int or value['version'] != 1
   56:             or type(value['archived_events']) is not int or value['archived_events'] < 0
   57:             or not isinstance(value['archived_collectors'], dict)
   58:             or any(not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(row, dict) or set(row) != {'runs', 'successes'}
   59:                 or any(type(count) is not int or count < 0 for count in row.values())
   60:                 or row['successes'] > row['runs'] for name, row in value['archived_collectors'].items())):
   61:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas history requires valid cumulative archive counts')
   62:     return value
   63: 
   64: 
   65: def prepare(database, snapshot, *, force=False, run_retain=RUN_RETAIN, event_retain=EVENT_RETAIN):
   66:     history = _history(snapshot)
   67:     removed = {'sync_run': 0, 'lifecycle_event': 0}
   68:     if database is None:
   69:         return database, history, removed
   70:     with closing(sqlite3.connect(':memory:')) as connection:
   71:         connection.deserialize(database)
   72:         connection.execute('PRAGMA trusted_schema=OFF')
   73:         runs = connection.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sync_run').fetchone()[0]
   74:         events = connection.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM lifecycle_event').fetchone()[0]
   75:         if runs > RUN_TRIGGER or force:
   76:             # Keep the maximum run identifier, every referenced run, and each collector's latest run.
   77:             connection.execute('CREATE TEMP TABLE retained_run (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)')
   78:             connection.execute('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO retained_run SELECT id FROM sync_run ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?', (run_retain,))
   79:             connection.execute('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO retained_run SELECT MAX(id) FROM sync_run GROUP BY collector')
   80:             connection.execute('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO retained_run SELECT (SELECT id FROM sync_run AS run '
   81:                 'WHERE run.collector=collector.collector ORDER BY finished_at DESC,id DESC LIMIT 1) '
   82:                 'FROM (SELECT DISTINCT collector FROM sync_run) AS collector')
   83:             for table in ('source_observation', 'edge_observation'):
   84:                 connection.execute('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO retained_run SELECT last_run_id FROM ' + table)
   85:             counts = connection.execute("SELECT collector,COUNT(*),SUM(status='success') FROM sync_run "
   86:                 'WHERE id NOT IN (SELECT id FROM retained_run) GROUP BY collector').fetchall()
   87:             for name, count, successes in counts:
   88:                 previous = history['archived_collectors'].setdefault(name, {'runs': 0, 'successes': 0})
   89:                 previous['runs'] += count
   90:                 previous['successes'] += successes
   91:             removed['sync_run'] = connection.execute('DELETE FROM sync_run WHERE id NOT IN (SELECT id FROM retained_run)').rowcount
   92:         if events > EVENT_TRIGGER or force:
   93:             removed['lifecycle_event'] = connection.execute('DELETE FROM lifecycle_event WHERE id NOT IN '
   94:                 '(SELECT id FROM lifecycle_event ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?)', (event_retain,)).rowcount
   95:             history['archived_events'] += removed['lifecycle_event']
   96:         if not any(removed.values()):
   97:             return database, history, removed
   98:         connection.commit()
   99:         connection.execute('VACUUM')
  100:         return connection.serialize(), history, removed
  101: 
  102: 
  103: def complete_snapshot(snapshot, history):
  104:     # The public collector totals count archived and retained runs, rather than resetting at retention.
  105:     for row in snapshot['status']['collectors']:
  106:         previous = history['archived_collectors'].get(row['collector'], {'runs': 0, 'successes': 0})
  107:         row['runs'] += previous['runs']
  108:         row['successes'] += previous['successes']
  109:     snapshot['history'] = history
  110: 
  111: 
  112: def archive_contents(before, database, snapshot, removed):
  113:     prior_snapshot = next((row[2].encode() for row in before['sources'][2] if row[0] == SNAPSHOT and len(row) == 3), None)
  114:     if prior_snapshot is None:
  115:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas retention requires the previous admitted snapshot')
  116:     digest = lambda content: hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()
  117:     old = {'database': digest(before['database']), 'snapshot': digest(prior_snapshot)}
  118:     manifest = {'version': 1, 'created_at': _now(), 'before': old,
  119:         'after': {'database': digest(database), 'snapshot': digest(snapshot)}, 'removed': removed,
  120:         'scope': before['scope'], 'retirement': before['retirement'],
  121:         'sources': {row[0]: digest(row[2].encode()) if len(row) == 3 else None
  122:             for row in before['sources'][2] if row[0] != SNAPSHOT}}
  123:     identifier = digest(json.dumps(manifest, sort_keys=True, allow_nan=False).encode())
  124:     manifest['archive_id'] = identifier
  125:     contents = {'atlas/history/'+identifier+'/atlas.db': before['database'],
  126:         'atlas/history/'+identifier+'/snapshot.json': prior_snapshot,
  127:         'atlas/history/'+identifier+'/manifest.json': json.dumps(manifest, sort_keys=True).encode()}
  128:     return identifier, contents
  129: 
  130: 
  131: def _read_file(memory, relative):
  132:     path = publication_path(memory, relative)
  133:     limit = 2*1024*1024 if path.name == 'atlas.db' else 256*1024
  134:     if not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size > limit:
  135:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas history requires complete bounded archive files')
  136:     before = path.stat()
  137:     with path.open('rb') as stream:
  138:         content = stream.read(limit + 1)
  139:     publication_path(memory, relative)
  140:     after = path.stat()
  141:     keys = lambda info: (info.st_dev, info.st_ino, info.st_size, info.st_mtime_ns)
  142:     if len(content) > limit or keys(before) != keys(after):
  143:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas history changes during its bounded byte read')
  144:     return content
  145: 
  146: 
  147: def read(memory, scope, identifier, table, offset, *, check_current):
  148:     _request(scope)
  149:     archive_paths(identifier)
  150:     if not isinstance(table, str) or table not in {'sync_run', 'lifecycle_event'} or type(offset) is not int or not 0 <= offset <= 2048:
  151:         raise ValueError('Atlas history requires a declared table and bounded row offset')
  152:     with memory._transaction() as connection:
  153:         check_current()
  154:         contents = {}
  155:         for relative in archive_paths(identifier):
  156:             contents[Path(relative).name] = _read_file(memory, relative)
  157:         manifest = json.loads(contents['manifest.json'])
  158:         digest = lambda content: hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest()
  159:         unsigned = {key: value for key, value in manifest.items() if key != 'archive_id'} if isinstance(manifest, dict) else {}
  160:         if (not isinstance(manifest, dict) or manifest.get('version') != 1 or manifest.get('archive_id') != identifier
  161:                 or manifest.get('before') != {'database': digest(contents['atlas.db']), 'snapshot': digest(contents['snapshot.json'])}
  162:                 or digest(json.dumps(unsigned, sort_keys=True, allow_nan=False).encode()) != identifier
  163:                 or json.dumps(manifest, sort_keys=True, allow_nan=False).encode() != contents['manifest.json']):
  164:             raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas history preserves changed archive files')
  165:         with closing(sqlite3.connect(':memory:')) as archive:
  166:             archive.deserialize(contents['atlas.db'])
  167:             archive.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
  168:             rows = [dict(row) for row in archive.execute('SELECT * FROM '+table+' ORDER BY id LIMIT 100 OFFSET ?', (offset,))]
  169:             total = archive.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM '+table).fetchone()[0]
  170:         _admit_output(memory, scope, connection, rows)
  171:         check_current()
  172:         for relative in archive_paths(identifier):
  173:             if _read_file(memory, relative) != contents[Path(relative).name]:
  174:                 raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas history changes during current owner admission')
  175:         return {'ok': True, 'archive_id': identifier, 'table': table, 'rows': rows, 'total': total,
  176:             'next_offset': offset + len(rows) if offset + len(rows) < total else None}
  177: 
  178: 
  179: def read_owner(configuration, route, identifier, table, offset):
  180:     from uuid import uuid4
  181:     from .memory_runtime import MemoryRuntime, MemoryAdmissionError
  182:     from .memory_service import MemoryService
  183:     runtime = MemoryRuntime(configuration)
  184:     runtime.clear()
  185:     try:
  186:         if not runtime.enabled():
  187:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('Atlas history requires activated memory ownership')
  188:         runtime.admit({'HERMES_SESSION_PLATFORM': 'cli', 'HERMES_SESSION_ID': 'atlas-history-'+uuid4().hex},
  189:             **route, is_first_turn=True)
  190:         return MemoryService(runtime.configuration).native(runtime.context(), 'atlas_history',
  191:             {'archive_id': identifier, 'table': table, 'offset': offset})
  192:     finally:
  193:         runtime.clear()

    1: # ABOUTME: Reconciles admitted Atlas collectors through native graph transactions.
    2: # ABOUTME: Publishes the database and snapshot together with current owner checks and recovery.
    3: import base64
    4: import hashlib
    5: import json
    6: import re

    9: from .memory_access import MemoryConflict, MemoryUnavailable, _now
   10: from .memory_atlas import (DATABASE, SNAPSHOT, CACHE, _collect, _graph, source_path, admit_graph,
   11:                           capacity_projection, PROJECTION_FIELD_LIMIT)
   12: from .memory_atlas_insight import _request, _admit_output
   13: from .memory_source_review import _retirement_digest
   14: from .memory_sources import SOURCE_LIMIT, CORPUS_LIMIT

   16: from . import memory_atlas_history as history

   18: 
   19: DATABASE_LIMIT = 2 * 1024 * 1024
   20: PUBLICATIONS = frozenset({DATABASE, SNAPSHOT})
   21: 
   22: 
   23: def publication_path(memory, relative):
   24:     path = source_path(memory, relative)
   25:     if relative == DATABASE:
   26:         for suffix in ('-wal', '-shm', '-journal'):
   27:             companion = path.with_name(path.name + suffix)
   28:             if companion.exists() or companion.is_symlink():
   29:                 raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas publication requires a closed database without companion files')
   30:     return path
   31: 
   32: 
   33: def publication_paths(memory, scope, payload):
   34:     _request(scope)
   35:     if (payload != {'operation': 'atlas_sync'} and
   36:             (set(payload) != {'operation', 'archive_id'} or payload['operation'] != 'atlas_sync')):
   37:         raise ValueError('Choose declared Atlas synchronization publication')
   38:     for name in PUBLICATIONS:
   39:         path = publication_path(memory, name)
   40:         limit = DATABASE_LIMIT if name == DATABASE else SOURCE_LIMIT
   41:         if path.exists() and (not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_size > limit):
   42:             raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas synchronization requires bounded fixed destinations')
   43:     archives = history.archive_paths(payload['archive_id']) if 'archive_id' in payload else []
   44:     for name in archives:
   45:         if history.publication_path(memory, name).exists():
   46:             raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas synchronization preserves existing archive files')
   47:     return sorted(PUBLICATIONS) + archives
   48: 
   49: 
   50: def _database(memory):
   51:     path = publication_path(memory, DATABASE)
   52:     if not path.exists():
   53:         return None
   54:     before = path.stat()
   55:     if not path.is_file() or before.st_size > DATABASE_LIMIT:
   56:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas synchronization requires a bounded database')
   57:     with path.open('rb') as stream:
   58:         content = stream.read(DATABASE_LIMIT + 1)
   59:     publication_path(memory, DATABASE)
   60:     after = path.stat()
   61:     keys = lambda info: (info.st_dev, info.st_ino, info.st_size, info.st_mtime_ns)
   62:     if len(content) > DATABASE_LIMIT or keys(before) != keys(after):
   63:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas database changes during synchronization preparation')
   64:     return content
   65: 
   66: 
   67: def _snapshot(memory, scope, connection, check_current):
   68:     _request(scope)
   69:     check_current()
   70:     database = _database(memory)
   71:     sources = _collect(memory, scope, connection, insights=True)
   72:     graph = _graph(memory, scope, connection)
   73:     if _database(memory) != database or _collect(memory, scope, connection, insights=True) != sources:
   74:         raise MemoryConflict('Atlas inputs change during synchronization preparation')
   75:     check_current()
   76:     return {'database': database, 'sources': sources, 'graph': graph,
   77:         'retirement': _retirement_digest(connection), 'scope': scope.signature,
   78:         'root': str(memory.physical_root)}
   79: 
   80: 
   81: def _plan(memory, scope, connection, before, runs, attempt):
   82:     prior_snapshot = next((row[2] for row in before['sources'][2] if row[0] == SNAPSHOT and len(row) == 3), None)
   83:     run_retain, event_retain = history.WINDOWS[attempt]
   84:     retained, counts, removed = history.prepare(before['database'], prior_snapshot, force=attempt > 0,
   85:         run_retain=run_retain, event_retain=event_retain)
   86:     plan = memory._native('atlas_sync_plan',
   87:         database=base64.b64encode(retained).decode() if retained is not None else None,
   88:         runs=runs)
   89:     if (set(plan) != {'database', 'snapshot', 'graph', 'runs'} or not isinstance(plan['database'], str)
   90:             or not isinstance(plan['snapshot'], dict) or not isinstance(plan['runs'], list)
   91:             or len(json.dumps(plan).encode()) > 2 * CORPUS_LIMIT):
   92:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Native Atlas synchronization changes its bounded publication plan')
   93:     try:
   94:         database = base64.b64decode(plan['database'], validate=True)
   95:     except ValueError as error:
   96:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Native Atlas synchronization requires complete database bytes') from error
   97:     history.complete_snapshot(plan['snapshot'], counts)
   98:     snapshot = json.dumps(plan['snapshot'], ensure_ascii=False).encode()
   99:     for label, count, limit in (('database bytes', len(database), DATABASE_LIMIT),
  100:                                ('snapshot bytes', len(snapshot), SOURCE_LIMIT)):
  101:         if count > limit:
  102:             raise MemoryUnavailable(f'Atlas capacity: {label} {count} exceed {limit}')
  103:     if (not database.startswith(b'SQLite format 3\x00') or len(plan['runs']) != len(runs)
  104:             or any(not isinstance(row, dict) or set(row) != {'collector', 'runId', 'swept'}
  105:                 or row['collector'] != name or type(row['runId']) is not int or row['runId'] < 1
  106:                 or type(row['swept']) is not bool for row, name in zip(plan['runs'], [run['collector'] for run in runs]))):
  107:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Native Atlas synchronization requires declared database, snapshot, and run fields')
  108:     _admit_output(memory, scope, connection, plan['snapshot'])
  109:     capacity = admit_graph(memory, scope, connection, plan['graph'], _now())
  110:     projected, fields = capacity_projection(snapshot.decode(), 'snapshot')
  111:     capacity.update(database_bytes={'used': len(database), 'limit': DATABASE_LIMIT},
  112:         snapshot_bytes={'used': len(snapshot), 'limit': SOURCE_LIMIT},
  113:         snapshot_fields={'used': fields, 'limit': PROJECTION_FIELD_LIMIT},
  114:         snapshot_projection_bytes={'used': len(projected.encode()), 'limit': CORPUS_LIMIT})
  115:     dimensions = {name: value for name, value in capacity.items() if name != 'tables'}
  116:     dimensions.update({'tables.'+name: value for name, value in capacity['tables'].items()})
  117:     capacity['warnings'] = sorted(name for name, value in dimensions.items()
  118:                                   if value['used'] * 5 >= value['limit'] * 4)
  119:     return plan, database, snapshot, capacity, removed
  120: 
  121: 
  122: def run(memory, scope, collectors, scope_name, *, check_current):
  123:     if (not isinstance(collectors, list) or not 1 <= len(collectors) <= 2
  124:             or any(not isinstance(name, str) or name not in {'gear', 'projects'} for name in collectors)
  125:             or len(set(collectors)) != len(collectors)
  126:             or not isinstance(scope_name, str) or re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z0-9:_./-]{1,128}', scope_name) is None):
  127:         raise ValueError('Choose distinct declared Atlas collectors and a bounded native scope')
  128:     _request(scope)
  129:     with memory._transaction() as connection:
  130:         before = _snapshot(memory, scope, connection, check_current)
  131:         rows = {row[0]: row[2] if len(row) == 3 else None for row in before['sources'][2]}
  132:         runs = [{'collector': name, 'scope': scope_name, 'result': memory._native('atlas_collect',
  133:             collector=name, content=rows['LIFEOS/USER/' + ('GEAR.md' if name == 'gear' else 'PROJECTS.md')])}
  134:             for name in collectors]
  135:         for attempt in range(len(history.WINDOWS)):
  136:             try:
  137:                 plan, database, snapshot, capacity, removed = _plan(memory, scope, connection, before, runs, attempt)
  138:                 break
  139:             except MemoryUnavailable as error:
  140:                 if attempt == len(history.WINDOWS)-1 or not str(error).startswith('Atlas capacity:'):
  141:                     raise
  142:         if _snapshot(memory, scope, connection, check_current) != before:
  143:             raise MemoryConflict('Atlas inputs change during native synchronization planning')
  144:     archive_id, contents = history.archive_contents(before, database, snapshot, removed) if any(removed.values()) else (None, {})
  145:     # Preserve the exact archive before replacing the live graph. All files share one recovery journal.
  146:     contents.update({DATABASE: database, SNAPSHOT: snapshot})
  147:     after = before
  148: 
  149:     def apply(connection):
  150:         nonlocal after
  151:         if _snapshot(memory, scope, connection, check_current) != before:
  152:             raise MemoryConflict('Atlas synchronization preserves later source and destination changes')
  153:         try:
  154:             for name, content in contents.items():
  155:                 check_current()
  156:                 path = memory._publication_path(name)
  157:                 if name not in PUBLICATIONS and path.exists():
  158:                     raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas synchronization preserves a later archive publication')
  159:                 if name not in PUBLICATIONS:
  160:                     path.parent.parent.mkdir(mode=0o700, exist_ok=True)
  161:                     path.parent.mkdir(mode=0o700, exist_ok=True)
  162:                     memory._publication_path(name)
  163:                 publish(path, content)
  164:             after = _snapshot(memory, scope, connection, check_current)
  165:         except MemoryConflict as error:
  166:             raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas synchronization recheck requires publication recovery') from error
  167:         unchanged = lambda record: [row for row in record['sources'][2] if row[0] != SNAPSHOT]
  168:         if (unchanged(after) != unchanged(before)
  169:                 or any(after[key] != before[key] for key in ('scope', 'root', 'retirement'))
  170:                 or any(memory._publication_path(name).read_bytes() != content for name, content in contents.items())):
  171:             raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas synchronization changes during publication and requires recovery')
  172:         check_current()
  173:         return {'status': 'committed'}
  174: 
  175:     payload = {'operation': 'atlas_sync'} if archive_id is None else {'operation': 'atlas_sync', 'archive_id': archive_id}
  176:     receipt = memory._operation(scope, 'atlas-sync-' + uuid4().hex, payload, apply,
  177:         publication_digests={name: hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest() for name, content in contents.items()})
  178:     if receipt['status'] != 'committed':
  179:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas synchronization needs recovery or a current retry')
  180:     with memory._transaction() as connection:
  181:         if _snapshot(memory, scope, connection, check_current) != after:
  182:             raise MemoryConflict('Atlas synchronization preserves later changes before delivery')
  183:         check_current()
  184:     return {'ok': True, 'runs': plan['runs'], 'capacity': capacity,
  185:         'history': {'archive_id': archive_id, 'removed': removed, 'run_trigger': history.RUN_TRIGGER,
  186:             'run_retain': history.WINDOWS[attempt][0], 'event_trigger': history.EVENT_TRIGGER,
  187:             'event_retain': history.WINDOWS[attempt][1], 'capacity_compacted': attempt > 0}}

    1: # ABOUTME: Governs native LifeOS memory writes and reads with stable references and permissions.
    2: # ABOUTME: Keeps operation metadata separate from authoritative native fact contents.
    3: 
    4: from __future__ import annotations
    5: 
    6: from contextlib import contextmanager
    7: from datetime import datetime, timezone
    8: import hashlib
    9: import json
   10: import os
   11: from pathlib import Path
   12: import re
   13: import shutil
   14: import sqlite3
   15: import subprocess
   16: import signal
   17: from typing import Any, Callable
   18: from uuid import uuid4
   19: 
   20: from .memory_policy import CATEGORIES, MemoryScope
   21: from .memory_transaction import MemoryTransaction
   22: 
   23: 
   24: SCHEMA_VERSION = 4
   25: JOB_OUTPUT_LIMIT = 4 * 1024 * 1024
   26: HOT_FILES = {
   27:     "principal": "LIFEOS/USER/PRINCIPAL/PRINCIPAL_MEMORY.md",
   28:     "assistant": "LIFEOS/USER/DIGITAL_ASSISTANT/DA_MEMORY.md",
   29: }
   30: HOT_WRITE_LOG = 'LIFEOS/MEMORY/OBSERVABILITY/memory-writes.jsonl'
   31: 
   32: 
   33: def _digest(text: str) -> str:
   34:     return hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()
   35: 
   36: 
   37: def _claim_words(text: str) -> list[str]:
   38:     body = re.sub(r"^(NAME|ROLE|RELATION|PREFERENCE|RULE):\s*", "", text)
   39:     body = re.sub(r"\s+~(?:explicit|deduced|inferred)\s*$", "", body)
   40:     return re.findall(r"\w+", body.casefold())
   41: 
   42: 
   43: def _claim_digest(text: str) -> str:
   44:     return _digest(" ".join(_claim_words(text)))
   45: 
   46: 
   47: def _now() -> str:
   48:     return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
   49: 
   50: 
   51: class MemoryUnavailable(RuntimeError):
   52:     pass
   53: 
   54: 
   55: class MemoryConflict(MemoryUnavailable):
   56:     pass
   57: 
   58: 
   59: class NativeMemory:
   60:     def __init__(self, installed_root: Path, *, bun: str | None = None, profile: Path | None = None):
   61:         self.root = Path(installed_root).absolute()
   62:         self.physical_root = self.root.resolve()
   63:         self.profile = Path(profile or self.root.parent / ".hermes").absolute()
   64:         self.physical_profile = self.profile.resolve()
   65:         self.bun = bun or shutil.which("bun") or "bun"
   66:         self.database = self.root / "LIFEOS/MEMORY/STATE/memory-access.sqlite"
   67:         self.worker = Path(__file__).with_name("memory_native.ts")
   68:         from .memory_content_disposal import resolve_move
   69:         from .memory_moves import MemoryMoves
   70:         self.transaction = MemoryTransaction(self.database.parent, self._publication_path,
   71:             moves=MemoryMoves(lambda name: resolve_move(self, name)))
   72: 
   73:     def _boundary(self) -> None:
   74:         if self.root.resolve() != self.physical_root:
   75:             raise MemoryUnavailable("The installed root alias changed during the memory operation")
   76:         user = self.root.parent / ".config/LIFEOS/USER"
   77:         if not user.is_dir():
   78:             raise MemoryUnavailable("The native USER_DATA boundary is missing")
   79:         for name in ("USER", "MEMORY"):
   80:             path = self.root / "LIFEOS" / name
   81:             if not path.is_symlink() or not path.is_dir() or not path.resolve().is_relative_to(user.resolve()):
   82:                 raise MemoryUnavailable(f"LifeOS {name} is outside the physical USER_DATA boundary")
   83:         if not (self.root / "LIFEOS/TOOLS/MemorySystem.ts").is_file():
   84:             raise MemoryUnavailable("The native LifeOS memory tools are unavailable")
   85: 
   86:     def _connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
   87:         self._boundary()
   88:         self.database.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
   89:         connection = sqlite3.connect(self.database, timeout=30, isolation_level=None)
   90:         connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
   91:         try:
   92:             connection.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=30000")
   93:             connection.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
   94:             version = connection.execute("PRAGMA user_version").fetchone()[0]
   95:             if version not in (0, 3, SCHEMA_VERSION):
   96:                 raise MemoryUnavailable("The memory metadata schema is unsupported")
   97:             connection.executescript("""
   98:                 CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS records (
   99:                     id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, path TEXT NOT NULL, category TEXT NOT NULL, project TEXT NOT NULL,
  100:                     digest TEXT NOT NULL, chars INTEGER NOT NULL, position INTEGER NOT NULL,
  101:                     revision INTEGER NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, writer TEXT NOT NULL,
  102:                     source_session TEXT NOT NULL, source_kind TEXT NOT NULL, updated TEXT NOT NULL,
  103:                     claim_digest TEXT NOT NULL, claim_words INTEGER NOT NULL
  104:                 );
  105:                 CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proposals (
  106:                     id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, path TEXT NOT NULL, target TEXT NOT NULL, target_digest TEXT NOT NULL, digest TEXT NOT NULL,
  107:                     revision INTEGER NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, writer TEXT NOT NULL,
  108:                     source_session TEXT NOT NULL, updated TEXT NOT NULL
  109:                 );
  110:                 CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS operations (
  111:                     writer TEXT NOT NULL, request_id TEXT NOT NULL, payload_digest TEXT NOT NULL,
  112:                     receipt TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(writer, request_id)
  113:                 );
  114:                 CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS source_reviews (
  115:                     principal TEXT NOT NULL, path TEXT NOT NULL, digest TEXT NOT NULL,
  116:                     retirement_digest TEXT NOT NULL, writer TEXT NOT NULL, reviewed_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  117:                     PRIMARY KEY(principal, path)
  118:                 );
  119:             """)
  120:             if version != SCHEMA_VERSION:
  121:                 connection.execute(f"PRAGMA user_version={SCHEMA_VERSION}")
  122:             connection.commit()
  123:             os.chmod(self.database, 0o600)
  124:             return connection
  125:         except BaseException:
  126:             connection.close()
  127:             raise
  128: 
  129:     @contextmanager
  130:     def _transaction(self):
  131:         self._boundary()
  132:         with self.transaction.lock():
  133:             connection = self._connect()
  134:             try:
  135:                 self.transaction.recover(connection)
  136:                 connection.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
  137:                 yield connection
  138:                 connection.commit()
  139:                 self.transaction.finish()
  140:             except BaseException:
  141:                 connection.rollback()
  142:                 raise
  143:             finally:
  144:                 connection.close()
  145: 
  146:     def _native(self, action: str, *, source_descriptors: tuple[int, ...] = (), **values: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
  147:         if (len(source_descriptors) > 1 or any(type(value) is not int or value < 3 for value in source_descriptors)):
  148:             raise MemoryUnavailable('Native snapshots require a declared source descriptor')
  149:         environment = dict(os.environ)
  150:         environment["HOME"] = str(self.root.parent)
  151:         environment["LIFEOS_DIR"] = str(self.root / "LIFEOS")
  152:         environment["LIFEOS_CONFIG_DIR"] = str(self.root / "LIFEOS/USER/CONFIG")
  153:         if action == 'algorithm_edit_commit':
  154:             environment['CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR'] = str(self.root)
  155:         environment.pop("LIFEOS_MEMORY_PUBLICATION_JOURNAL", None)
  156:         if self.transaction.inherited_descriptors():
  157:             environment["LIFEOS_MEMORY_PUBLICATION_JOURNAL"] = str(self.transaction.journal)
  158:         environment["LIFEOS_MEMORY_INTERNAL"] = "1"
  159:         environment["BUN_CONFIG_NO_AUTO_INSTALL"] = "1"
  160:         arguments = [self.bun, "--no-install", str(self.worker), str(self.root)]
  161:         content = json.dumps({"action": action, **values})
  162:         descriptors = (*self.transaction.inherited_descriptors(), *source_descriptors)
  163:         if action == 'algorithm_edit_commit':
  164:             # The commit group must end before the enclosing HTTP relay reaches its deadline.
  165:             with subprocess.Popen(arguments, stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
  166:                     text=True, env=environment, cwd=self.root, pass_fds=descriptors, start_new_session=True) as process:
  167:                 try:
  168:                     output, errors = process.communicate(content, timeout=20)
  169:                 finally:
  170:                     try: os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
  171:                     except ProcessLookupError: pass
  172:                     process.wait()
  173:                 result = subprocess.CompletedProcess(arguments, process.returncode, output, errors)
  174:         else:
  175:             result = subprocess.run(arguments, input=content, text=True, capture_output=True,
  176:                 timeout=30, env=environment, cwd=self.root, pass_fds=descriptors)
  177:         if result.returncode:
  178:             raise MemoryUnavailable("Native memory operation failed: " + result.stderr.strip()[:500])
  179:         try:
  180:             response = json.loads(result.stdout)
  181:         except ValueError as error:
  182:             raise MemoryUnavailable("Native memory returned an invalid response") from error
  183:         if not isinstance(response, dict):
  184:             raise MemoryUnavailable("Native memory returned an invalid result")
  185:         return response
  186: 
  187:     def _path(self, name: str) -> Path:
  188:         relative = Path(name)
  189:         if relative.is_absolute() or ".." in relative.parts or not name.startswith(("LIFEOS/USER/", "LIFEOS/MEMORY/")):
  190:             raise MemoryUnavailable("Invalid native memory reference")
  191:         path = self.root / relative
  192:         user = (self.root.parent / ".config/LIFEOS/USER").resolve()
  193:         if not path.resolve().is_relative_to(user):
  194:             raise MemoryUnavailable("Native memory reference leaves the user boundary")
  195:         if path.exists() and self.database.exists() and path.samefile(self.database):
  196:             raise MemoryUnavailable("Native memory content cannot alias its SQLite registry")
  197:         return path
  198: 
  199:     def _publication_path(self, name: str) -> Path:
  200:         if name.startswith('atlas/history/'):
  201:             from .memory_atlas_history import publication_path
  202:             return publication_path(self, name)
  203:         if name in {'atlas/graph.json', 'atlas/snapshot.json'}:
  204:             from .memory_atlas_sync import publication_path
  205:             return publication_path(self, name)
  206:         if name.startswith(("LIFEOS/USER/", "LIFEOS/MEMORY/")):
  207:             return self._path(name)
  208:         from .memory_freshness import SYSTEM_PUBLICATIONS
  209:         from .memory_freshness_migration import is_system_backup
  210:         from .memory_deny_hashes import SYSTEM_PUBLICATIONS as DENY_PUBLICATIONS
  211:         from .memory_user_index_publish import PUBLICATIONS as INDEX_PUBLICATIONS
  212:         from .memory_manual_state import SYSTEM_PUBLICATIONS as MANUAL_PUBLICATIONS
  213:         from .memory_algorithm_edit import system_publication
  214:         if name not in SYSTEM_PUBLICATIONS | DENY_PUBLICATIONS | INDEX_PUBLICATIONS | MANUAL_PUBLICATIONS and not is_system_backup(name) and not system_publication(name):
  215:             raise MemoryUnavailable("This is not a journaled native system publication")
  216:         path = self.root / name
  217:         if (path.resolve() != self.physical_root / name or path.is_symlink()
  218:                 or path.exists() and (not path.is_file() or path.stat().st_uid != os.getuid()
  219:                                       or self.database.exists() and path.samefile(self.database))):
  220:             raise MemoryUnavailable("The system publication changes its permitted owner path")
  221:         return path
  222: 
  223:     @staticmethod
  224:     def _allowed(scope: MemoryScope, record: sqlite3.Row, *, write: bool = False) -> bool:
  225:         permissions = scope.write if write else scope.read
  226:         if record["category"] not in permissions:
  227:             return False
  228:         if NativeMemory._private_entity(record["path"]) and "principal" not in permissions:
  229:             return False
  230:         if record['category'] == 'project' and not record['project'] and not CATEGORIES <= set(permissions):
  231:             return False
  232:         return record["category"] != "project" or "*" in scope.projects or record["project"] in scope.projects
  233: 
  234:     def _content(self, record: sqlite3.Row, hot_entries: dict[Path, list[str]] | None = None) -> str:
  235:         path = self._path(record["path"])
  236:         if not path.is_file():
  237:             raise MemoryUnavailable("The referenced native memory file is missing")
  238:         text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
  239:         length = record["chars"]
  240:         position = record["position"]
  241:         candidate = text[position:position + length]
  242:         if record["category"] == "project":
  243:             boundary = re.search(r"\n## Appended \d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T[^\n]+Z\n<!-- source_session: [^\n]* -->\n", text[position:])
  244:             end = position + boundary.start() if boundary else len(text)
  245:             section = text[position:end].rstrip("\r\n")
  246:             if section == candidate and _digest(candidate) == record["digest"]:
  247:                 return candidate
  248:         if record["category"] != "project":
  249:             if hot_entries is None:
  250:                 entries = self._native("read_hot", path=str(path)).get("entries", [])
  251:             else:
  252:                 if path not in hot_entries:
  253:                     hot_entries[path] = self._native("read_hot", path=str(path)).get("entries", [])
  254:                 entries = hot_entries[path]
  255:             matches = [entry for entry in entries if _digest(entry) == record["digest"]]
  256:             if len(matches) == 1:
  257:                 return matches[0]
  258:         raise MemoryConflict("The referenced fact changed outside its recorded revision")
  259: 
  260:     def _validate(self, item: dict[str, Any], content: str, category: str) -> str | None:
  261:         checked = self._native("validate", item=item)
  262:         if not checked.get("ok"):
  263:             return checked.get("message", "Native validation rejected the fact")
  264:         if checked["item"] != item:
  265:             return "Native validation changed the requested fact or metadata"
  266:         if category != "project" and (len(content.split(": ", 1)[-1].encode("utf-16-le")) // 2 > 256 or "\n" in content or "\r" in content):
  267:             return "A native hot-memory entry requires one line of at most 256 characters"
  268:         return None
  269: 
  270:     @staticmethod
  271:     def _blocked(connection: sqlite3.Connection, content: str) -> bool:
  272:         return connection.execute("""SELECT id FROM records AS retained WHERE claim_digest=? AND status IN ('forgotten','superseded')
  273:                                   AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM records AS current WHERE current.status='active'
  274:                                   AND current.claim_digest=retained.claim_digest AND current.category=retained.category
  275:                                   AND current.project=retained.project)""",
  276:                                   (_claim_digest(content),)).fetchone() is not None
  277: 
  278:     def _archive_write(self, connection: sqlite3.Connection, item: dict[str, Any], *, check_current=None) -> dict[str, Any]:
  279:         path = Path(self._native("route", item=item)["path"])
  280:         relative = path.relative_to(self.root).as_posix()
  281:         path = self._path(relative)
  282:         rows = connection.execute("SELECT * FROM records WHERE path=? AND status='active'", (relative,)).fetchall()
  283:         old_text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") if path.exists() else ""
  284:         for row in rows:
  285:             self._content(row)
  286:         if check_current is not None:
  287:             check_current(connection)
  288:         result = self._native("add", item=item)
  289:         if not result.get("ok"):
  290:             return result
  291:         if Path(result["path"]) != path:
  292:             raise MemoryUnavailable("Native publication changed its declared archive destination")
  293:         if rows:
  294:             text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
  295:             old_frontmatter = re.match(r"^---\n.*?\n---\n", old_text, re.DOTALL)
  296:             frontmatter = re.match(r"^---\n.*?\n---\n", text, re.DOTALL)
  297:             if old_frontmatter is None or frontmatter is None or not text[frontmatter.end():].startswith(old_text[old_frontmatter.end():].rstrip("\r\n")):
  298:                 raise MemoryUnavailable("Native archive publication changed an earlier section; recovery is required")
  299:             delta = frontmatter.end() - old_frontmatter.end()
  300:             for row in rows:
  301:                 connection.execute("UPDATE records SET position=position+? WHERE id=?", (delta, row["id"]))
  302:                 updated = connection.execute("SELECT * FROM records WHERE id=?", (row["id"],)).fetchone()
  303:                 self._content(updated)
  304:         return result
  305: 
  306:     @staticmethod
  307:     def _archive_item(path: Path, content: str, source_session: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
  308:         common = {"content": content, "source_session": source_session or "none"}
  309:         if 'LEARNING' in path.parts:
  310:             return {**common, 'type':'knowledge', 'entity_type':'research', 'name':path.stem}
  311:         if path.parent.name == "Ideas":
  312:             return {**common, "type": "idea", "title": path.stem}
  313:         entity = {"People": "person", "Companies": "company", "Research": "research"}.get(path.parent.name)
  314:         if entity is None:
  315:             raise MemoryUnavailable("The archive reference has no supported native routing type")
  316:         return {**common, "type": "knowledge", "entity_type": entity, "name": path.stem}
  317: 
  318:     @staticmethod
  319:     def _private_entity(path: str) -> bool:
  320:         return path.startswith(("LIFEOS/MEMORY/KNOWLEDGE/People/", "LIFEOS/MEMORY/KNOWLEDGE/Companies/",
  321:                                 "LIFEOS/MEMORY/KNOWLEDGE/_archive/People/", "LIFEOS/MEMORY/KNOWLEDGE/_archive/Companies/"))
  322: 
  323:     def _duplicate(self, connection: sqlite3.Connection, scope: MemoryScope, content: str,
  324:                    category: str, project: str, destination: str) -> sqlite3.Row | None:
  325:         candidates = connection.execute("SELECT * FROM records WHERE digest=? AND category=? AND project=? AND status='active'",
  326:                                         (_digest(content), category, project)).fetchall()
  327:         for candidate in candidates:
  328:             if self._allowed(scope, candidate, write=True) and self._private_entity(candidate["path"]) == self._private_entity(destination):
  329:                 self._content(candidate)
  330:                 return candidate
  331:         return None
  332: 
  333:     def _record(self, connection: sqlite3.Connection, scope: MemoryScope, path: Path,
  334:                 content: str, category: str, project: str, source: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, Any]:
  335:         relative = path.relative_to(self.root).as_posix()
  336:         text = self._path(relative).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
  337:         if category == "project":
  338:             position = len(text.rstrip("\r\n")) - len(content)
  339:         else:
  340:             start = text.index("<!-- BEGIN ENTRIES -->") + len("<!-- BEGIN ENTRIES -->")
  341:             position = text.find(content, start, text.index("<!-- END ENTRIES -->"))
  342:         if position < 0 or text[position:position + len(content)] != content:
  343:             raise MemoryUnavailable("The native writer did not save the requested fact")
  344:         identifier = uuid4().hex
  345:         connection.execute("INSERT INTO records VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)", (
  346:             identifier, relative, category, project, _digest(content), len(content), position,
  347:             1, "active", scope.writer, source.get("session", ""), source.get("kind", "explicit"), _now(), _claim_digest(content), len(_claim_words(content)),
  348:         ))
  349:         return {"id": identifier, "revision": 1}
  350: 
  351:     def _operation(self, scope: MemoryScope, request_id: str, payload: dict[str, Any],
  352:                    callback: Callable[[sqlite3.Connection], dict[str, Any]], *,
  353:                    identity_payload: dict[str, Any] | None = None, publication_digests=None) -> dict[str, Any]:
  354:         if not isinstance(request_id, str) or not request_id or len(request_id) > 256:
  355:             return {"status": "rejected", "reason": "A bounded request identifier is required"}
  356:         payload_digest = _digest(json.dumps(payload if identity_payload is None else identity_payload, sort_keys=True))
  357:         reserved = False
  358:         try:
  359:             with self._transaction() as connection:
  360:                 prior = connection.execute("SELECT * FROM operations WHERE writer=? AND request_id=?",
  361:                                            (scope.writer, request_id)).fetchone()
  362:                 if prior is not None:
  363:                     if prior["payload_digest"] != payload_digest:
  364:                         return {"status": "conflict", "reason": "This request identifier names another operation"}
  365:                     return json.loads(prior["receipt"])
  366:                 unknown = {"status": "unknown", "reason": "The operation outcome needs recovery before retry",
  367:                            "writer": scope.writer, "request_id": request_id}
  368:                 paths = self._publication_paths(connection, scope, payload)
  369:                 self.transaction.prepare(scope.writer, request_id, paths, expected=publication_digests)
  370:                 connection.execute("INSERT INTO operations VALUES (?,?,?,?)",
  371:                                    (scope.writer, request_id, payload_digest, json.dumps(unknown)))
  372:                 connection.commit()
  373:                 reserved = True
  374:                 connection.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
  375:                 try:
  376:                     receipt = callback(connection)
  377:                 except MemoryConflict as error:
  378:                     receipt = {"status": "conflict", "reason": str(error)}
  379:                 receipt.setdefault("writer", scope.writer)
  380:                 receipt.setdefault("request_id", request_id)
  381:                 connection.execute("UPDATE operations SET receipt=? WHERE writer=? AND request_id=?",
  382:                                    (json.dumps(receipt), scope.writer, request_id))
  383:                 self.transaction.flush_publication()
  384:                 return receipt
  385:         except MemoryConflict as error:
  386:             return {"status": "conflict", "reason": str(error), "writer": scope.writer, "request_id": request_id}
  387:         except (MemoryUnavailable, OSError, sqlite3.Error, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as error:
  388:             return {"status": "unknown" if reserved else "rejected",
  389:                     "reason": str(error), "writer": scope.writer, "request_id": request_id}
  390: 
  391:     def _publication_paths(self, connection: sqlite3.Connection, scope: MemoryScope,
  392:                            payload: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
  393:         if payload['operation'] == 'local_run':
  394:             from .memory_local_runs import publication_paths
  395:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  396:         if payload['operation'] == 'local_refresh':
  397:             from .memory_local_refresh import publication_paths
  398:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  399:         if payload['operation'] in {'content_run', 'content_delete'}:
  400:             from .memory_content_action import publication_paths
  401:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  402:         if payload['operation'] == 'algorithm_edit':
  403:             from .memory_algorithm_edit import publication_paths
  404:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  405:         if payload['operation'] == 'algorithm_summary':
  406:             from .memory_algorithm_summary import publication_paths
  407:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  408:         if payload['operation'] == 'atlas_insight':
  409:             from .memory_atlas_insight import publication_paths
  410:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  411:         if payload['operation'] == 'atlas_sync':
  412:             from .memory_atlas_sync import publication_paths
  413:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  414:         if payload['operation'] == 'conduit_command':
  415:             from .memory_conduit_capture import publication_paths
  416:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  417:         if payload['operation'] == 'conduit_insight':
  418:             from .memory_conduit_insight import publication_paths
  419:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  420:         if payload['operation'] == 'conduit_initialize':
  421:             from .memory_conduit import publication_paths
  422:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  423:         if payload['operation'] == 'manual_state':
  424:             from .memory_manual_state import publication_paths
  425:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload['tool'], paths=payload['paths'])
  426:         if payload['operation'] == 'telos_file_edit':
  427:             from .memory_telos_file import publication_paths
  428:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  429:         if payload['operation'] == 'user_index_publish':
  430:             from .memory_user_index_publish import publication_paths
  431:             return publication_paths(self, connection, scope, payload)
  432:         if payload['operation'] == 'upgrade_store':
  433:             from .memory_upgrades import publication_paths
  434:             return publication_paths(self,scope,payload)
  435:         if payload['operation'] == 'hypothesis_review':
  436:             from .memory_hypothesis_review import publication_paths
  437:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  438:         if payload['operation'] == 'event_append':
  439:             from .memory_events import publication_paths
  440:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  441:         if payload['operation'] == 'knowledge_conformance':
  442:             from .memory_knowledge_conformance import publication_paths
  443:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  444:         if payload['operation'] == 'knowledge_view':
  445:             from .memory_knowledge_views import publication_paths
  446:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  447:         if payload['operation'] == 'knowledge_harvest':
  448:             from .memory_knowledge_harvest import publication_paths
  449:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  450:         if payload['operation'] == 'session_harvest':
  451:             from .memory_session_harvest import publication_paths
  452:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  453:         if payload['operation'] == 'proposal_gc':
  454:             from .memory_proposal_gc import publication_paths
  455:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  456:         if payload['operation'] == 'context_audit':
  457:             from .memory_context_audit import publication_paths
  458:             return publication_paths(self, scope)
  459:         if payload['operation'] == 'seed_pulse':
  460:             from .memory_seed import publication_paths
  461:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  462:         if payload['operation'] == 'learning_hypotheses':
  463:             from .memory_hypotheses import publication_paths
  464:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  465:         if payload['operation'] == 'recurrence_append':
  466:             from .memory_recurrence import publication_paths
  467:             return publication_paths(self, scope)
  468:         if payload['operation'] == 'learning_ratings':
  469:             from .memory_learning import publication_paths
  470:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  471:         if payload['operation'] in {'wisdom_frame_update', 'wisdom_synthesis'}:
  472:             from .memory_wisdom import publication_paths
  473:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  474:         if payload['operation'] == 'distill_synthesis':
  475:             from .memory_distill import synthesis_paths
  476:             return synthesis_paths(self, scope, payload['date'])
  477:         if payload['operation'] == 'distill_mark':
  478:             from .memory_distill import publication_paths
  479:             return publication_paths(self, scope)
  480:         if payload['operation'] == 'pulse_data':
  481:             from .memory_pulse_adapters import publication_paths
  482:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  483:         if payload['operation'] == 'derived_sync':
  484:             from .memory_derived_sync import publication_paths
  485:             return publication_paths(self, scope)
  486:         if payload['operation'] == 'deny_hashes':
  487:             from .memory_deny_hashes import publication_paths
  488:             return publication_paths(self, scope)
  489:         if payload['operation'] in {'interview_due_cache_write', 'interview_due_mark'}:
  490:             from .memory_interview import publication_paths
  491:             return publication_paths(self, scope, payload)
  492:         if payload['operation'] == 'state_evidence_cache':
  493:             from .memory_evidence import publication_paths
  494:             return publication_paths(self, scope)
  495:         if payload['operation'] == 'freshness_migration':
  496:             from .memory_freshness_migration import publication_paths
  497:             return publication_paths(self, connection, scope, payload)
  498:         if payload['operation'] == 'freshness_cache':
  499:             from .memory_freshness_cache import publication_paths
  500:             return publication_paths(self, scope)
  501:         if payload['operation'] == 'freshness_write':
  502:             from .memory_freshness import publication_paths
  503:             return publication_paths(self, connection, scope, payload)
  504:         if payload['operation'] == 'telos_summary':
  505:             from .memory_telos import publication_paths
  506:             return publication_paths(self, scope)
  507:         if payload['operation'] == 'lifeos_state':
  508:             from .memory_state import publication_paths
  509:             return publication_paths(self, scope)
  510:         if payload['operation'] == 'memory_graph':
  511:             from .memory_graph import publication_paths
  512:             return publication_paths(self, scope)
  513:         if payload['operation'] == 'restore':
  514:             from .memory_restore import publication_paths
  515:             return publication_paths(self,connection,scope,payload)
  516:         if payload['operation'] == 'staged_promote':
  517:             from .memory_staging import publication_paths
  518:             return publication_paths(self,connection,scope,payload)
  519:         if payload['operation'] == 'staged_reject':
  520:             from .memory_staging import rejection_paths
  521:             return rejection_paths(self,scope,payload)
  522:         if payload["operation"] == "proposal_decision":
  523:             from .memory_proposals import _permitted
  524:             permission = 'auto_apply' if payload['decision'] == 'auto_apply' else 'approve'
  525:             if not _permitted(scope, permission):
  526:                 return []
  527:             row = connection.execute("SELECT * FROM proposals WHERE id=?", (payload['reference']['id'],)).fetchone()
  528:             if row is None or row['status'] != 'pending' or row['revision'] != payload['reference']['revision']:
  529:                 return []
  530:             return [row['path'], row['target'], "LIFEOS/MEMORY/OBSERVABILITY/identity-proposals.jsonl"]
  531:         if payload["operation"] == "native_proposal":
  532:             return ["LIFEOS/MEMORY/OBSERVABILITY/pending-proposals.jsonl"]
  533:         if payload["operation"] == "native_set":
  534:             return self._hot_publication_paths(payload['category'])
  535:         if payload["operation"] in ("remember", "native_add"):
  536:             checked = self._native("validate", item=payload["item"])
  537:             if not checked.get("ok") or checked["item"] != payload["item"]:
  538:                 return []
  539:             result = self._native("route", item=checked["item"])
  540:             relative = Path(result["path"]).relative_to(self.root).as_posix()
  541:             category = next((category for category, path in HOT_FILES.items() if path == relative), None)
  542:             return self._hot_publication_paths(category) if category else [relative]
  543:         reference = payload.get("reference")
  544:         if not isinstance(reference, dict) or not isinstance(reference.get("id"), str):
  545:             return []
  546:         row = self._target(connection, scope, reference)
  547:         if row is None or row['status'] != 'active' or row['revision'] != reference['revision']:
  548:             return []
  549:         if row['category'] in HOT_FILES:
  550:             if row['category'] not in scope.read:
  551:                 return []
  552:             # Refuse drift before the recovery journal copies the whole source file.
  553:             self._hot_snapshot(connection, row['category'])
  554:         paths = self._hot_publication_paths(row['category']) if row['category'] in HOT_FILES else [row['path']]
  555:         if payload['operation'] == 'correct' and row['category'] == 'project' and isinstance(payload['content'],str):
  556:             item = self._archive_item(self._path(row['path']),payload['content'],row['source_session'])
  557:             checked = self._native('validate',item=item)
  558:             if checked.get('ok') and checked.get('item') == item:
  559:                 target = Path(self._native('route',item=item)['path']).relative_to(self.root).as_posix()
  560:                 self._path(target)
  561:                 paths.append(target)
  562:         return paths
  563: 
  564:     def _hot_publication_paths(self, category: str) -> list[str]:
  565:         log = self._path(HOT_WRITE_LOG)
  566:         expected = self.root.parent / '.config/LIFEOS/USER/MEMORY/OBSERVABILITY/memory-writes.jsonl'
  567:         if log.resolve() != expected.absolute() or (log.exists() and not log.is_file()):
  568:             raise MemoryUnavailable('The native hot-write log changes its permitted physical path')
  569:         # Native append evidence must recover with the fact publication it describes.
  570:         return [HOT_FILES[category], HOT_WRITE_LOG]
  571: 
  572:     @staticmethod
  573:     def _native_receipt(receipt: dict[str, Any], *, category: str, path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
  574:         if receipt["status"] not in ("committed", "unchanged"):
  575:             return {"ok": False, "code": "EINVAL_ITEM", "message": receipt.get("reason", "Memory operation did not commit"),
  576:                     "receipt": receipt}
  577:         return {"ok": True, "type": "memory" if category != "project" else "knowledge", "path": str(path),
  578:                 "detail": receipt.get("detail", {}), "receipt": receipt}
  579: 
  580:     def _hot_snapshot(self, connection: sqlite3.Connection, category: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
  581:         path = self._path(HOT_FILES[category])
  582:         snapshot = self._native("read_hot", path=str(path))
  583:         if "entries" not in snapshot or snapshot.get("dropped_invalid"):
  584:             raise MemoryUnavailable("Native hot memory needs repair before governed curation")
  585:         indexed = connection.execute("SELECT * FROM records WHERE category=? AND status='active'", (category,)).fetchall()
  586:         entries = snapshot["entries"]
  587:         if sorted(_digest(entry) for entry in entries) != sorted(row["digest"] for row in indexed):
  588:             raise MemoryConflict("Native hot memory changed outside governed publication; review it before adoption")
  589:         return {**snapshot, "revision": _digest(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))}
  590: 
  591:     def read_hot(self, scope: MemoryScope, category: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
  592:         if category not in HOT_FILES or category not in scope.read:
  593:             return {"ok": False, "code": "EINVAL_PATH", "message": "This hot-memory file has no read grant"}
  594:         with self._transaction() as connection:
  595:             return self._hot_snapshot(connection, category)
  596: 
  597:     def _curate_hot(self, connection: sqlite3.Connection, scope: MemoryScope, category: str,
  598:                     entries: list[str], observed_revision: str, *, allow_drastic: bool = False,
  599:                     source: dict[str, str] | None = None, native_writer: str | None = None,
  600:                     check_current=None) -> dict[str, Any]:
  601:         current = self._hot_snapshot(connection, category)
  602:         if not observed_revision or observed_revision != current["revision"]:
  603:             return {"status": "conflict", "reason": "The native memory revision changed after the reviewer read it"}
  604:         desired = list(dict.fromkeys(entries))
  605:         prior = connection.execute("SELECT * FROM records WHERE category=? AND status='active'", (category,)).fetchall()
  606:         by_digest = {row["digest"]: row for row in prior}
  607:         by_claim: dict[str, list[sqlite3.Row]] = {}
  608:         for row in prior:
  609:             by_claim.setdefault(row["claim_digest"], []).append(row)
  610:         assignments = []
  611:         preserved = set()
  612:         desired_digests = {_digest(entry) for entry in desired}
  613:         for entry in desired:
  614:             item = {"type": "memory", "actor": category, "content": entry}
  615:             if not re.match(r"^(NAME|ROLE|RELATION|PREFERENCE|RULE): ", entry):
  616:                 return {"status": "rejected", "reason": "Every native hot entry requires a recognized prefix"}
  617:             invalid = self._validate(item, entry, category)
  618:             if invalid:
  619:                 return {"status": "rejected", "reason": invalid}
  620:             if entry not in current["entries"] and self._blocked(connection, entry):
  621:                 return {"status": "rejected", "reason": "This fact needs explicit reactivation after correction or forgetting"}
  622:             row = by_digest.get(_digest(entry))
  623:             equivalent = by_claim.get(_claim_digest(entry), [])
  624:             if row is None and len(equivalent) == 1 and equivalent[0]["digest"] not in desired_digests:
  625:                 row = equivalent[0]
  626:             if row is not None:
  627:                 if row["id"] in preserved:
  628:                     return {"status": "conflict", "reason": "Curation maps several replacements to one native fact"}
  629:                 preserved.add(row["id"])
  630:             assignments.append(row)
  631:         path = self._path(HOT_FILES[category])
  632:         if check_current is not None:
  633:             check_current(connection)
  634:         # Native summaries recognize the addition label; registry authorship stays in scope.writer.
  635:         result = self._native("set_hot", path=str(path), entries=desired,
  636:                               writer=native_writer or scope.writer, allowDrastic=allow_drastic)
  637:         if not result.get("ok"):
  638:             return {"status": "rejected", "reason": result.get("message", "Native curation rejected the update")}
  639:         if result.get("dropped_malformed") or result.get("dropped_overlength"):
  640:             raise MemoryUnavailable("Native curation dropped submitted facts; publication requires recovery")
  641:         actual = self._native("read_hot", path=str(path)).get("entries")
  642:         if actual != desired:
  643:             raise MemoryUnavailable("Native curation did not publish the complete requested snapshot")
  644:         references = []
  645:         for row in prior:
  646:             if row["id"] not in preserved:
  647:                 connection.execute("UPDATE records SET status='superseded',revision=revision+1,updated=? WHERE id=?", (_now(), row["id"]))
  648:         for entry, row in zip(desired, assignments):
  649:             if row is None:
  650:                 references.append(self._record(connection, scope, path, entry, category, "",
  651:                                                source if source is not None else {"kind": "native-curation"}))
  652:             elif row["digest"] == _digest(entry):
  653:                 references.append({"id": row["id"], "revision": row["revision"]})
  654:             else:
  655:                 text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
  656:                 start = text.index("<!-- BEGIN ENTRIES -->") + len("<!-- BEGIN ENTRIES -->")
  657:                 position = text.find(entry, start, text.index("<!-- END ENTRIES -->"))
  658:                 if position < 0:
  659:                     raise MemoryUnavailable("Native curation did not save the replacement fact")
  660:                 connection.execute("UPDATE records SET digest=?,chars=?,position=?,revision=revision+1,updated=? WHERE id=?",
  661:                                    (_digest(entry), len(entry), position, _now(), row["id"]))
  662:                 references.append({"id": row["id"], "revision": row["revision"] + 1})
  663:         return {"status": "committed", "references": references, "detail": result}
  664: 
  665:     def native_set(self, scope: MemoryScope, category: str, entries: list[str], request_id: str,
  666:                    observed_revision: str, *, allow_drastic: bool = False, source_session: str = '',
  667:                    check_current=None) -> dict[str, Any]:
  668:         if category not in HOT_FILES or category not in scope.read or category not in scope.write:
  669:             return {"ok": False, "code": "EINVAL_PATH", "message": "Native curation needs read and write grants for the whole file"}
  670:         if not isinstance(entries, list) or any(not isinstance(entry, str) for entry in entries) or type(allow_drastic) is not bool:
  671:             return {"ok": False, "code": "EINVAL_ITEM", "message": "Native curation requires a validated entry list"}
  672:         payload = {"operation": "native_set", "category": category, "entries": entries,
  673:                    "observed_revision": observed_revision, "allow_drastic": allow_drastic, 'source_session': source_session}
  674:         receipt = self._operation(scope, request_id, payload, lambda connection:
  675:                                   self._curate_hot(connection, scope, category, entries, observed_revision, allow_drastic=allow_drastic,
  676:                                                    source={'kind': 'native-curation', 'session': source_session},
  677:                                                    check_current=check_current))
  678:         result = self._native_receipt(receipt, category=category, path=self._path(HOT_FILES[category]))
  679:         return {**receipt["detail"], "receipt": receipt} if result["ok"] else {**result, "code": "EWRITE_FAILED"}
  680:
