  703: 
  704:     def native_add(self, scope: MemoryScope, item: dict[str, Any], *, request_id: str, project: str,
  705:                    observed_revision: str = "", source_session: str = "", check_current=None) -> dict[str, Any]:
  706:         if not isinstance(item, dict):
  707:             return {"ok": False, "code": "EINVAL_ITEM", "message": "A native memory item is required"}
  708:         if item.get("type") == "proposal":
  709:             from .memory_proposals import enqueue
  710:             return enqueue(self, scope, item, request_id, source_session, check_current=check_current)
  711:         category = item.get("actor") if item.get("type") == "memory" else "project"
  712:         if not isinstance(category, str) or item.get("type") not in ("memory", "knowledge", "idea") or category not in CATEGORIES:
  713:             return {"ok": False, "code": "EINVAL_ITEM", "message": "This native memory item needs a supported governed operation"}
  714:         if category not in scope.write or (category == "project" and (not project or ("*" not in scope.projects and project not in scope.projects))):
  715:             return {"ok": False, "code": "EINVAL_ITEM", "message": "This native memory item has no write grant"}
  716:         if category != "project" and category not in scope.read:
  717:             return {"ok": False, "code": "EINVAL_ITEM", "message": "Native curation requires a read grant for the current hot file"}
  718:         if item.get("type") == "knowledge" and item.get("entity_type") in ("person", "company") and "principal" not in scope.write:
  719:             return {"ok": False, "code": "EINVAL_ITEM", "message": "Native private entity notes need principal write permission"}
  720:         checked = self._native("validate", item=item)
  721:         if not checked.get("ok") or checked["item"] != item:
  722:             return {"ok": False, "code": "EINVAL_ITEM", "message": checked.get("message", "Native validation changed the requested item")}
  723:         if category != "project" and item.get("op") == "set":
  724:             result = self.native_set(scope, category, item.get("entries"), request_id, observed_revision,
  725:                                      source_session=source_session, check_current=check_current)
  726:             if not result.get("ok"):
  727:                 return result
  728:             return {"ok": True, "type": "memory", "path": str(self._path(HOT_FILES[category])), "detail": result, "receipt": result["receipt"]}
  729:         project = project if category == "project" else ""
  730:         content = item.get("content")
  731:         if not isinstance(content, str) or not content.strip():
  732:             return {"ok": False, "code": "EINVAL_ITEM", "message": "A native fact requires content"}
  733:         def save(connection):
  734:             invalid = self._validate(item, content, category)
  735:             if invalid:
  736:                 return {"status": "rejected", "reason": invalid}
  737:             if category != "project":
  738:                 snapshot = self._hot_snapshot(connection, category)
  739:                 return self._curate_hot(connection, scope, category, [*snapshot["entries"], content], snapshot["revision"],
  740:                                         native_writer='MemorySystem.add', source={'kind': 'native', 'session': source_session},
  741:                                         check_current=check_current)
  742:             if self._blocked(connection, content):
  743:                 return {"status": "rejected", "reason": "This fact needs explicit reactivation after correction or forgetting"}
  744:             result = self._archive_write(connection, item, check_current=check_current)
  745:             if not result.get("ok"):
  746:                 return {"status": "rejected", "reason": result.get("message", "Native memory rejected the fact")}
  747:             reference = self._record(connection, scope, Path(result["path"]), content, category, project,
  748:                                      {"kind": "native", "session": source_session})
  749:             return {"status": "committed", "reference": reference, "detail": result["detail"]}
  750:         receipt = self._operation(scope, request_id, {"operation": "native_add", "item": item, "project": project,
  751:                                                     "source_session": source_session}, save)
  752:         path = self._path(HOT_FILES[category]) if category in HOT_FILES else self.root / "LIFEOS/MEMORY"
  753:         result = self._native_receipt(receipt, category=category, path=path)
  754:         if result["ok"] and category == "project":
  755:             result["type"] = item["type"]
  756:             with self._transaction() as connection:
  757:                 row = connection.execute("SELECT path FROM records WHERE id=?", (receipt["reference"]["id"],)).fetchone()
  758:                 result["path"] = str(self._path(row["path"]))
  759:         return result
  760: 
  761:     def remember(self, scope: MemoryScope, *, category: str, content: str, title: str, project: str,
  762:                  request_id: str, source: dict[str, str] | None = None,
  763:                  check_current=None, record_result=None) -> dict[str, Any]:
  764:         if any(callback is not None and not callable(callback) for callback in (check_current, record_result)):
  765:             raise ValueError('Memory publication requires callable review and receipt checks')
  766:         source = source or {"kind": "explicit", "session": ""}
  767:         if category not in CATEGORIES or category not in scope.write or not isinstance(content, str) or not content.strip():
  768:             return {"status": "rejected", "reason": "The fact or write permission is invalid"}
  769:         if category == "project" and (not project or ("*" not in scope.projects and project not in scope.projects)):
  770:             return {"status": "rejected", "reason": "The project has no write grant"}
  771:         if category in HOT_FILES and category not in scope.read:
  772:             return {"status": "rejected", "reason": "Hot-memory publication requires a read grant for the current file"}
  773:         if category != "project":
  774:             project = ""
  775:         content = content.strip()
  776:         if category != "project" and not re.match(r"^(NAME|ROLE|RELATION|PREFERENCE|RULE): ", content):
  777:             content = ("PREFERENCE: " if category == "principal" else "RULE: ") + content
  778:         item = ({"type": "knowledge", "entity_type": "research", "name": title, "content": content,
  779:                  "source_session": source.get("session", "") or "none"} if category == "project" else
  780:                 {"type": "memory", "actor": "principal" if category == "principal" else "assistant", "content": content})
  781: 
  782:         def save_fact(connection):
  783:             invalid = self._validate(item, content, category)
  784:             if invalid:
  785:                 return {"status": "rejected", "reason": invalid}
  786:             snapshot = self._hot_snapshot(connection, category) if category in HOT_FILES else None
  787:             if self._blocked(connection, content):
  788:                 return {"status": "rejected", "reason": "This fact needs explicit reactivation after correction or forgetting"}
  789:             destination = Path(self._native("route", item=item)["path"]).relative_to(self.root).as_posix()
  790:             existing = self._duplicate(connection, scope, content, category, project, destination)
  791:             if existing:
  792:                 return {"status": "unchanged", "reference": {"id": existing["id"], "revision": existing["revision"]}, "source": source}
  793:             if snapshot is not None:
  794:                 result = self._curate_hot(connection, scope, category, [*snapshot['entries'], content],
  795:                                           snapshot['revision'], source=source, native_writer='MemorySystem.add',
  796:                                           check_current=check_current)
  797:                 if result['status'] != 'committed':
  798:                     return result
  799:                 return {'status': 'committed', 'reference': result['references'][-1], 'source': source}
  800:             result = self._archive_write(connection, item, check_current=check_current)
  801:             if not result.get("ok"):
  802:                 return {"status": "rejected", "reason": result.get("message", "Native memory rejected the fact")}
  803:             reference = self._record(connection, scope, Path(result["path"]), content, category, project, source)
  804:             return {"status": "committed", "reference": reference, "source": source}
  805: 
  806:         def save(connection):
  807:             if check_current is not None:
  808:                 check_current(connection)
  809:             receipt = save_fact(connection)
  810:             if record_result is not None:
  811:                 receipt.setdefault('writer', scope.writer)
  812:                 receipt.setdefault('request_id', request_id)
  813:                 record_result(connection, receipt)
  814:             return receipt
  815: 
  816:         return self._operation(scope, request_id, {"operation": "remember", "item": item, "project": project, "source": source}, save)
  817: 
  818:     def recall(self, scope: MemoryScope, query: str, *, limit: int = 20) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  819:         if not isinstance(query, str) or not query.strip():
  820:             raise ValueError("A memory query is required")
  821:         if type(limit) is not int:
  822:             raise ValueError("The memory result limit must be an integer")
  823:         with self._transaction() as connection:
  824:             records, corpus = self._corpus(connection, scope)
  825:             if not corpus:
  826:                 return []
  827:             ranked = self._native("rank", query=query, corpus=corpus, limit=max(1, min(limit, 100)))
  828:             return [{**records[item["path"]], "score": item["score"]} for item in ranked["results"]]
  829: 
  830:     def _corpus(self, connection: sqlite3.Connection, scope: MemoryScope) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], list[dict[str, Any]]]:
  831:         from .memory_sources import source_labels, source_projection
  832:         records = {}
  833:         corpus = []
  834:         declared = []
  835:         hot_entries = {}
  836:         for row in connection.execute("SELECT * FROM records WHERE status='active' ORDER BY updated DESC"):
  837:             if not self._allowed(scope, row):
  838:                 continue
  839:             # Reuse native parsing inside this locked retrieval, keeping each reference's digest check.
  840:             content = self._content(row, hot_entries)
  841:             if row['category'] == 'project' and self._filter_history(connection, scope,
  842:                     source_labels(row['path']), _now())['excluded']:
  843:                 continue
  844:             records[row["id"]] = {"reference": {"id": row["id"], "revision": row["revision"]},
  845:                                   "content": content, "category": row["category"], "project": row["project"],
  846:                                   "writer": row["writer"], "status": 'historical' if row['source_kind']=='learning' else row["status"],
  847:                                   "source":{'kind':row['source_kind'],'session':row['source_session'],'path':row['path']}}
  848:             corpus.append({"filePath": row["id"], "frontmatter": {"type": ("idea" if "/KNOWLEDGE/Ideas/" in row["path"] else "knowledge") if row["category"] == "project" else "memory",
  849:                                                                  "title": row["project"] or row["category"]},
  850:                            "body": content, "wordCount": max(1, len(content.split())),
  851:                            "noteClass": 'learning' if row['source_kind']=='learning' else "knowledge" if row["category"] == "project" else "memory"})
  852:             declared.append(source_projection(self, row['path'], content))
  853:         if declared:
  854:             checked = self._native('validate_source_batch', contents=declared)['accepted']
  855:             corpus = [item for item, accepted in zip(corpus, checked, strict=True) if accepted is True]
  856:             records = {item['filePath']: records[item['filePath']] for item in corpus}
  857:         return records, corpus
  858: 
  859:     def relevant_context(self, scope: MemoryScope, query: str, options: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
  860:         if not isinstance(query, str) or len(query) > 4096 or not isinstance(options, dict):
  861:             raise ValueError("Invalid native memory retrieval request")
  862:         if set(options) - {"topK", "threshold", "excerptChars", "typeFilter"}:
  863:             raise ValueError("Unsupported native memory retrieval options")
  864:         for key, maximum in (("topK", 100), ("excerptChars", 65536)):
  865:             if key in options and (type(options[key]) is not int or not 1 <= options[key] <= maximum):
  866:                 raise ValueError("Invalid native retrieval limit")
  867:         if "threshold" in options and (type(options["threshold"]) not in (int, float) or not 0 <= options["threshold"] < float("inf")):
  868:             raise ValueError("Invalid native retrieval threshold")
  869:         if "typeFilter" in options and options["typeFilter"] not in ("memory", "idea", "knowledge", "unknown"):
  870:             raise ValueError("Invalid native retrieval type")
  871:         with self._transaction() as connection:
  872:             _, corpus = self._corpus(connection, scope)
  873:             return self._native("rank", query=query, corpus=corpus, options=options)

  875:     def filter_history(self, scope: MemoryScope, content: str, timestamp: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
  876:         if not isinstance(content, str) or len(content) > 65536 or not isinstance(timestamp, str):
  877:             raise ValueError("Invalid reviewer history input")
  878:         if not CATEGORIES <= set(scope.read) or "*" not in scope.projects:
  879:             return {"content": "", "excluded": True}
  880:         with self._transaction() as connection:
  881:             return self._filter_history(connection,scope,content,timestamp)

  883:     def filter_job_output(self, scope: MemoryScope, content: str, timestamp: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
  884:         if (not isinstance(content, str) or not isinstance(timestamp, str)
  885:                 or len(content.encode('utf-8')) > JOB_OUTPUT_LIMIT):
  886:             raise ValueError('Invalid native job output')
  887:         if (not CATEGORIES <= set(scope.read) or not CATEGORIES <= set(scope.write)
  888:                 or '*' not in scope.projects):
  889:             return {'content': '', 'excluded': True}
  890:         with self._transaction() as connection:
  891:             return self._filter_history(connection, scope, content, timestamp)

  893:     def _filter_history(self, connection: sqlite3.Connection, scope: MemoryScope, content: str, timestamp: str,
  894:                         *, reviewed: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
  895:         retained = connection.execute("""SELECT * FROM records AS retained WHERE status IN ('forgotten','superseded')
  896:                                       AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM records AS current WHERE current.status='active'
  897:                                       AND current.claim_digest=retained.claim_digest AND current.category=retained.category
  898:                                       AND current.project=retained.project)""").fetchall()
  899:         retained = [row for row in retained if self._allowed(scope, row)]
  900:         if not retained:
  901:             return {"content": content, "excluded": False}
  902:         try:
  903:             source_time = datetime.fromisoformat(timestamp.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
  904:             if source_time.tzinfo is None:
  905:                 raise ValueError("Reviewer sources need an explicit timezone")
  906:             if not reviewed and source_time <= max(datetime.fromisoformat(row["updated"]) for row in retained):
  907:                 return {"content": "", "excluded": True}
  908:         except ValueError:
  909:             return {"content": "", "excluded": True}
  910:         words = _claim_words(content)
  911:         hashes_by_length: dict[int, set[str]] = {}
  912:         for row in retained:
  913:             if row["claim_words"] > 0:
  914:                 hashes_by_length.setdefault(row["claim_words"], set()).add(row["claim_digest"])
  915:         for length, hashes in hashes_by_length.items():
  916:             for start in range(len(words) - length + 1):
  917:                 if _digest(" ".join(words[start:start + length])) in hashes:
  918:                     return {"content": "", "excluded": True}
  919:         return {"content": content, "excluded": False}
  920: 
  921:     def _target(self, connection: sqlite3.Connection, scope: MemoryScope, reference: dict[str, Any]):
  922:         if not isinstance(reference, dict) or not isinstance(reference.get("id"), str) or type(reference.get("revision")) is not int:
  923:             return None
  924:         row = connection.execute("SELECT * FROM records WHERE id=?", (reference["id"],)).fetchone()
  925:         if row is None or not self._allowed(scope, row, write=True):
  926:             return None
  927:         return row
  928: 
  929:     def get(self, scope: MemoryScope, reference: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
  930:         if not isinstance(reference, dict) or not isinstance(reference.get("id"), str) or type(reference.get("revision")) is not int:
  931:             return {"status": "rejected", "reason": "A current memory reference is required"}
  932:         with self._transaction() as connection:
  933:             row = connection.execute("SELECT * FROM records WHERE id=?", (reference["id"],)).fetchone()
  934:             if row is None or not self._allowed(scope, row):
  935:                 return {"status": "rejected", "reason": "The memory reference is unavailable or has no read grant"}
  936:             if row["status"] != "active" or row["revision"] != reference["revision"]:
  937:                 return {"status": "conflict", "reason": "The referenced revision is no longer current"}
  938:             return {"status": "ok", "reference": reference, "content": self._content(row),
  939:                     "category": row["category"], "project": row["project"], "writer": row["writer"],
  940:                     "source": {"session": row["source_session"], "kind": row["source_kind"]}}

  942:     def correct(self, scope: MemoryScope, reference: dict[str, Any], content: str, request_id: str,
  943:                 check_current=None) -> dict[str, Any]:
  944:         def change(connection):
  945:             if check_current is not None:
  946:                 check_current(connection)
  947:             row = self._target(connection, scope, reference)
  948:             if row is None:
  949:                 return {"status": "rejected", "reason": "The memory reference is unavailable or has no write grant"}
  950:             if row["status"] != "active" or row["revision"] != reference["revision"]:
  951:                 return {"status": "conflict", "reason": "The referenced revision is no longer current"}
  952:             if row['category'] in HOT_FILES and row['category'] not in scope.read:
  953:                 return {'status': 'rejected', 'reason': 'Hot-memory correction requires read and write grants'}
  954:             if not isinstance(content, str) or not content.strip():
  955:                 return {"status": "rejected", "reason": "A replacement fact is required"}
  956:             current = self._hot_snapshot(connection, row['category']) if row['category'] in HOT_FILES else None
  957:             old = self._content(row)
  958:             replacement = content.strip()
  959:             path = self._path(row["path"])
  960:             if row["category"] == "project":
  961:                 item = self._archive_item(path, replacement, row["source_session"])
  962:             else:
  963:                 if not re.match(r"^(NAME|ROLE|RELATION|PREFERENCE|RULE): ", replacement):
  964:                     replacement = old.split(": ", 1)[0] + ": " + replacement
  965:                 item = {"type": "memory", "actor": "principal" if row["category"] == "principal" else "assistant",
  966:                         "content": replacement}
  967:             invalid = self._validate(item, replacement, row["category"])
  968:             if invalid:
  969:                 return {"status": "rejected", "reason": invalid}
  970:             if replacement == old:
  971:                 return {"status": "unchanged", "reference": reference}
  972:             if self._blocked(connection, replacement):
  973:                 return {"status": "rejected", "reason": "This fact needs explicit reactivation after correction or forgetting"}
  974:             existing = self._duplicate(connection, scope, replacement, row["category"], row["project"], row["path"])
  975:             if row["category"] == "project" and not existing:
  976:                 # Native path slugs must remain unchanged for section references.
  977:                 result = self._archive_write(connection, item, check_current=check_current)
  978:                 if not result.get("ok"):
  979:                     return {"status": "rejected", "reason": result.get("message", "Native correction rejected")}
  980:                 path = Path(result["path"])
  981:             elif row["category"] != "project":
  982:                 entries = current['entries']
  983:                 if old not in entries:
  984:                     return {"status": "conflict", "reason": "The native fact changed before correction"}
  985:                 updated = [value for value in entries if value != old] if existing else [replacement if value == old else value for value in entries]
  986:                 if check_current is not None:
  987:                     check_current(connection)
  988:                 result = self._native("set_hot", path=str(path), entries=updated, writer=scope.writer, allowDrastic=True)
  989:                 if not result.get("ok"):
  990:                     return {"status": "rejected", "reason": result.get("message", "Native correction rejected")}
  991:             elif check_current is not None:
  992:                 check_current(connection)
  993:             new_reference = ({"id": existing["id"], "revision": existing["revision"]} if existing else
  994:                              self._record(connection, scope, path, replacement, row["category"], row["project"],
  995:                                           {"session": row["source_session"], "kind": "correction"}))
  996:             connection.execute("UPDATE records SET status='superseded',revision=revision+1,updated=? WHERE id=?", (_now(), row["id"]))
  997:             return {"status": "committed", "reference": new_reference, "supersedes": reference}
  998:         return self._operation(scope, request_id, {"operation": "correct", "reference": reference, "content": content}, change)

   62: def tool_schemas() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   63:     return [{"name": name, "description": description,
   64:              "parameters": {"type": "object", "properties": {key: FIELDS[key] for key in (*required, *optional)},
   65:                             "required": list(required), "additionalProperties": False}}
   66:             for name, (description, required, optional) in TOOLS.items()]
   67: 
   68: 
   69: def _validate_arguments(name: str, arguments: Any) -> None:
   70:     if name not in TOOLS or not isinstance(arguments, dict):
   71:         raise ValueError("Unknown memory tool or invalid arguments")
   72:     _, required, optional = TOOLS[name]
   73:     if set(arguments) - set((*required, *optional)) or set(required) - set(arguments):
   74:         raise ValueError("Memory tool arguments contain missing or unrecognized fields")
   75:     for key, value in arguments.items():
   76:         schema = FIELDS[key]
   77:         if schema["type"] == "string":
   78:             if not isinstance(value, str) or not schema.get("minLength", 0) <= len(value) <= schema.get("maxLength", 65536):
   79:                 raise ValueError(f"Invalid {key}")
   80:             if "enum" in schema and value not in schema["enum"]:
   81:                 raise ValueError(f"Invalid {key}")
   82:         elif schema["type"] == "integer":
   83:             if type(value) is not int or not schema["minimum"] <= value <= schema["maximum"]:
   84:                 raise ValueError(f"Invalid {key}")
   85:         elif key == "proposal":
   86:             if (not isinstance(value, dict) or set(value) - set(schema["properties"])
   87:                     or set(schema["required"]) - set(value) or value.get("type") != "proposal"):
   88:                 raise ValueError("Invalid native proposal fields")
   89:             # A client controls every nested field; bound them before the native worker serializes them.
   90:             for field, item in value.items():
   91:                 rule = schema["properties"][field]
   92:                 if rule["type"] == "string":
   93:                     valid = isinstance(item, str) and rule.get("minLength", 0) <= len(item) <= rule.get("maxLength", 65536)
   94:                 elif rule["type"] == "integer":
   95:                     valid = type(item) is int and rule["minimum"] <= item <= rule["maximum"]
   96:                 else:
   97:                     valid = type(item) in (int, float) and rule["minimum"] <= item <= rule["maximum"]
   98:                 if not valid:
   99:                     raise ValueError(f"Invalid native proposal field: {field}")
  100:         elif key == "reference":
  101:             if not isinstance(value, dict) or set(value) != {"id", "revision"} or not isinstance(value["id"], str) or not value["id"]:
  102:                 raise ValueError("Invalid memory reference")
  103:             if type(value["revision"]) is not int or value["revision"] < 1:
  104:                 raise ValueError("Invalid memory revision")

  111:     @staticmethod
  112:     def check_owner(configuration: dict[str, Any], account: str | None) -> None:
  113:         # Internal owner helpers have no HTTP account; HTTP callers supply a verified qualified account.
  114:         if account is not None and (not isinstance(account, str)
  115:                 or configuration.get('accounts', {}).get(account) != configuration['principal']):
  116:             raise PermissionError('This dashboard account has no installation owner binding')
  117: 
  118:     @staticmethod
  119:     def validate(configuration: Any) -> None:
  120:         if not isinstance(configuration, dict) or type(configuration.get("version")) is not int or configuration["version"] != 1:
  121:             raise ValueError("Unsupported memory configuration")
  122:         if not isinstance(configuration.get("root"), str) or not Path(configuration["root"]).is_absolute():
  123:             raise ValueError("Memory needs an absolute installed LifeOS root")
  124:         if type(configuration.get("ownership_enabled", False)) is not bool:
  125:             raise ValueError("Memory ownership must be enabled explicitly")
  126:         if type(configuration.get("sharing_enabled", False)) is not bool:
  127:             raise ValueError("Memory sharing must be enabled explicitly")
  128:         workspace = configuration.get('hermes_workspace')

  144:                 if not isinstance(values, list) or any(not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip() for value in values):
  145:                     raise ValueError("Invalid memory client permissions")
  146:             if not set(grant.get("read", [])) <= CATEGORIES or not set(grant.get("write", [])) <= {"project"}:

  154:     def load(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  155:         if self.path.is_symlink() or not self.path.is_file():
  156:             raise MemoryUnavailable("Memory setup is unavailable")
  157:         info = self.path.stat()
  158:         if info.st_uid != os.getuid() or info.st_mode & 0o077:
  159:             raise MemoryUnavailable("Memory configuration needs private owner permissions")
  160:         configuration = json.loads(self.path.read_text())
  161:         self.validate(configuration)
  162:         if 'LIFEOS_MEMORY_CONFIGURATION_REVISION' in os.environ:
  163:             self.check_revision(configuration, os.environ['LIFEOS_MEMORY_CONFIGURATION_REVISION'])
  164:         return configuration
  165: 
  166:     @staticmethod
  167:     def check_revision(configuration, expected):
  168:         if (not isinstance(expected, str) or re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{64}', expected) is None
  169:                 or MemoryPolicy(configuration).revision != expected):
  170:             raise MemoryUnavailable('The initiating owner configuration changed before this job operation')
  171: 
  172:     def save(self, configuration: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
  173:         self.validate(configuration)
  174:         with self._lock():
  175:             self._publish(configuration)
  176: 
  177:     @contextmanager
  178:     def _lock(self):
  179:         self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
  180:         descriptor = os.open(self.path.with_name(self.path.name + '.lock'),
  181:                              os.O_CREAT | os.O_RDWR | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o600)
  182:         try:
  183:             info = os.fstat(descriptor)
  184:             if not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) or info.st_uid != os.getuid() or info.st_mode & 0o077:
  185:                 raise MemoryUnavailable('Memory configuration lock needs private owner permissions')
  186:             fcntl.flock(descriptor, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
  187:             yield
  188:         finally:
  189:             os.close(descriptor)
  190: 
  191:     def _publish(self, configuration: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
  192:         if self.path.is_symlink():
  193:             raise MemoryUnavailable('Memory configuration must not be a symlink')
  194:         publish(self.path, (json.dumps(configuration, indent=2) + '\n').encode())
  195: 
  196:     def update(self, change) -> dict[str, Any]:
  197:         with self._lock():
  198:             configuration = self.load()
  199:             change(configuration)
  200:             self.validate(configuration)
  201:             self._publish(configuration)
  202:             return configuration

  215:         memory = NativeMemory(Path(configuration['root']))
  216:         if operation == 'prompt_bundle' and set(arguments) == {'keepOutputFormat'}:
  217:             result = {'ok': True, 'bundle': bundle(memory, scope, keep_output_format=arguments['keepOutputFormat'])}
  218:         elif operation in ('prompt_preview', 'prompt_publish'):
  219:             if not isinstance(arguments.get('home'), str) or Path(arguments['home']).absolute() != self.configuration.path.parent.absolute():
  220:                 raise ValueError('Prompt publication requires the configured Hermes profile')
  221:             if operation == 'prompt_preview' and set(arguments) == {'home', 'keepOutputFormat'}:
  222:                 result = {'ok': True, **preview(memory, scope, self.configuration.path.parent,
  223:                                                keep_output_format=arguments['keepOutputFormat'])}
  224:             elif operation == 'prompt_publish' and set(arguments) == {'home', 'keepOutputFormat', 'signature', 'previous_digest'}:
  225:                 def check_current():
  226:                     if self.configuration.load() != configuration:
  227:                         raise MemoryUnavailable('The memory configuration changed during prompt publication')
  228:                     if check_authority is not None:
  229:                         check_authority()
  230:                 receipt = publish_prompt(memory, scope, self.configuration.path.parent,
  231:                     arguments['signature'], arguments['previous_digest'],
  232:                     keep_output_format=arguments['keepOutputFormat'], check_current=check_current)
  233:                 result = {'ok': receipt['status'] in ('committed', 'unchanged'), 'receipt': receipt}
  234:             else:
  235:                 raise ValueError('Choose a fixed prompt preview or publication action')
  236:         else:
  237:             raise ValueError('Choose a fixed prompt preview or publication action')
  238:         if check_authority is not None:
  239:             check_authority()
  240:         return result
  241: 
  242:     def administrative(self, authorization, operation, arguments):
  243:         from .memory_administration import validate
  244:         try:
  245:             if operation not in ('prompt_bundle', 'prompt_preview', 'prompt_publish') or not isinstance(arguments, dict):
  246:                 raise ValueError('Administrative authorization permits only prompt mount operations')
  247:             configuration, scope = validate(self.configuration, authorization)
  248:             return self._prompt(configuration, scope, operation, arguments,
  249:                                 check_authority=lambda: validate(self.configuration, authorization))
  250:         except (MemoryUnavailable, PermissionError, ValueError, OSError, sqlite3.Error, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
  251:             return {'ok': False, 'code': 'EWRITE_FAILED', 'message': 'Administrative prompt mounting is unavailable'}
  252: 
  253:     def native(self, context: SessionContext, operation: str, arguments: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
  254:         try:
  255:             configuration = self.configuration.load()
  256:             scope = self._context_scope(configuration, context)
  257:         except (MemoryUnavailable, ValueError, OSError, sqlite3.Error, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as error:
  258:             return {'ok':False, 'code':'EWRITE_FAILED' if operation in ('add','set') else 'EINVAL_PATH',
  259:                     'message':str(error)}
  260:         result = self._native_operation(context, operation, arguments, configuration, scope)
  261:         try:
  262:             self._check_current_context(configuration, context, scope)
  263:         except (MemoryUnavailable, ValueError, OSError):
  264:             return {'ok':False, 'code':'EACCESS_CHANGED',
  265:                     'message':'Current memory authority is unavailable; the operation response is withheld.'}
  266:         return result
  267:

  620:                     current = self.configuration.load()

  622:                         raise MemoryUnavailable('Interview seed authority changes during publication')

  646:             if operation == "check_source" and set(arguments) == {"path"}:
  647:                 from .memory_sources import check

  651:                 return filter_content(memory,scope,**arguments)
  652:             if operation == "read_source" and set(arguments) == {"path"}:
  653:                 from .memory_sources import read
  654:                 return read(memory,scope,arguments['path'])
  655:             if operation == "read_diagnostic" and set(arguments) == {"path"}:
  656:                 from .memory_diagnostics import read
  657:                 return read(memory,scope,arguments['path'])
  658:             if operation == "check_diagnostic" and set(arguments) == {"path"}:
  659:                 from .memory_diagnostics import check
  660:                 return check(memory,scope,arguments['path'])
  661:             if operation == "check_diagnostic_report" and set(arguments) == {"path"}:
  662:                 from .memory_diagnostics import check
  663:                 return check(memory,scope,arguments['path'],report=True)
  664:             if operation == "filter_diagnostic" and set(arguments) == {"content", "timestamp"}:
  665:                 from .memory_diagnostics import filter_report
  666:                 return filter_report(memory,scope,**arguments)
  667:             if operation == "diagnose_hot" and set(arguments) == {"path"}:
  668:                 from .memory_diagnostics import diagnose_hot
  669:                 return diagnose_hot(memory,scope,arguments['path'])
  670:             if operation == "proposal_list" and set(arguments) == {"path"}:
  671:                 from .memory_proposals import QUEUE
  672:                 if not isinstance(arguments['path'], str) or Path(arguments['path']).absolute() != memory._path(QUEUE):
  673:                     raise ValueError("Native proposal review needs the installed queue")
  674:                 return {"rows":memory.review_proposals(scope, include_resolved=True)}
  675:             if operation == "proposal_decision":
  676:                 required = {"reference", "decision", "request_id"}
  677:                 if not required <= set(arguments) or set(arguments) - required - {"content", "note", "confidence_threshold"}:
  678:                     raise ValueError("Invalid native proposal decision fields")
  679:                 receipt = memory.decide_proposal(scope, **arguments,
  680:                     check_current=lambda _connection: self._check_current_context(configuration, context, scope))

  682:                 return {"ok":receipt['status'] == 'committed', "row":row, "receipt":receipt,
  683:                         "reason":receipt.get('reason', '')}
  684:             if operation == "retrieve" and set(arguments) == {"query", "options"}:
  685:                 return memory.relevant_context(scope, **arguments)
  686:             if operation == "filter_history" and set(arguments) == {"content", "timestamp"}:
  687:                 return memory.filter_history(scope, **arguments)
  688:             if operation == 'filter_job_output' and set(arguments) == {'content', 'timestamp'}:
  689:                 return memory.filter_job_output(scope, **arguments)
  690:             if operation in ("read", "set"):
  691:                 if not isinstance(arguments.get("path"), str):
  692:                     raise ValueError("A native hot-memory path is required")
  693:                 categories = {str(memory._path(path)): category for category, path in HOT_FILES.items()}
  694:                 category = categories.get(str(Path(arguments["path"]).absolute()))
  695:                 if category is None:
  696:                     raise ValueError("This is not a supported native hot-memory path")
  697:                 if operation == "read" and set(arguments) == {"path"}:
  698:                     return memory.read_hot(scope, category)
  699:                 if operation == "set" and set(arguments) == {"path", "entries", "request_id", "observed_revision", "allow_drastic"}:
  700:                     return memory.native_set(scope, category, source_session=context.session_id,
  701:                                              check_current=lambda _connection: self._check_current_context(configuration, context, scope),
  702:                                              **{key: value for key, value in arguments.items() if key != "path"})
  703:             if operation == "add" and set(arguments) == {"item", "request_id", "project", "observed_revision"}:
  704:                 return memory.native_add(scope, **arguments, source_session=context.session_id,
  705:                     check_current=lambda _connection: self._check_current_context(configuration, context, scope))
  706:             raise ValueError("Unsupported native memory operation or arguments")
  707:         except (MemoryUnavailable, ValueError, OSError, sqlite3.Error, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as error:
  708:             return {"ok": False, "code": "EWRITE_FAILED" if operation in ("add", "set") else "EINVAL_PATH", "message": str(error)}
  709: 
  710:     @staticmethod
  711:     def client_scope(configuration: dict[str, Any], identifier: str) -> MemoryScope:
  712:         grant = configuration.get("clients", {}).get(identifier)
  713:         if not configuration.get("sharing_enabled", False) or grant is None or not grant["enabled"]:
  714:             raise MemoryUnavailable("This memory connection is disabled or revoked")
  715:         principal = f"client:{identifier}"
  716:         policy = MemoryPolicy({"version": 1, "principal": configuration["principal"],
  717:                                "accounts": {f"mcp:{identifier}": principal},
  718:                                "destinations": {f"mcp:{identifier}": {
  719:                                    "visibility": "private", "participants": [principal],
  720:                                    "read": grant.get("read", ["project"]), "write": grant.get("write", []),
  721:                                    "projects": grant.get("projects", []), "model_routes": [grant.get("model_route", "unknown")],
  722:                                    "proposals": grant.get("proposals", []),
  723:                                }}})
  724:         return policy.resolve(SessionContext("mcp", identifier, identifier, "private", (principal,),
  725:                                              grant.get("model_route", "unknown"), ""))
  726: 
  727:     def call_client(self, identifier: str, name: str, arguments: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
  728:         try:
  729:             configuration = self.configuration.load()
  730:             scope = self.client_scope(configuration, identifier)
  731:         except (MemoryUnavailable, ValueError, OSError) as error:
  732:             return {"status": "rejected", "reason": str(error)}
  733: 
  734:         def check_current(*_):
  735:             current = self.configuration.load()
  736:             if current["root"] != configuration["root"] or self.client_scope(current, identifier) != scope:
  737:                 raise MemoryUnavailable("This memory connection changed during the request")
  738:         result = self._call(configuration, scope, name, arguments, check_current=check_current)
  739:         try:
  740:             check_current()
  741:         except (MemoryUnavailable, ValueError, OSError) as error:
  742:             return {"status": "rejected", "reason": str(error)}
  743:         return result
  744: 
  745:     def call_context(self, context: SessionContext, name: str, arguments: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
  746:         try:
  747:             configuration = self.configuration.load()
  748:             scope = self._context_scope(configuration, context)
  749:         except (MemoryUnavailable, ValueError, OSError) as error:
  750:             return {"status": "unavailable", "reason": str(error)}
  751:         def check_current(_connection=None):
  752:             self._check_current_context(configuration, context, scope)
  753:         result = self._call(configuration, scope, name, arguments,
  754:                             source_session=context.session_id, check_current=check_current)
  755:         try:
  756:             check_current()
  757:         except (MemoryUnavailable, ValueError, OSError) as error:
  758:             return {'status':'unavailable', 'reason':str(error)}
  759:         return result
  760: 
  761:     def _context_scope(self, configuration, context):
  762:         self._check_audience(configuration, context)
  763:         return MemoryPolicy(configuration).resolve(context)
  764: 
  765:     def _check_current_context(self, configuration, context, scope):
  766:         current = self.configuration.load()
  767:         if current != configuration or self._context_scope(current, context).signature != scope.signature:
  768:             raise MemoryUnavailable('This memory conversation changes during the request')
  769: 
  770:     def _check_audience(self, configuration, context):
  771:         from .discord_audience import context_audience_is_current
  772:         if not context_audience_is_current(configuration, context, audience_lookup=self.audience_lookup):
  773:             raise MemoryUnavailable('The Discord channel audience no longer permits private memory')
  774: 
  775:     def call(self, scope: MemoryScope, name: str, arguments: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
  776:         try:
  777:             configuration = self.configuration.load()
  778:         except (MemoryUnavailable, ValueError, OSError) as error:
  779:             return {"status": "unavailable", "reason": str(error)}
  780:         return self._call(configuration, scope, name, arguments)
  781: 
  782:     def _call(self, configuration: dict[str, Any], scope: MemoryScope, name: str, arguments: dict[str, Any],
  783:               *, source_session: str = '', check_current=None) -> dict[str, Any]:
  784:         try:
  785:             _validate_arguments(name, arguments)
  786:         except ValueError as error:
  787:             return {"status": "rejected", "reason": str(error)}
  788:         try:
  789:             memory = NativeMemory(Path(configuration["root"]))
  790:             if not scope.read and not scope.write and not scope.proposals:
  791:                 return {"status": "rejected", "reason": scope.reason or "This context has no memory grant"}
  792:             if name == "lifeos_memory_propose":
  793:                 result = memory.native_add(scope, arguments["proposal"], request_id=arguments["request_id"], project="",
  794:                                            source_session=source_session, check_current=check_current)
  795:                 return result.get("receipt", {"status":"rejected", "reason":result.get("message", "Native proposal failed")})
  796:             if name == "lifeos_memory_proposals":
  797:                 return {"status":"ok", "results":memory.review_proposals(scope)}
  798:             if name == "lifeos_memory_decide_proposal":
  799:                 return memory.decide_proposal(scope, **arguments, check_current=check_current)
  800:             if name == "lifeos_memory_search":
  801:                 return {"status": "ok", "results": memory.recall(scope, **arguments)}
  802:             if name == "lifeos_memory_get":
  803:                 return memory.get(scope, arguments["reference"])
  804:             if name == "lifeos_memory_remember":
  805:                 return memory.remember(scope, **arguments, source={'kind': 'explicit', 'session': source_session},
  806:                                        check_current=check_current)
  807:             if name == "lifeos_memory_correct":
  808:                 return memory.correct(scope, **arguments, check_current=check_current)
  809:             if name == "lifeos_memory_forget":
  810:                 return memory.forget(scope, **arguments, check_current=check_current)
  811:             memory._boundary()
  812:             with memory._transaction() as connection:
  813:                 connection.execute("SELECT id FROM records LIMIT 1").fetchone()
  814:             memory._native("rank", query="memory health", corpus=[], limit=1)
  815:             return {"status": "ok", "owner": "LifeOS", "read": list(scope.read), "write": list(scope.write),
  816:                     "projects": list(scope.projects), "sharing_enabled": configuration.get("sharing_enabled", False)}
  817:         except (MemoryUnavailable, ValueError, OSError, sqlite3.Error, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as error:
  818:             return {"status": "unavailable", "reason": str(error)}

   17: def configure_probe(parser: argparse.ArgumentParser) -> None:

   19:     checks.add_argument("--check", action="store_true")

   30: def configure_backup(parser: argparse.ArgumentParser) -> None:
   31:     parser.add_argument('--scope', choices=('native', 'profile'), default='native',
   32:                         help='Select native data or the selected Hermes profile together with native data.')
   33:     actions = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
   34:     actions.add_argument('--create', metavar='DIRECTORY', help='Create a private snapshot for the selected scope.')
   35:     actions.add_argument('--inspect', metavar='DIRECTORY', help='Verify a snapshot without restoring it.')
   36:     actions.add_argument('--recover', metavar='DIRECTORY', help='Recover a snapshot into a separate tree without selecting ownership.')
   37:     parser.add_argument('--signature', help='Require the recorded manifest signature during inspection or recovery.')
   38:     parser.add_argument('--destination', metavar='DIRECTORY', help='Create the separate recovery tree at this path.')
   39: 
   40: 
   41: def run_backup(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
   42:     from hermes_constants import get_hermes_home
   43:     from .installation_lock import installation_lock
   44:     from .memory_access import NativeMemory
   45:     from .memory_backup import create, inspect
   46:     from .memory_backup_recovery import recover
   47:     from .memory_preferences import MemoryPreferences
   48:     from .memory_service import MemoryConfiguration
   49:     from . import profile_backup
   50:     from .profile_backup_recovery import recover as recover_profile
   51:     configuration = MemoryConfiguration(get_hermes_home() / 'lifeos-memory.json')
   52:     if args.recover and (not args.signature or not args.destination):
   53:         print(json.dumps({'status': 'rejected', 'message': 'Recovery requires --signature and --destination.'}), file=sys.stderr)
   54:         return 1
   55:     if args.destination and not args.recover:
   56:         print(json.dumps({'status': 'rejected', 'message': '--destination applies to recovery.'}), file=sys.stderr)
   57:         return 1
   58:     try:
   59:         if args.create and args.signature:
   60:             raise ValueError('Manifest signatures apply to inspection')
   61:         if args.scope == 'profile' and (args.create or args.recover):
   62:             if args.create:
   63:                 result = profile_backup.create(configuration, Path(args.create).expanduser())
   64:             else:
   65:                 result = recover_profile(configuration, Path(args.recover).expanduser(), args.signature,
   66:                                          Path(args.destination).expanduser())
   67:         else:
   68:             with installation_lock(configuration.path.parent), configuration._lock():
   69:                 config = configuration.load()
   70:                 if args.scope == 'profile':
   71:                     manifest = profile_backup.inspect(configuration, Path(args.inspect).expanduser(), args.signature)
   72:                     result = {'status': 'verified', 'profile_files': len(manifest['files']), 'created': manifest['created']}
   73:                 else:
   74:                     memory = NativeMemory(Path(config['root']))
   75:                     scope = MemoryPreferences._owner_scope(config)
   76:                     if args.create:
   77:                         result = create(memory, scope, Path(args.create).expanduser())
   78:                     elif args.recover:
   79:                         result = recover(memory, scope, Path(args.recover).expanduser(), args.signature, Path(args.destination).expanduser())
   80:                     else:
   81:                         manifest = inspect(memory, scope, Path(args.inspect).expanduser(), args.signature)
   82:                         result = {'status': 'verified', 'files': len(manifest['files']),
   83:                                   'schema': manifest['schema'], 'created': manifest['created']}
   84:     except profile_backup.ProfileBackupTooLarge as error:
   85:         print(json.dumps({'status': 'rejected', 'message': str(error)}), file=sys.stderr)
   86:         return 1
   87:     except (ValueError, OSError, RuntimeError, sqlite3.Error, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
   88:         print(json.dumps({'status': 'rejected', 'message':
   89:                          'The backup is unavailable under the selected profile. Check its configuration, store, permissions, and destination.'}),
   90:               file=sys.stderr)
   91:         return 1
   92:     print(json.dumps(result))
   93:     return 0
   94:

   96: def configure_owner_job(parser: argparse.ArgumentParser) -> None:
   97:     from .memory_owner_jobs import JOBS
   98:     parser.add_argument('job', choices=tuple(JOBS), help='Run one configured native owner job.')
   99:     parser.add_argument('--configuration-revision', help='Require the initiating owner configuration revision.')

  102: def run_owner_job(args: argparse.Namespace, read_setting) -> int:
  103:     from hermes_constants import get_hermes_home
  104:     from hermes_cli.inventory import load_picker_context
  105:     from hermes_cli.runtime_provider import resolve_runtime_provider
  106:     from .memory_owner_jobs import OwnerJobs
  107:     from .model_tiers import configured_model_map
  108:     try:
  109:         current = load_picker_context()
  110:         provider = resolve_runtime_provider(requested=current.current_provider, target_model=current.current_model)
  111:         route = {'provider': provider['provider'], 'model': current.current_model,
  112:                  'base_url': provider['base_url'], 'api_mode': provider['api_mode']}
  113:         mapping = configured_model_map(read_setting, current.current_provider, current.current_model)
  114:         result = OwnerJobs(get_hermes_home() / 'lifeos-memory.json').run(args.job, route=route, mapping=mapping,
  115:             expected_revision=getattr(args, 'configuration_revision', None))
  116:     except (ValueError, OSError, RuntimeError, KeyError, sqlite3.Error, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
  117:         result = {'status': 'rejected', 'job': args.job,
  118:                   'message': 'The native job needs current local owner permission, an admitted model route, and a managed store.'}
  119:     print(json.dumps(result))
  120:     return 0 if result['status'] == 'completed' else 1

  159:     )

  162:         setup_fn=configure_owner_job, handler_fn=lambda args: run_owner_job(args, ctx.get_config),

  165:         'lifeos-atlas-history', help='Read verified Atlas archive rows with current local owner permission.',
  166:         setup_fn=configure_atlas_history, handler_fn=run_atlas_history,
  167:     )

FILE /home/outsider/Projects/Hermes_agent/LifeOS_plugin/lifeos_hook_bridge/memory_atlas.py
    1: # ABOUTME: Supplies current admitted graph snapshots to the native Atlas dashboard renderer.
    2: # ABOUTME: Rechecks fixed collector sources, snapshot bytes, and owner authority before delivery.
    3: from datetime import datetime, timezone
    4: import json
    5: 
    6: from .memory_access import MemoryUnavailable
    7: from .memory_sources import (authorize, _admit, _source_time, SOURCE_LIMIT, CORPUS_LIMIT,
    8:                              json_projection)
    9: from .memory_tab_freshness import _checked
   10: from .memory_operational_views import projection, project_value, PROJECTION_FIELD_LIMIT
   11: 
   12: 
   13: SNAPSHOT = 'atlas/snapshot.json'
   14: CACHE = 'LIFEOS/MEMORY/STATE/atlas-insights.json'
   15: DATABASE = 'atlas/graph.json'
   16: COLLECTOR_SOURCES = frozenset({'LIFEOS/USER/GEAR.md', 'LIFEOS/USER/PROJECTS.md'})
   17: SOURCES = COLLECTOR_SOURCES | frozenset({SNAPSHOT, CACHE})
   18: GRAPH_TABLES = frozenset({'asset', 'edge', 'source_observation', 'edge_observation', 'lifecycle_event', 'sync_run'})
   19: TABLE_ROW_LIMIT = 2048
   20: 
   21: 
   22: def capacity_projection(content, label):
   23:     count = 0
   24:     def collect(text):
   25:         nonlocal count
   26:         count += 1
   27:         return False
   28:     project_value(json.loads(content), collect)
   29:     if count > PROJECTION_FIELD_LIMIT:
   30:         raise MemoryUnavailable(f'Atlas capacity: {label} projection fields {count} exceed {PROJECTION_FIELD_LIMIT}')
   31:     decoded = projection(content)
   32:     if decoded is None:
   33:         raise MemoryUnavailable(f'Atlas capacity: {label} projection exceeds {CORPUS_LIMIT} bytes')
   34:     return decoded, count
   35: 
   36: 
   37: def admit_graph(memory, scope, connection, graph, timestamp):
   38:     if (not isinstance(graph, dict) or set(graph) != {'graph', 'metrics'} or not isinstance(graph['graph'], dict)
   39:             or set(graph['graph']) != GRAPH_TABLES or not isinstance(graph['metrics'], dict)
   40:             or any(not isinstance(rows, list) for rows in graph['graph'].values())
   41:             or len(json.dumps(graph).encode()) > CORPUS_LIMIT):
   42:         raise MemoryUnavailable('The Atlas graph changes its declared bounded response')
   43:     counts = {name: len(rows) for name, rows in graph['graph'].items()}
   44:     for name, count in counts.items():
   45:         if count > TABLE_ROW_LIMIT:
   46:             raise MemoryUnavailable(f'Atlas capacity: {name} rows {count} exceed {TABLE_ROW_LIMIT}')
   47:     content = json.dumps(graph['graph'], ensure_ascii=False)
   48:     decoded, fields = capacity_projection(content, 'graph')
   49:     if (memory._native('validate_source_batch', contents=[decoded])['accepted'] != [True] or _admit(
   50:             memory, connection, scope, content, DATABASE, timestamp, projection=decoded)['excluded']):
   51:         raise MemoryUnavailable('The Atlas graph is excluded under the current owner policy')
   52:     return {'graph_fields': {'used': fields, 'limit': PROJECTION_FIELD_LIMIT},
   53:         'graph_projection_bytes': {'used': len(decoded.encode()), 'limit': CORPUS_LIMIT},
   54:         'tables': {name: {'used': count, 'limit': TABLE_ROW_LIMIT} for name, count in counts.items()}}

   57: def source_path(memory, relative):
   58:     if relative not in SOURCES | {DATABASE}:
   59:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas requires fixed native sources')
   60:     path = (memory.root.parent / '.local/state/lifeos/atlas' / ('snapshot.json' if relative == SNAPSHOT else 'atlas.db')
   61:             if relative in {SNAPSHOT, DATABASE} else memory.root / relative)
   62:     return _checked(memory, path)

   65: def _collect(memory, scope, connection, *, insights=False, sources=None):
   66:     candidates, fingerprints = [], []
   67:     keys = lambda info: (info.st_dev, info.st_ino, info.st_size, info.st_mtime_ns)
   68:     total = 0
   69:     for relative in sorted(sources if sources is not None else SOURCES if insights else SOURCES - {CACHE}):
   70:         path = source_path(memory, relative)
   71:         if not path.exists():
   72:             fingerprints.append((relative, None))
   73:             continue
   74:         if not path.is_file():
   75:             raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas sources require regular owner files')
   76:         before = path.stat()
   77:         if before.st_size > SOURCE_LIMIT:
   78:             raise MemoryUnavailable('An Atlas source exceeds its byte limit')
   79:         with path.open('rb') as stream: raw = stream.read(SOURCE_LIMIT + 1)
   80:         if len(raw) > SOURCE_LIMIT:
   81:             raise MemoryUnavailable('An Atlas source exceeds its byte limit')
   82:         try: content = raw.decode('utf-8')
   83:         except UnicodeError as error:
   84:             raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas sources require valid UTF-8') from error
   85:         source_path(memory, relative)
   86:         after = path.stat()
   87:         if keys(before) != keys(after):
   88:             raise MemoryUnavailable('An Atlas source changes during collection')
   89:         decoded = projection(content) if relative in {SNAPSHOT, CACHE} else content
   90:         if decoded is None:
   91:             raise MemoryUnavailable('The Atlas snapshot requires complete JSON')
   92:         total += len(raw) + len(decoded.encode())
   93:         if total > CORPUS_LIMIT:
   94:             raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas sources exceed their transport limit')
   95:         fingerprints.append((relative, keys(after), content))
   96:         candidates.append((relative, content, decoded, _source_time(after), after.st_mtime_ns / 1_000_000))
   97:     checked = memory._native('validate_source_batch', contents=[decoded + '\n' + relative
   98:         for relative, _, decoded, _, _ in candidates])['accepted'] if candidates else []
   99:     for (relative, content, decoded, timestamp, _), accepted in zip(candidates, checked, strict=True):
  100:         if accepted is not True or _admit(memory, connection, scope, content, relative,
  101:                 timestamp, projection=decoded)['excluded']:
  102:             raise MemoryUnavailable('An Atlas source is excluded under the current owner policy')
  103:     snapshot = next(({'content': content, 'modified_ms': modified}
  104:         for relative, content, _, _, modified in candidates if relative == SNAPSHOT), None)
  105:     cache = next((content for relative, content, _, _, _ in candidates if relative == CACHE), None)
  106:     return snapshot, cache, fingerprints
  107: 
  108: 
  109: def collect(memory, scope, collector, *, check_current=None):
  110:     if not isinstance(collector, str) or collector not in {'gear', 'projects'}:
  111:         raise ValueError('Choose a declared Atlas owner collector')
  112:     authorize(scope)
  113:     if not scope.principal: raise MemoryUnavailable('Atlas collectors require a bound owner')
  114:     sources = frozenset({'LIFEOS/USER/' + ('GEAR.md' if collector == 'gear' else 'PROJECTS.md')})
  115:     if check_current is not None: check_current()
  116:     with memory._transaction() as connection:
  117:         _, _, fingerprints = _collect(memory, scope, connection, sources=sources)
  118:         content = fingerprints[0][2] if len(fingerprints[0]) == 3 else None
  119:         result = memory._native('atlas_collect', collector=collector, content=content)
  120:         if check_current is not None: check_current()
  121:         if _collect(memory, scope, connection, sources=sources)[2] != fingerprints:
  122:             raise MemoryUnavailable('An Atlas collector source changes during native rendering')
  123:         if (not isinstance(result, dict) or set(result) != {'complete', 'assets', 'edges'}
  124:                 or type(result['complete']) is not bool or not isinstance(result['assets'], list)
  125:                 or not isinstance(result['edges'], list) or len(json.dumps(result).encode()) > CORPUS_LIMIT):
  126:             raise MemoryUnavailable('The native Atlas collector changes its declared result')
  127:         if memory._filter_history(connection, scope, json.dumps(result),
  128:                 datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat())['excluded']:
  129:             raise MemoryUnavailable('The native Atlas collector contains excluded source text')
  130:         if check_current is not None: check_current()
  131:         return {'ok': True, 'result': result}
  132: 
  133: 
  134: def _graph(memory, scope, connection):
  135:     path = _checked(memory, memory.root.parent / '.local/state/lifeos/atlas/atlas.db')
  136:     if not path.exists(): return None
  137:     before = path.stat()
  138:     graph = memory._native('atlas_graph_state')
