FILE /home/outsider/Projects/Hermes_agent/LifeOS_plugin/lifeos_hook_bridge/memory_transaction.py
    1: # ABOUTME: Journals native file publication so interrupted memory operations can recover.
    2: # ABOUTME: Serializes cooperating writers and keeps temporary recovery copies private.
    3: 
    4: from contextlib import contextmanager
    5: from contextvars import ContextVar
    6: import base64
    7: import fcntl
    8: import hashlib
    9: import json
   10: import os
   11: from pathlib import Path
   12: import re
   13: import stat
   14: import tempfile
   15: 
   16: 
   17: def publish(path: Path, data: bytes, *, mode: int = 0o600) -> None:
   18:     if type(mode) is not int or not 0 <= mode <= 0o777:
   19:         raise ValueError('Atomic publication requires a supported file mode')
   20:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
   21:     descriptor, name = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=".memory-", dir=path.parent)
   22:     temporary = Path(name)
   23:     try:
   24:         with os.fdopen(descriptor, "wb") as stream:
   25:             stream.write(data)
   26:             stream.flush()
   27:             os.fchmod(stream.fileno(), mode)
   28:             os.fsync(stream.fileno())
   29:         os.replace(temporary, path)
   30:         descriptor = os.open(path.parent, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY)
   31:         try:
   32:             os.fsync(descriptor)
   33:         finally:
   34:             os.close(descriptor)
   35:     finally:
   36:         temporary.unlink(missing_ok=True)
   37: 
   38: 
   39: class MemoryTransaction:
   40:     def __init__(self, state: Path, resolve, *, moves=None):
   41:         self.state = state
   42:         self.resolve = resolve
   43:         self.moves = moves
   44:         self.journal = state / "memory-operation.json"
   45:         self._descriptor = ContextVar("memory_lock_descriptor", default=None)
   46: 
   47:     @contextmanager
   48:     def lock(self):
   49:         self.state.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True, mode=0o700)
   50:         descriptor = os.open(self.state / "memory-access.lock", os.O_CREAT | os.O_RDWR, 0o600)
   51:         token = self._descriptor.set(descriptor)
   52:         try:
   53:             fcntl.flock(descriptor, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
   54:             yield
   55:         finally:
   56:             self._descriptor.reset(token)
   57:             os.close(descriptor)
   58: 
   59:     def inherited_descriptors(self) -> tuple[int, ...]:
   60:         descriptor = self._descriptor.get()
   61:         return () if descriptor is None else (descriptor,)
   62: 
   63:     def flush_publication(self) -> None:
   64:         if not self.journal.exists():
   65:             return
   66:         for copy in json.loads(self.journal.read_text())["copies"]:
   67:             path = self.resolve(copy["path"])
   68:             if path.exists():
   69:                 descriptor = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY)
   70:                 try:
   71:                     os.fsync(descriptor)
   72:                 finally:
   73:                     os.close(descriptor)
   74:             if path.parent.exists():
   75:                 descriptor = os.open(path.parent, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY)
   76:                 try:
   77:                     os.fsync(descriptor)
   78:                 finally:
   79:                     os.close(descriptor)
   80: 
   81:     def prepare(self, writer: str, request_id: str, paths: list[str], *, expected=None) -> None:
   82:         if expected is not None and (not isinstance(expected, dict) or set(expected) != set(paths)
   83:                 or any(value is not None and (not isinstance(value, str) or re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{64}', value) is None)
   84:                        for value in expected.values())):
   85:             raise ValueError('Expected publications require one exact digest for each destination')
   86:         copies = []
   87:         for name in sorted(set(paths)):
   88:             path = self.resolve(name)
   89:             present = path.exists()
   90:             copies.append({"path": name, "data": base64.b64encode(path.read_bytes()).decode() if present else None,
   91:                            "mode": stat.S_IMODE(path.stat().st_mode) if present else None})
   92:             if expected is not None:
   93:                 copies[-1]['after_digest'] = expected[name]
   94:         publish(self.journal, json.dumps({"writer": writer, "request_id": request_id, "copies": copies}).encode())
   95: 
   96:     @staticmethod
   97:     def _move_publications(operation) -> None:
   98:         from .memory_access import MemoryUnavailable
   99:         copies = operation.get('copies')
  100:         if (not isinstance(copies, list) or not copies
  101:                 or any(not isinstance(copy, dict) or not isinstance(copy.get('after_digest'), str)
  102:                        or re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{64}', copy['after_digest']) is None for copy in copies)):
  103:             raise MemoryUnavailable('Content move recovery requires expected ledger publications')
  104: 
  105:     def prepare_moves(self, entries) -> None:
  106:         from .memory_access import MemoryUnavailable
  107:         if self.moves is None or not self.journal.exists():
  108:             raise MemoryUnavailable('Content moves require a reserved owner publication')
  109:         operation = json.loads(self.journal.read_text())
  110:         if 'moves' in operation:
  111:             raise MemoryUnavailable('Content moves already have a recovery reservation')
  112:         self._move_publications(operation)
  113:         self.moves.before(entries)
  114:         operation['moves'] = entries
  115:         publish(self.journal, json.dumps(operation).encode())
  116: 
  117:     def _check_restore(self, copy):
  118:         from .memory_access import MemoryUnavailable
  119:         if (not isinstance(copy, dict) or set(copy) not in ({'path', 'data', 'after_digest'}, {'path', 'data', 'mode', 'after_digest'})
  120:                 or not isinstance(copy['path'], str) or not copy['path']
  121:                 or copy['after_digest'] is not None and (not isinstance(copy['after_digest'], str)
  122:                     or re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{64}', copy['after_digest']) is None)
  123:                 or copy['data'] is not None and not isinstance(copy['data'], str)):
  124:             raise MemoryUnavailable('The expected publication journal has invalid artifact metadata')
  125:         try:
  126:             original = None if copy['data'] is None else hashlib.sha256(
  127:                 base64.b64decode(copy['data'], validate=True)).hexdigest()
  128:         except ValueError as error:
  129:             raise MemoryUnavailable('The expected publication journal has invalid recovery bytes') from error
  130:         path = self.resolve(copy['path'])
  131:         current = hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest() if path.exists() else None
  132:         if current not in {original, copy['after_digest']}:
  133:             raise MemoryUnavailable('Publication recovery preserves a later artifact edit')
  134: 
  135:     def _preserve_current(self, operation) -> None:
  136:         # Recovery cannot tell an interrupted write from a later owner edit, so it keeps
  137:         # every destination whose bytes differ from the journal original before it restores.
  138:         kept = {}
  139:         for copy in operation['copies']:
  140:             path = self.resolve(copy['path'])
  141:             if not path.is_file():
  142:                 continue
  143:             current = path.read_bytes()
  144:             original = None if copy['data'] is None else base64.b64decode(copy['data'], validate=True)
  145:             if current != original:
  146:                 kept[copy['path']] = current
  147:         if not kept:
  148:             return
  149:         identity = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps([operation['writer'], operation['request_id']]).encode()).hexdigest()[:32]
  150:         folder = self.state / 'recovery-preserved' / identity
  151:         files = {}
  152:         for name, data in kept.items():
  153:             digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
  154:             publish(folder / digest, data)
  155:             files[name] = digest
  156:         publish(folder / 'preserved.json', (json.dumps({
  157:             'writer': operation['writer'], 'request_id': operation['request_id'], 'files': files},
  158:             sort_keys=True, indent=2) + '\n').encode())
  159: 
  160:     def recover(self, connection) -> None:
  161:         from .memory_access import MemoryUnavailable
  162:         if not self.journal.exists():
  163:             return
  164:         try:
  165:             operation = json.loads(self.journal.read_text())
  166:         except (ValueError, UnicodeError, RecursionError) as error:
  167:             raise MemoryUnavailable('The publication recovery journal has invalid content') from error
  168:         if (not isinstance(operation, dict) or set(operation) not in ({'writer', 'request_id', 'copies'}, {'writer', 'request_id', 'copies', 'moves'})
  169:                 or not isinstance(operation['writer'], str) or not operation['writer']
  170:                 or not isinstance(operation['request_id'], str) or not 1 <= len(operation['request_id']) <= 256
  171:                 or not isinstance(operation['copies'], list)):
  172:             raise MemoryUnavailable('The publication recovery journal has invalid operation metadata')
  173:         if 'moves' in operation:
  174:             self._move_publications(operation)
  175:             if self.moves is None:
  176:                 raise MemoryUnavailable('This owner cannot recover Content moves')
  177:             self.moves.validate(operation['moves'])
  178:         names = set()
  179:         for copy in operation['copies']:
  180:             if (not isinstance(copy, dict) or set(copy) not in ({'path', 'data'}, {'path', 'data', 'after_digest'},
  181:                     {'path', 'data', 'mode'}, {'path', 'data', 'mode', 'after_digest'})
  182:                     or not isinstance(copy['path'], str) or not copy['path'] or copy['path'] in names
  183:                     or copy['data'] is not None and not isinstance(copy['data'], str)):
  184:                 raise MemoryUnavailable('The publication recovery journal has invalid recovery targets')
  185:             if 'mode' in copy and (copy['mode'] is not None if copy['data'] is None else
  186:                     type(copy['mode']) is not int or not 0 <= copy['mode'] <= 0o777):
  187:                 raise MemoryUnavailable('The publication recovery journal has invalid original permissions')
  188:             names.add(copy['path'])
  189:             try:
  190:                 if copy['data'] is not None:
  191:                     base64.b64decode(copy['data'], validate=True)
  192:             except ValueError as error:
  193:                 raise MemoryUnavailable('The publication recovery journal has invalid recovery bytes') from error
  194:             self.resolve(copy['path'])
  195:         row = connection.execute("SELECT receipt FROM operations WHERE writer=? AND request_id=?",
  196:                                  (operation["writer"], operation["request_id"])).fetchone()
  197:         if row is not None and json.loads(row["receipt"])["status"] != "unknown":
  198:             if 'moves' in operation:
  199:                 self.moves.finish(operation['moves'])
  200:             self.journal.unlink()
  201:             return
  202:         expected = any('after_digest' in copy for copy in operation['copies'])
  203:         if expected:
  204:             for copy in operation['copies']:
  205:                 self._check_restore(copy)
  206:         if 'moves' in operation:
  207:             self.moves.recovery_check(operation['moves'])
  208:         self._preserve_current(operation)
  209:         if 'moves' in operation:
  210:             self.moves.recover(operation['moves'])
  211:         for copy in operation["copies"]:
  212:             if expected:
  213:                 self._check_restore(copy)
  214:             path = self.resolve(copy["path"])
  215:             if copy["data"] is None:
  216:                 path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
  217:             else:
  218:                 publish(path, base64.b64decode(copy["data"], validate=True), mode=copy.get('mode', 0o600))
  219:         connection.execute("DELETE FROM operations WHERE writer=? AND request_id=?",
  220:                            (operation["writer"], operation["request_id"]))
  221:         connection.commit()
  222:         self.journal.unlink()
  223: 
  224:     def finish(self) -> None:
  225:         if self.journal.exists():
  226:             operation = json.loads(self.journal.read_text())
  227:             if 'moves' in operation:
  228:                 self.moves.finish(operation['moves'])
  229:         self.journal.unlink(missing_ok=True)

FILE /home/outsider/Projects/Hermes_agent/LifeOS_plugin/lifeos_hook_bridge/memory_policy.py
    1: # ABOUTME: Resolves memory permissions from host-approved identities and conversation metadata.
    2: # ABOUTME: Uses the same access rules for every messaging app and restricts unknown contexts.
    3: 
    4: from __future__ import annotations
    5: 
    6: from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
    7: import hashlib
    8: import json
    9: from typing import Any
   10: 
   11: 
   12: CATEGORIES = frozenset({"project", "principal", "assistant"})
   13: PROPOSAL_PERMISSIONS = frozenset({"create", "review", "approve", "auto_apply"})
   14: 
   15: 
   16: @dataclass(frozen=True)
   17: class SessionContext:
   18:     transport: str
   19:     author: str
   20:     destination: str
   21:     visibility: str
   22:     participants: tuple[str, ...]
   23:     model_route: str
   24:     session_id: str
   25: 
   26: 
   27: @dataclass(frozen=True)
   28: class MemoryScope:
   29:     principal: str
   30:     writer: str
   31:     read: tuple[str, ...]
   32:     write: tuple[str, ...]
   33:     projects: tuple[str, ...]
   34:     signature: str
   35:     reason: str = ""
   36:     proposals: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   37: 
   38:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
   39:         return asdict(self)
   40: 
   41: 
   42: def _strings(value: Any, label: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   43:     if not isinstance(value, list) or any(not isinstance(v, str) or not v.strip() for v in value):
   44:         raise ValueError(f"{label} must contain nonempty strings")
   45:     return tuple(sorted(set(value)))
   46: 
   47: 
   48: class MemoryPolicy:
   49:     def __init__(self, configuration: dict[str, Any]):
   50:         if configuration.get("version") != 1 or not isinstance(configuration.get("principal"), str):
   51:             raise ValueError("Unsupported memory policy or missing principal")
   52:         self.principal = configuration["principal"]
   53:         self.accounts = configuration.get("accounts", {})
   54:         destinations = configuration.get("destinations", {})
   55:         if not self.principal or not isinstance(self.accounts, dict) or not isinstance(destinations, dict):
   56:             raise ValueError("Invalid memory identity or destination policy")
   57:         if any(not isinstance(k, str) or ":" not in k or not isinstance(v, str) or not v
   58:                for k, v in self.accounts.items()):
   59:             raise ValueError("Account bindings must map qualified account IDs to principals")
   60:         self.destinations = {}
   61:         for identifier, grant in destinations.items():
   62:             if not isinstance(identifier, str) or ":" not in identifier or not isinstance(grant, dict):
   63:                 raise ValueError("Invalid destination grant")
   64:             if grant.get("visibility") not in {"private", "shared"}:
   65:                 raise ValueError("Destination visibility must be private or shared")
   66:             clean = dict(grant)
   67:             for kind in ("read", "write"):
   68:                 clean[kind] = _strings(grant.get(kind, []), kind)
   69:                 if not set(clean[kind]) <= CATEGORIES:
   70:                     raise ValueError(f"Unknown memory category in {kind}")
   71:             clean["proposals"] = _strings(grant.get("proposals", []), "proposals")
   72:             if not set(clean["proposals"]) <= PROPOSAL_PERMISSIONS:
   73:                 raise ValueError("Unknown proposal permission")
   74:             clean["participants"] = _strings(grant.get("participants", []), "participants")
   75:             clean["projects"] = _strings(grant.get("projects", ["*"]), "projects")
   76:             clean["model_routes"] = _strings(grant.get("model_routes", ["local"]), "model_routes")
   77:             self.destinations[identifier] = clean
   78:         self.revision = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(configuration, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
   79: 
   80:     def resolve(self, context: SessionContext) -> MemoryScope:
   81:         writer = f"{context.transport}:{context.author}"
   82:         principal = self.accounts.get(writer, "")
   83:         destination = self.destinations.get(f"{context.transport}:{context.destination}")
   84:         reason = ""
   85:         if not principal:
   86:             reason = "The current account has no approved identity binding"
   87:         elif destination is None:
   88:             reason = "The destination has no approved memory grant"
   89:         elif context.visibility != destination["visibility"]:
   90:             reason = "Conversation visibility is unknown or differs from its approved grant"
   91:         elif tuple(sorted(set(context.participants))) != destination["participants"]:
   92:             reason = "Conversation participants differ from the approved audience"
   93:         elif principal not in context.participants:
   94:             reason = "The authenticated author is outside the approved audience"
   95:         elif context.model_route not in destination["model_routes"]:
   96:             reason = "The model route has no approved memory grant"
   97:         read = destination["read"] if not reason else ()
   98:         write = destination["write"] if not reason else ()
   99:         projects = destination["projects"] if not reason else ()
  100:         proposals = destination["proposals"] if not reason else ()
  101:         signature = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps({
  102:             "context": asdict(context), "policy": self.revision, "principal": principal,
  103:             "read": read, "write": write, "projects": projects, "proposals": proposals,
  104:         }, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()
  105:         return MemoryScope(principal, writer, read, write, projects, signature, reason, proposals)

    1: # ABOUTME: Binds native memory access to admitted Hermes conversations and model routes.
    2: # ABOUTME: Blocks retained context after permission changes, corrections, or forgetting.
    3: from __future__ import annotations

   10: import json

   26: class MemoryAdmissionError(RuntimeError):
   27:     pass
   28: 
   29: 
   30: # Hermes imports the same installed plugin through general and provider namespaces.
   31: _binding = sys.modules.setdefault('_lifeos_memory_admission', types.ModuleType('_lifeos_memory_admission'))
   32: if not hasattr(_binding, 'context'):
   33:     _binding.context = ContextVar('lifeos_memory_admission', default=None)
   34: _BOUND = _binding.context

   36: 
   37: def _prompt_text(value: Any) -> str:
   38:     if value is None:
   39:         return ''
   40:     if isinstance(value,str):
   41:         return value
   42:     if isinstance(value,(list,tuple)):
   43:         if any(not isinstance(block,ContentMapping) for block in value):
   44:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('Memory admission requires materialized prompt blocks')
   45:         return '\n'.join(block['text'] for block in value
   46:                          if isinstance(block.get('text'),str))
   47:     raise MemoryAdmissionError('Memory admission requires materialized prompt text')
   48: 
   49: 
   50: def _request_body(request: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   51:     extra = request.get('extra_body')
   52:     if extra is None:
   53:         return request
   54:     if not isinstance(extra,ContentMapping):
   55:         raise MemoryAdmissionError('Memory admission requires a materialized request body')
   56:     # The supported SDK merges these fields after its typed request conversion.
   57:     return {**request,**extra}
   58: 
   59: 
   60: def _messages(request: dict[str, Any]):
   61:     for key in ('messages','input'):
   62:         messages = request.get(key)
   63:         if messages is None or isinstance(messages,str) and key == 'input':
   64:             continue
   65:         if not isinstance(messages,(list,tuple)) or any(not isinstance(message,ContentMapping) for message in messages):
   66:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('Memory admission requires materialized request messages')
   67:         yield from messages
   68: 
   69: 
   70: def _system_text(request: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
   71:     values = [request.get(key) for key in ('system','instructions')]
   72:     values.extend(message.get('content') for message in _messages(request)
   73:                   if message.get('role') in ('system','developer'))
   74:     for value in values:
   75:         _prompt_text(value)
   76:     return '\n'.join(text for value in values for text in _generated_text(value))
   77: 
   78: 
   79: def _generated_text(value: Any, depth: int = 0) -> list[str]:
   80:     if depth > 32:
   81:         raise MemoryAdmissionError('Memory admission requires bounded generated input')
   82:     if isinstance(value,str):
   83:         # Tool results and arguments can contain JSON with escaped claim text.
   84:         try:
   85:             decoded = json.loads(value)
   86:         except RecursionError as error:
   87:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('Memory admission requires bounded generated input') from error
   88:         except ValueError:
   89:             return [value]
   90:         return [value,*_generated_text(decoded,depth+1)]
   91:     if isinstance(value,ContentMapping):
   92:         return [text for key,item in value.items() for part in (key,item)
   93:                 for text in _generated_text(part,depth+1)]
   94:     if isinstance(value,(list,tuple)):
   95:         return [text for item in value for text in _generated_text(item,depth+1)]
   96:     if value is None or isinstance(value,(bool,int,float)):
   97:         return []
   98:     raise MemoryAdmissionError('Memory admission requires materialized generated input')

  101: class MemoryRuntime:
  102:     def __init__(self, configuration: Path, *, audience_lookup=None):
  103:         self.configuration = MemoryConfiguration(configuration)
  104:         self.audience_lookup = audience_lookup
  105:         self.key = str(configuration.absolute())
  106:         self.state_path = configuration.parent / 'lifeos-memory-contexts.json'
  107: 
  108:     def enabled(self) -> bool:
  109:         if not self.configuration.path.exists() and not self.configuration.path.is_symlink():
  110:             return False
  111:         return self.configuration.load().get('ownership_enabled', False)
  112: 
  113:     def clear(self) -> None:
  114:         bound = _BOUND.get()
  115:         if bound and bound[0] == self.key:
  116:             _BOUND.set(None)
  117: 
  118:     def context(self) -> SessionContext | None:
  119:         bound = _BOUND.get()
  120:         return bound[1] if bound and bound[0] == self.key else None
  121: 
  122:     @staticmethod
  123:     def _scope(configuration: dict[str, Any], context: SessionContext):
  124:         if context.model_route == 'unknown':
  125:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('The actual model route is unknown and cannot receive lasting memory')
  126:         scope = MemoryPolicy(configuration).resolve(context)
  127:         if scope.reason:
  128:             raise MemoryAdmissionError(scope.reason)
  129:         # The installed LifeOS identity prompt is not yet category-filtered.
  130:         if set(scope.read) != CATEGORIES or '*' not in scope.projects:
  131:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('This installed LifeOS prompt requires unrestricted owner recall. Restricted conversation prompts are not yet verified.')
  132:         return scope
  133: 
  134:     def _rendered_prompt(self, configuration: dict[str, Any], scope, connection) -> str:
  135:         path = self.configuration.path.parent/'SOUL.md'
  136:         try:
  137:             info = path.lstat()
  138:         except FileNotFoundError:
  139:             return 'absent'
  140:         if not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode) or info.st_uid != os.getuid():
  141:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('LifeOS lasting memory requires a regular owner prompt file')
  142:         content = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
  143:         memory = NativeMemory(Path(configuration['root']))
  144:         # SOUL is a current prompt, not historical learning. Compare its exact
  145:         # claims without excluding unrelated constitution text by snapshot age.
  146:         filtered = memory._filter_history(connection,scope,content,datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat())
  147:         if filtered['excluded']:
  148:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('The rendered LifeOS prompt contains a removed or superseded claim. Refresh the LifeOS prompt before starting a conversation.')
  149:         return hashlib.sha256(content.encode()).hexdigest()
  150: 
  151:     def _stamp(self, configuration: dict[str, Any], context: SessionContext, connection, user_input=None,
  152:                compression_parent=None) -> dict[str, Any]:
  153:         scope = self._scope(configuration, replace(context, session_id=''))
  154:         retained = [tuple(row) for row in connection.execute("SELECT id, revision, status FROM records WHERE status != 'active' ORDER BY id")]
  155:         applied = [tuple(row) for row in connection.execute("SELECT id, revision, status FROM proposals WHERE status IN ('accepted','edited','auto-applied') ORDER BY id")]
  156:         rendered = self._rendered_prompt(configuration,scope,connection)
  157:         generation = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps({'retained':retained,'applied':applied,'rendered':rendered}, separators=(',', ':')).encode()).hexdigest()
  158:         proposal_generation = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(applied, separators=(',', ':')).encode()).hexdigest()
  159:         stamp = {'scope': scope.signature, 'generation': generation, 'proposal_generation': proposal_generation, 'rendered': rendered,
  160:                  'context': json.loads(json.dumps(asdict(context))), 'user_input':user_input}
  161:         if compression_parent is not None:
  162:             stamp['compression_parent'] = compression_parent
  163:         return stamp
  164: 
  165:     def _states(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
  166:         if not self.state_path.exists() and not self.state_path.is_symlink():
  167:             return {}
  168:         info = self.state_path.stat()
  169:         if self.state_path.is_symlink() or info.st_uid != os.getuid() or info.st_mode & 0o077:
  170:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('Memory conversation state requires private owner permissions')
  171:         states = json.loads(self.state_path.read_text())
  172:         if not isinstance(states, dict):
  173:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('Memory conversation state is invalid')
  174:         return states
  175: 
  176:     def admit(self, metadata: Mapping[str, str], *, provider: str = '', model: str = '', base_url: str = '',
  177:               api_mode: str = '', is_first_turn: bool = False, session_id: str = '', platform: str = '', **kwargs) -> None:
  178:         if not self.enabled():
  179:             self._refuse_inactive_context(session_id or metadata.get('HERMES_SESSION_ID', ''),
  180:                                           os.environ.get('LIFEOS_MEMORY_CONTEXT', ''))
  181:             _BOUND.set(None)
  182:             return
  183:         _BOUND.set(None)
  184:         configuration = self.configuration.load()
  185:         markers = Path(configuration['root']) / 'LIFEOS/USER/CONFIG'
  186:         if not any((markers / name).exists() or (markers / name).is_symlink()
  187:                    for name in ('memory-access.json', 'memory-http.json')):
  188:             # Native code falls back to unmanaged memory reads when both markers are gone.
  189:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('LifeOS memory connection files are missing. Repair the LifeOS mount '
  190:                                        'from the plugin page before continuing.')
  191:         metadata = dict(metadata)
  192:         metadata['HERMES_SESSION_ID'] = session_id or metadata.get('HERMES_SESSION_ID', '')
  193:         if not metadata.get('HERMES_SESSION_PLATFORM'):
  194:             metadata['HERMES_SESSION_PLATFORM'] = platform
  195:         context = host_context(configuration, metadata, model_route=route_identity(provider, model, base_url, api_mode),
  196:                                hermes_home=str(self.configuration.path.parent), audience_lookup=self.audience_lookup)
  197:         self._scope(configuration, context)
  198:         if not context.session_id:
  199:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('Memory needs a host-bound conversation identifier')
  200:         memory = NativeMemory(Path(configuration['root']))
  201:         with memory._transaction() as connection:
  202:             stamp = self._stamp(configuration, context, connection,user_proof(kwargs.get('user_message')))
  203:             states = self._states()
  204:             previous = states.get(context.session_id)
  205:             if previous is None and not is_first_turn:
  206:                 previous = self._recover_compression(configuration,context,connection,states)
  207:             if isinstance(previous,dict) and 'compression_parent' in previous:
  208:                 stamp['compression_parent'] = previous['compression_parent']
  209:             if previous is not None:
  210:                 if (not isinstance(previous,dict) or not isinstance(previous.get('generation'),str)
  211:                         or any(previous.get(key)!=stamp.get(key) for key in ('scope','proposal_generation','rendered','context'))):
  212:                     raise MemoryAdmissionError('Memory permissions or current facts changed. Start a new conversation before continuing.')
  213:                 # Keep the fact generation stale until foreground projection
  214:                 # repairs readable history and final admission accepts it.
  215:                 stamp['generation'] = previous['generation']
  216:             states[context.session_id] = stamp
  217:             publish(self.state_path, (json.dumps(states, sort_keys=True) + '\n').encode())
  218:         _BOUND.set((self.key, context, stamp))
  219: 
  220:     def _recover_compression(self, configuration, context, connection, states):
  221:         from .memory_lineage import compression_parent
  222:         try:
  223:             parent_id = compression_parent(self.configuration.path.parent, context)
  224:             previous = states.get(parent_id)
  225:             if not isinstance(previous,dict) or not isinstance(previous.get('generation'),str):
  226:                 raise ValueError('The parent has no verified admission')
  227:             parent = parse_context(previous.get('context'))
  228:             if parent.session_id != parent_id or replace(parent,session_id=context.session_id) != context:
  229:                 raise ValueError('The parent authority differs from the current host')
  230:             current = self._stamp(configuration,parent,connection,previous.get('user_input'),previous.get('compression_parent'))
  231:             if any(previous.get(key)!=current.get(key) for key in ('scope','proposal_generation','rendered','context')):
  232:                 raise ValueError('The parent authority changed')
  233:             return {**previous, 'context':json.loads(json.dumps(asdict(context))), 'compression_parent':parent_id}
  234:         except (MemoryUnavailable, ValueError) as error:
  235:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('This conversation has no verified memory context. Compression recovery requires unchanged parent authority and verified native lineage.') from error
  236: 
  237:     def rotate_session(self, new_session_id: str, parent_session_id: str, *, metadata=None) -> None:
  238:         if not self.enabled():
  239:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('Memory ownership changed during compression')
  240:         metadata = current_host_metadata() if metadata is None else metadata
  241:         if (not isinstance(new_session_id,str) or not new_session_id or not isinstance(parent_session_id,str)
  242:                 or not parent_session_id or not metadata or metadata.get('HERMES_SESSION_ID') != new_session_id):
  243:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('Compression needs the current host-bound destination and verified parent')
  244:         configuration = self.configuration.load()
  245:         memory = NativeMemory(Path(configuration['root']))
  246:         with memory._transaction() as connection:
  247:             states = self._states()
  248:             previous = states.get(parent_session_id)
  249:             if not isinstance(previous,dict):
  250:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError('Compression has no verified parent memory context')
  251:             try:
  252:                 parent = parse_context(previous.get('context'))
  253:             except ValueError as error:
  254:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError('Compression has an invalid parent memory context') from error
  255:             if parent.session_id != parent_session_id:
  256:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError('Compression belongs to a different parent conversation')
  257:             context = replace(parent,session_id=new_session_id)
  258:             observed = host_context(configuration,metadata,model_route=parent.model_route,
  259:                                     hermes_home=str(self.configuration.path.parent), audience_lookup=self.audience_lookup)
  260:             if asdict(observed) != asdict(context):
  261:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError('The current compression author or destination differs from its parent')
  262:             if self._stamp(configuration,parent,connection,previous.get('user_input'),previous.get('compression_parent')) != previous:
  263:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError('Compression contains an invalidated memory context')
  264:             lineage = previous.get('compression_parent') if new_session_id == parent_session_id else parent_session_id
  265:             stamp = self._stamp(configuration,context,connection,previous.get('user_input'),lineage)
  266:             existing = states.get(new_session_id)
  267:             if existing is not None and existing != stamp:
  268:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError('Compression cannot overwrite a different conversation')
  269:             if existing is None:
  270:                 states[new_session_id] = stamp
  271:                 publish(self.state_path,(json.dumps(states,sort_keys=True)+'\n').encode())
  272:         _BOUND.set((self.key,context,stamp))
  273: 
  274:     def fork_owner_job(self, session_id: str, *, route: dict[str, str]) -> None:
  275:         """Create a separate research turn without changing the admitted owner job."""
  276:         if not isinstance(session_id, str) or not session_id.startswith('owner-research-') or len(session_id) > 128:
  277:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('Owner research needs a bounded separate session identifier')
  278:         configuration = self.configuration.load()
  279:         parent = self.context()
  280:         if (not self.enabled() or parent is None or parent.transport != 'terminal'
  281:                 or not parent.session_id.startswith('owner-job-')
  282:                 or set(self._scope(configuration, parent).write) != CATEGORIES):
  283:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('Native web research requires the selected unrestricted owner job')
  284:         memory = NativeMemory(Path(configuration['root']))
  285:         with memory._transaction() as connection:
  286:             states = self._states()
  287:             previous = states.get(parent.session_id)
  288:             if (not isinstance(previous, dict) or previous.get('context') != json.loads(json.dumps(asdict(parent)))
  289:                     or self._stamp(configuration, parent, connection, previous.get('user_input')) != previous):
  290:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError('Owner research preserves only current parent admission')
  291:             context = replace(parent, session_id=session_id, model_route=route_identity(**route))
  292:             parent_scope, child_scope = self._scope(configuration, parent), self._scope(configuration, context)
  293:             if any(getattr(parent_scope, name) != getattr(child_scope, name)
  294:                     for name in ('principal', 'writer', 'read', 'write', 'projects', 'proposals')):
  295:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError('Owner research cannot change parent permission')
  296:             stamp = self._stamp(configuration, context, connection)
  297:             if session_id in states:
  298:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError('Owner research preserves existing sessions')
  299:             states[session_id] = stamp
  300:             publish(self.state_path, (json.dumps(states, sort_keys=True) + '\n').encode())
  301:         _BOUND.set((self.key, context, stamp))
  302: 
  303:     def _refuse_inactive_context(self, session_id: str, inherited: str) -> None:
  304:         if self.context() is not None or inherited or (session_id and session_id in self._states()):
  305:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('Memory ownership changed for retained context. Start a new conversation.')
  306: 
  307:     def bind_environment(self, environment: dict[str, str], *, session_id: str = '') -> None:
  308:         inherited = environment.get('LIFEOS_MEMORY_CONTEXT', '')
  309:         environment.pop('LIFEOS_MEMORY_INTERNAL', None)
  310:         environment.pop('LIFEOS_MEMORY_CONTEXT', None)
  311:         environment['LIFEOS_MEMORY_CONFIGURATION'] = self.key
  312:         environment['HERMES_HOME'] = str(self.configuration.path.parent.absolute())
  313:         context = self.context()
  314:         environment['LIFEOS_MEMORY_SESSION'] = session_id or (context.session_id if context is not None else '')
  315:         if not self.enabled():
  316:             self._refuse_inactive_context(session_id, inherited)
  317:             return
  318:         if context is not None:
  319:             environment['LIFEOS_MEMORY_CONTEXT'] = json.dumps(asdict(context))
  320: 
  321:     def check_call(self, **kwargs) -> None:
  322:         self._check_call(**kwargs)
  323: 
  324:     def project_call(self, *, request, next_call, **kwargs):
  325:         projected = self._check_call(request=request, project=True, **kwargs)
  326:         return next_call(projected)
  327: 
  328:     def _check_call(self, *, request: dict[str, Any], provider: str = '', model: str = '', base_url: str = '',
  329:                    api_mode: str = '', session_id: str = '', platform: str = '',
  330:                    metadata: Mapping[str, str] | None = None, project: bool = False, **kwargs):
  331:         # Review forks use the main chat pipeline with a generated user-role prompt.
  332:         provenance = sys.modules.get('tools.skill_provenance')
  333:         auxiliary = bool(kwargs.get('aux_task') or provenance and provenance.is_background_review())
  334:         execution = project
  335:         project = project and not auxiliary
  336:         bound = _BOUND.get()
  337:         metadata = current_host_metadata() if metadata is None else metadata
  338:         session_id = session_id or (metadata or {}).get('HERMES_SESSION_ID', '') or os.environ.get('LIFEOS_MEMORY_SESSION', '')
  339:         if metadata is not None or platform:
  340:             metadata = dict(metadata or {})
  341:             metadata['HERMES_SESSION_ID'] = session_id
  342:             if not metadata.get('HERMES_SESSION_PLATFORM'):
  343:                 metadata['HERMES_SESSION_PLATFORM'] = platform
  344:         states = self._states()
  345:         if not self.enabled():
  346:             if (bound and bound[0] == self.key) or (session_id and session_id in states) or os.environ.get('LIFEOS_MEMORY_CONTEXT'):
  347:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError('Memory ownership changed for retained context. Start a new conversation.')
  348:             return request
  349:         configuration = self.configuration.load()
  350:         if bound is None or bound[0] != self.key:
  351:             try:
  352:                 if os.environ.get('LIFEOS_MEMORY_CONTEXT'):
  353:                     inherited = parse_context(json.loads(os.environ['LIFEOS_MEMORY_CONTEXT']))
  354:                 else:
  355:                     saved = states.get(session_id, {})
  356:                     inherited = parse_context(saved.get('context'))
  357:                     if metadata is None or not metadata.get('HERMES_SESSION_PLATFORM'):
  358:                         raise ValueError('The caller has no trusted host identity metadata')
  359:                     observed = host_context(configuration, metadata, model_route=inherited.model_route,
  360:                                             hermes_home=str(self.configuration.path.parent), audience_lookup=self.audience_lookup)
  361:                     if asdict(observed) != asdict(inherited):
  362:                         raise ValueError('The current host metadata differs from admission')
  363:                 admitted = states.get(inherited.session_id)
  364:                 if not isinstance(admitted, dict):
  365:                     raise ValueError('The inherited conversation has no recorded admission')
  366:                 bound = (self.key, inherited, admitted)
  367:             except ValueError as error:
  368:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError('This model call has no admitted memory context') from error
  369:         _, context, admitted = bound
  370:         if session_id and session_id != context.session_id and (execution or auxiliary):
  371:             child = states.get(session_id)
  372:             if (isinstance(child,dict) and child.get('compression_parent') == context.session_id
  373:                     and child.get('context') == json.loads(json.dumps(asdict(replace(context,session_id=session_id))))
  374:                     and all(child.get(key)==admitted.get(key) for key in ('scope','generation','proposal_generation','rendered'))
  375:                     and metadata and metadata.get('HERMES_SESSION_PLATFORM')):
  376:                 context = replace(context,session_id=session_id)
  377:                 admitted = child
  378:                 bound = (self.key,context,admitted)
  379:         if session_id and session_id != context.session_id:
  380:             raise MemoryAdmissionError('The model call belongs to a different conversation')
  381:         if metadata and metadata.get('HERMES_SESSION_PLATFORM'):
  382:             observed = host_context(configuration, metadata, model_route=context.model_route,
  383:                                     hermes_home=str(self.configuration.path.parent), audience_lookup=self.audience_lookup)
  384:             if asdict(observed) != asdict(context):
  385:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError('The current author or destination differs from admission')
  386:         else:
  387:             from .discord_audience import context_audience_is_current
  388:             if not context_audience_is_current(configuration, context, audience_lookup=self.audience_lookup):
  389:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError('The Discord channel audience no longer permits private memory')
  390:         body = _request_body(request)
  391:         route = route_identity(provider, body.get('model',model), base_url, api_mode)
  392:         self._scope(configuration, replace(context, model_route=route))
  393:         memory = NativeMemory(Path(configuration['root']))
  394:         with memory._transaction() as connection:
  395:             states = self._states()
  396:             saved = states.get(context.session_id)
  397:             # Prompt workers publish the next human input without changing this thread's binding.
  398:             if ((execution or auxiliary) and isinstance(saved,dict) and saved.get('user_input') is not None
  399:                     and all(saved.get(key)==admitted.get(key) for key in ('scope','generation','proposal_generation','rendered','context','compression_parent'))):
  400:                 admitted = saved
  401:                 bound = (self.key,context,admitted)
  402:             current = self._stamp(configuration, context, connection,admitted.get('user_input'),admitted.get('compression_parent'))
  403:             can_refresh = (project and saved in (admitted,current)
  404:                            and all(current.get(key)==admitted.get(key) for key in ('scope','proposal_generation','context','rendered','user_input')))
  405:             if (current != admitted or saved != admitted) and not can_refresh:
  406:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError('The model call contains an invalidated memory context. Start a new conversation.')
  407:             scope = self._scope(configuration,context)
  408:             user_input = None if auxiliary else admitted.get('user_input')
  409:             timestamp = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
  410:             excluded = lambda content: memory._filter_history(connection,scope,content,timestamp)['excluded']
  411:             if project and ('messages' in body or isinstance(body.get('input'),(list,tuple)) or current != admitted):
  412:                 try:
  413:                     projected = project_request(body,excluded,user_input)
  414:                 except ValueError as error:
  415:                     raise MemoryAdmissionError(str(error)) from error
  416:                 field = 'messages' if 'messages' in body else 'input'
  417:                 request = {**request,field:projected[field]}
  418:                 if isinstance(request.get('extra_body'),ContentMapping) and field in request['extra_body']:
  419:                     request['extra_body'] = {**request['extra_body'],field:projected[field]}
  420:                 body = _request_body(request)
  421:             content = _system_text(body)
  422:             if content and memory._filter_history(connection,self._scope(configuration,context),content,
  423:                                                   datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat())['excluded']:
  424:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError('The model system prompt contains a removed or superseded claim. Refresh the LifeOS prompt and start a new conversation.')
  425:             if auxiliary:
  426:                 content = '\n'.join(text for key in ('messages','input')
  427:                                     for text in _generated_text(body.get(key)))
  428:                 if content and memory._filter_history(connection,self._scope(configuration,context),content,
  429:                                                       datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat())['excluded']:
  430:                     label = 'compression' if kwargs.get('aux_task') == 'compression' else 'auxiliary'
  431:                     raise MemoryAdmissionError(f'The {label} prompt contains a removed or superseded claim. Rebuild the conversation before continuing.')
  432:             # Retained user turns and generated messages must not recreate a
  433:             # removed fact. The current explicit user quote remains permitted.
  434:             messages = list(_messages(body))
  435:             try:
  436:                 retained = list(enumerate(messages)) if auxiliary else retained_messages(messages,user_input)
  437:                 content = '\n'.join(text for _,message in retained
  438:                                     for text in _generated_text(message))
  439:             except ValueError as error:
  440:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError(str(error)) from error
  441:             if content and excluded(content):
  442:                 raise MemoryAdmissionError('The model history contains a removed or superseded claim. Rebuild the conversation before continuing.')
  443:             if project and current != admitted:
  444:                 states[context.session_id] = current
  445:                 publish(self.state_path,(json.dumps(states,sort_keys=True)+'\n').encode())
  446:                 bound = (self.key,context,current)
  447:         _BOUND.set(bound)
  448:         return request

FILE /home/outsider/Projects/Hermes_agent/LifeOS_plugin/lifeos_hook_bridge/memory_source_review.py
    1: # ABOUTME: Records owner review of declared native system, owner context, and Life sources.
    2: # ABOUTME: Keeps source approvals bound to current retirement state without storing another fact body.
    3: import hashlib
    4: import json
    5: from pathlib import Path
    6: import re
    7: 
    8: from .memory_access import MemoryUnavailable, _now
    9: from .memory_policy import CATEGORIES
   10: from .memory_sources import (CONTEXT_FILES, TELOS_SOURCES, TELOS_EDITOR_SOURCES, FRESHNESS_TELOS_SOURCES, is_state_source,
   11:                              SYSTEM_FILES, SYSTEM_PREFIXES, CORPUS_LIMIT,
   12:                              SOURCE_COUNT_LIMIT, _markdown_source, _text_source, authorize,
   13:                              is_evidence_source, json_projection, markdown_projection, INTERVIEW_SETUP_FILES, is_deny_source, is_sync_source)
   14: 
   15: 
   16: def _digest(value):
   17:     return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(value, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False).encode()).hexdigest()
   18: 
   19: 
   20: def _source_digest(memory, scope, relative, content):
   21:     return _digest({'root': str(memory.root), 'principal': scope.principal,
   22:                     'path': relative, 'content': content})
   23: 
   24: 
   25: def _classification(relative):
   26:     from .memory_life_source_review import classification
   27:     if relative in INTERVIEW_SETUP_FILES:
   28:         return 'interview_setup'
   29:     if is_evidence_source(relative):
   30:         return 'evidence'
   31:     if relative in CONTEXT_FILES | TELOS_SOURCES | TELOS_EDITOR_SOURCES | FRESHNESS_TELOS_SOURCES or is_state_source(relative):
   32:         return 'owner_context'
   33:     if is_deny_source(relative):
   34:         return 'deny_hashes'
   35:     if is_sync_source(relative):
   36:         return 'derived_sync'
   37:     life = classification(relative)
   38:     if life is not None:
   39:         return life
   40:     if (relative in SYSTEM_FILES or relative.startswith(SYSTEM_PREFIXES)
   41:             or re.fullmatch(r'skills/[^/.][^/]*/SKILL\.md', relative)):
   42:         return 'system'
   43:     raise ValueError('Source review supports declared system, owner context, and Life sources')
   44: 
   45: 
   46: def _retirement_digest(connection):
   47:     rows = connection.execute("""SELECT id, claim_digest, claim_words, category, project, revision, status, updated
   48:         FROM records AS retained WHERE status IN ('forgotten','superseded')
   49:         AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM records AS current WHERE current.status='active'
   50:             AND current.claim_digest=retained.claim_digest AND current.category=retained.category
   51:             AND current.project=retained.project) ORDER BY id""").fetchall()
   52:     return _digest([dict(row) for row in rows])
   53: 
   54: 
   55: def is_reviewed(memory, connection, scope, relative, content):
   56:     row = connection.execute('SELECT * FROM source_reviews WHERE principal=? AND path=?',
   57:                              (scope.principal, relative)).fetchone()
   58:     if row is None:
   59:         return False
   60:     if _classification(relative) == 'life_metadata':
   61:         from .memory_life_source_review import snapshot
   62:         content = snapshot(memory, scope, relative)[0]['content']
   63:     return (row['digest'] == _source_digest(memory, scope, relative, content)
   64:             and row['retirement_digest'] == _retirement_digest(connection))
   65: 
   66: 
   67: def _snapshot(memory, connection, scope, paths):
   68:     authorize(scope)
   69:     if not CATEGORIES <= set(scope.write):
   70:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Source review requires unrestricted owner write access')
   71:     if (not isinstance(paths, list) or not paths or len(paths) > SOURCE_COUNT_LIMIT
   72:             or any(not isinstance(path, str) for path in paths) or len(set(paths)) != len(paths)):
   73:         raise ValueError('Choose distinct installed source paths within the review limit')
   74:     sources = []
   75:     total = 0
   76:     for relative in paths:
   77:         if relative.startswith('/') or any(part in ('.', '..', '') for part in relative.split('/')):
   78:             raise ValueError('Source review needs exact installation-relative paths')
   79:         classification = _classification(relative)
   80:         from .memory_life_source_review import snapshot, projection as life_projection
   81:         source, timestamp = (snapshot(memory, scope, relative)
   82:                              if classification in {'life_text', 'life_metadata'} else
   83:                              _text_source(memory, scope, str(memory.root / relative),
   84:                                          suffix=Path(relative).suffix, interview_setup=True)
   85:                              if classification == 'interview_setup' else
   86:                              _text_source(memory, scope, str(memory.root / relative), suffix='.json', evidence=True)
   87:                              if classification == 'evidence' else
   88:                              _text_source(memory, scope, str(memory.root / relative),
   89:                                           suffix=Path(relative).suffix, deny_hashes=True)
   90:                              if classification == 'deny_hashes' else
   91:                              _text_source(memory, scope, str(memory.root / relative), suffix=Path(relative).suffix, derived_sync=True)
   92:                              if classification == 'derived_sync' else
   93:                              _markdown_source(memory, scope, str(memory.root / relative)))
   94:         projection = (life_projection(memory, relative, source['content']) if classification in {'life_text', 'life_metadata'} else
   95:                       json_projection(source['content']) if classification == 'evidence' or classification in {'deny_hashes', 'derived_sync'} and relative.endswith('.json') else
   96:                       markdown_projection(memory, relative, source['content']))
   97:         total += len(source['content'].encode()) + len((projection or '').encode())
   98:         if total > CORPUS_LIMIT:
   99:             raise MemoryUnavailable('The reviewed sources exceed their transport limit')
  100:         sources.append({**source, 'classification': classification, 'timestamp': timestamp, 'projection': projection})
  101:     checked = memory._native('validate_source_batch',
  102:         contents=[(source['projection'] or '') + '\n' + source['path'] for source in sources])['accepted']
  103:     result = []
  104:     for source, valid in zip(sources, checked, strict=True):
  105:         relative = source['relative']
  106:         labels = relative.replace('-', ' ').replace('_', ' ')
  107:         accepted = (valid is True and source['projection'] is not None and not memory._filter_history(connection, scope,
  108:             '\n'.join((source['projection'], relative, labels)), source['timestamp'], reviewed=True)['excluded'])
  109:         result.append({'path': relative, 'classification': source['classification'],
  110:                        'digest': _source_digest(memory, scope, relative, source['content']),
  111:                        'lastModified': source['lastModified'],
  112:                        'accepted': accepted, 'content': source['content'] if accepted else ''})
  113:     retirement = _retirement_digest(connection)
  114:     signature = _digest({'root': str(memory.root), 'scope': scope.signature,
  115:                          'retirement': retirement, 'sources': result})
  116:     return {'sources': result, 'signature': signature, 'retirement_digest': retirement}
  117: 
  118: 
  119: def preview(memory, scope, paths):
  120:     with memory._transaction() as connection:
  121:         return _snapshot(memory, connection, scope, paths)
  122: 
  123: 
  124: def approve(memory, scope, paths, signature, *, check_current=None):
  125:     if not isinstance(signature, str) or re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{64}', signature) is None:
  126:         raise ValueError('Provide the exact reviewed source signature')
  127:     with memory._transaction() as connection:
  128:         snapshot = _snapshot(memory, connection, scope, paths)
  129:         if check_current is not None:
  130:             check_current()
  131:         if signature != snapshot['signature']:
  132:             return {'status': 'conflict', 'reason': 'Sources or retirement state changed. Review a fresh preview.'}
  133:         if not all(source['accepted'] for source in snapshot['sources']):
  134:             return {'status': 'rejected', 'reason': 'Source review cannot admit private or known retired content.'}
  135:         unchanged = True
  136:         for source in snapshot['sources']:
  137:             row = connection.execute('SELECT digest, retirement_digest FROM source_reviews WHERE principal=? AND path=?',
  138:                                      (scope.principal, source['path'])).fetchone()
  139:             if row is not None and tuple(row) == (source['digest'], snapshot['retirement_digest']):
  140:                 continue
  141:             unchanged = False
  142:             connection.execute('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO source_reviews VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)',
  143:                 (scope.principal, source['path'], source['digest'], snapshot['retirement_digest'], scope.writer, _now()))
  144:         return {'status': 'unchanged' if unchanged else 'committed', 'reviewed_sources': len(snapshot['sources'])}

   84:         or relative in EVIDENCE_FILES) or (str(Path(relative).parent) in EVIDENCE_DIRECTORIES

   88: def json_projection(content):
   89:     try:
   90:         value = json.loads(content)
   91:         normalized = json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False)
   92:         normalized.encode()
   93:         strings = []
   94:         pending = [value]
   95:         while pending:
   96:             item = pending.pop()
   97:             if isinstance(item, str):
   98:                 strings.append(item)
   99:             elif isinstance(item, dict):
  100:                 pending.extend(item.keys())
  101:                 pending.extend(item.values())
  102:             elif isinstance(item, list):
  103:                 pending.extend(item)
  104:         return '\n'.join((normalized, *strings))
  105:     except (ValueError, RecursionError, UnicodeError):
  106:         return None

  111:             and Path(relative).name not in ('README.md', 'INDEX.md'))

  114: def authorize(scope: MemoryScope) -> dict[str, Any]:
  115:     if not CATEGORIES <= set(scope.read) or '*' not in scope.projects:
  116:         raise MemoryUnavailable('This unclassified native source requires unrestricted owner recall')

  120: def _source_path(memory, scope: MemoryScope, path: str, *, diagnostic: bool = False,
  121:                  require_file: bool = True, evidence: bool = False,
  122:                  interview_setup: bool = False, deny_hashes: bool = False, derived_sync: bool = False) -> tuple[Path, str]:
  123:     authorize(scope)
  124:     if not isinstance(path,str):
  125:         raise MemoryUnavailable('A supported native source path is required')
  126:     relative = Path(path).relative_to(memory.root).as_posix()
  127:     if '..' in Path(relative).parts:
  128:         raise MemoryUnavailable('The native source cannot leave its installed root')
  129:     system = (relative in SYSTEM_FILES or relative.startswith(SYSTEM_PREFIXES)
  130:               or re.fullmatch(r'skills/[^/.][^/]*/SKILL\.md', relative) is not None
  131:               or derived_sync and relative.startswith("LIFEOS/PULSE/pages/")
  132:               or interview_setup and relative in {'.env', 'LIFEOS/PULSE/PULSE.toml'})
  133:     if derived_sync:
  134:         directory = False
  135:         permitted = is_sync_source(relative)
  136:     elif deny_hashes:
  137:         directory = False
  138:         permitted = is_deny_source(relative)
  139:     elif interview_setup:
  140:         directory = False
  141:         permitted = relative in INTERVIEW_SETUP_FILES
  142:     elif evidence:
  143:         directory = False
  144:         permitted = is_evidence_source(relative)
  145:     elif diagnostic:
  146:         from .memory_diagnostics import DIAGNOSTIC_FILES, DIAGNOSTIC_DIRECTORIES
  147:         directory = not require_file and relative in DIAGNOSTIC_DIRECTORIES
  148:         report = not require_file and re.fullmatch(
  149:             r'LIFEOS/MEMORY/OBSERVABILITY/reports/[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9_-]*\.json', relative)
  150:         permitted = relative in DIAGNOSTIC_FILES or directory or report
  151:     else:
  152:         directory = False
  153:         permitted = (relative in FILES | LOG_FILES | CACHE_FILES | CONTEXT_FILES | TELOS_SOURCES | TELOS_EDITOR_SOURCES | FRESHNESS_TELOS_SOURCES
  154:                      or is_state_source(relative)
  155:                      or relative.startswith(PREFIXES) or system)
  156:     if not permitted:
  157:         raise MemoryUnavailable('This is not a supported native history or context source')
  158:     source = memory.root / relative if system else memory._path(relative)
  159:     physical = memory.physical_root / relative if system else memory.root.parent/'.config/LIFEOS/USER'/Path(relative).relative_to(
  160:         'LIFEOS/USER' if relative.startswith('LIFEOS/USER/') else 'LIFEOS')
  161:     if (source.resolve() != physical.absolute() or (require_file and not source.is_file())
  162:             or (not require_file and source.exists() and not (source.is_dir() if directory else source.is_file()))):
  163:         raise MemoryUnavailable('The native source is missing or changes its permitted physical path')
  164:     # Closing another descriptor for the SQLite inode releases this process's transaction locks.
  165:     if system and source.exists() and memory.database.exists() and source.samefile(memory.database):
  166:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Native memory content cannot alias its SQLite registry')
  167:     return source, relative

  170: def _source_time(info, *, milliseconds: bool = False) -> str:
  171:     # Native Bun displays floating-point millisecond Dates. Admission preserves integer microseconds.
  172:     microseconds = (int(info.st_mtime_ns / 1_000_000) * 1000 if milliseconds else info.st_mtime_ns // 1000)
  173:     return (datetime(1970, 1, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc) + timedelta(microseconds=microseconds)).isoformat()

  176: def _text_source(memory, scope: MemoryScope, path: str, *, suffix='.md', evidence=False, interview_setup=False, deny_hashes=False, preserve_newlines=True, derived_sync=False):
  177:     source, relative = _source_path(memory, scope, path, evidence=evidence, interview_setup=interview_setup, deny_hashes=deny_hashes, derived_sync=derived_sync)
  178:     if source.suffix != suffix:
  179:         raise MemoryUnavailable('The declared wiki source must be native Markdown')
  180:     before = source.stat()
  181:     if before.st_size > SOURCE_LIMIT:
  182:         raise MemoryUnavailable('The native wiki source exceeds the 256 KiB limit')
  183:     try:
  184:         with source.open('r', encoding='utf-8', newline='' if preserve_newlines else None) as stream:
  185:             content = stream.read()
  186:     except UnicodeError as error:
  187:         raise MemoryUnavailable('The native wiki source is not valid UTF-8') from error
  188:     after = source.stat()
  189:     if ((before.st_dev, before.st_ino, before.st_size, before.st_mtime_ns)
  190:             != (after.st_dev, after.st_ino, after.st_size, after.st_mtime_ns)):
  191:         raise MemoryUnavailable('The native wiki source changed during collection')
  192:     _source_path(memory, scope, path, evidence=evidence, interview_setup=interview_setup, deny_hashes=deny_hashes, derived_sync=derived_sync)
  193:     if len(content.encode()) > SOURCE_LIMIT:
  194:         raise MemoryUnavailable('The native wiki source exceeds the 256 KiB limit')
  195:     return ({'path': path, 'relative': relative, 'content': content,
  196:              'lastModified': _source_time(after, milliseconds=True)}, _source_time(after))

  199: def _markdown_source(memory, scope: MemoryScope, path: str):
  200:     return _text_source(memory, scope, path)

  202: 
  203: def markdown_projection(memory, relative, content):
  204:     if relative != 'LIFEOS/USER/DIGITAL_ASSISTANT/DA_IDENTITY.md':
  205:         return content
  206:     name = memory._native('interview_scan_name', content=content)
  207:     if set(name) != {'name'} or not isinstance(name['name'], str):
  208:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Native identity admission returns an invalid assistant name')
  209:     banner = memory._native('banner_source_projection', content=content)
  210:     if (set(banner) != {'name', 'catchphrase'} or any(not isinstance(value, str) for value in banner.values())):
  211:         raise MemoryUnavailable('Native identity admission returns invalid banner fields')
  212:     decoded = '\n'.join(banner.values())
  213:     return content + '\n' + name['name'] + '\n' + decoded if len(name['name']) <= 256 and len(decoded.encode()) <= SOURCE_LIMIT else None

  216: def _admit(memory, connection, scope, content, relative, timestamp, *, projection=None, review_content=None,
  217:            source_reviewed: bool | None = None):
  218:     from .memory_source_review import is_reviewed
  219:     labels = re.sub(r'(^|/)\d{8}-\d{6}_', r'\1', relative).replace('-', ' ').replace('_', ' ')
  220:     return memory._filter_history(connection, scope, '\n'.join((content if projection is None else projection,
  221:                                                               relative, labels)), timestamp,
  222:                                   reviewed=(is_reviewed(memory, connection, scope, relative,
  223:                                                        content if review_content is None else review_content)
  224:                                             if source_reviewed is None else source_reviewed))

  227: def source_labels(relative: str) -> str:
  228:     parts = Path(relative).parts
  229:     # Knowledge type directories name native formats, not user claims.
  230:     start = 4 if parts[:3] == ('LIFEOS', 'MEMORY', 'KNOWLEDGE') else 3
  231:     dynamic = '/'.join(parts[start:])
  232:     normalized = re.sub(r'(^|/)\d{8}-\d{6}_', r'\1', dynamic).replace('-', ' ').replace('_', ' ')
  233:     return '\n'.join((dynamic, normalized))

  235: 
  236: def source_projection(memory, relative: str, content: str) -> str:
  237:     path = memory._path(relative)
  238:     text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
  239:     front = re.match(r'^---\n.*?\n---\n', text, re.DOTALL)
  240:     return '\n'.join((front.group() if front else '', content, str(path)))

  242: 
  243: def read_markdown(memory, scope: MemoryScope, paths: list[str], *, connection=None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  244:     authorize(scope)
  245:     if len(paths) > SOURCE_COUNT_LIMIT or len(set(paths)) != len(paths):
  246:         raise MemoryUnavailable('The declared native sources exceed their count limit or repeat a path')
  247:     with (memory._transaction() if connection is None else nullcontext(connection)) as connection:
  248:         sources = []
  249:         timestamps = {}
  250:         projections = {}
  251:         total = 0
  252:         for path in paths:
  253:             source, timestamp = _markdown_source(memory, scope, path)
  254:             size = len(source['content'].encode())
  255:             total += size
  256:             if total > CORPUS_LIMIT:
  257:                 raise MemoryUnavailable('The declared native wiki sources exceed their transport limit')
  258:             timestamps[path] = timestamp
  259:             projections[path] = markdown_projection(memory, source['relative'], source['content'])
  260:             sources.append(source)
  261:         if not sources:
  262:             return []
  263:         checked = memory._native('validate_source_batch',
  264:             contents=[(projections[source['path']] or '') + '\n' + source['path'] for source in sources])['accepted']
  265:         admitted = []
  266:         for source, accepted in zip(sources, checked, strict=True):
  267:             if accepted is not True or projections[source['path']] is None:
  268:                 continue
  269:             relative = source['relative']
  270:             if not _admit(memory, connection, scope, source['content'], relative, timestamps[source['path']],
  271:                           projection=projections[source['path']])['excluded']:
  272:                 admitted.append(source)
  273:         return admitted
  274: 
  275: 
  276: def check(memory, scope: MemoryScope, path: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
  277:     with memory._transaction():
  278:         _source_path(memory,scope,path)
  279:     return {'ok':True}
  280: 
  281: 
  282: def _strings(value: Any) -> list[str]:
  283:     if isinstance(value,str):
  284:         return [value]
  285:     if isinstance(value,list):
  286:         return [text for item in value for text in _strings(item)]
  287:     if isinstance(value,dict):
  288:         return [text for key,item in value.items() for text in [key,*_strings(item)]]
  289:     return []

  291: 
  292: def _valid_log_row(row: dict[str, Any], relative: str) -> bool:
  293:     if relative.endswith('/memory-writes.jsonl'):
  294:         if not isinstance(row.get('ts'),str) or not isinstance(row.get('file'),str):
  295:             return False
  296:         try:
  297:             if datetime.fromisoformat(row['ts'].replace('Z','+00:00')).tzinfo is None:
  298:                 return False
  299:         except ValueError:
  300:             return False
  301:         return all(isinstance(row.get(key,[]),list) and all(isinstance(item,str) for item in row.get(key,[]))
  302:                    for key in ('additions','evictions'))
  303:     return (row.get('overall') in ('ok','warn','critical') and isinstance(row.get('findings',[]),list)
  304:             and all(isinstance(finding,dict) and finding.get('severity') in ('ok','warn','critical')
  305:                     and isinstance(finding.get('message'),str) for finding in row.get('findings',[])))
  306:

  308: def _health_status(row: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
  309:     status = {'overall':row['overall'], 'findings':[]}
  310:     if row['overall'] == 'critical':
  311:         status['findings'] = [{'severity':'critical',
  312:                               'message':'Memory diagnostic details are unavailable under the current policy.'}]
  313:     return json.dumps(status)

  316: def _read_log(memory, connection, scope: MemoryScope, relative: str, content: str, timestamp: str) -> str:
  317:     health = relative.endswith('/memory-health.jsonl')
  318:     lines = [line for line in content.splitlines() if line.strip()][-1 if health else -500:]
  319:     candidates, items = [], []
  320:     for line in lines:
  321:         try:
  322:             row = json.loads(line)
  323:         except ValueError:
  324:             continue
  325:         if not isinstance(row,dict) or not _valid_log_row(row,relative):
  326:             continue
  327:         text = line + '\n' + '\n'.join(_strings(row))
  328:         candidates.append((line,row,text))
  329:         items.append({'type':'idea','title':'Native retained log','content':text})
  330:     if not items:
  331:         return ''
  332:     checked = memory._native('validate_batch',items=items)['results']
  333:     rows = []
  334:     hot_categories = {path: category for category, path in HOT_FILES.items()}
  335:     current_additions = {}
  336:     for (line,row,text),item,result in zip(candidates,items,checked,strict=True):
  337:         row_timestamp = row.get('ts') if isinstance(row.get('ts'),str) else timestamp
  338:         if (result.get('ok') and result.get('item') == item
  339:                 and not memory._filter_history(connection,scope,text,row_timestamp)['excluded']):
  340:             if health:
  341:                 rows.append(line)
  342:             elif row.get('updated_by') == 'MemorySystem.add':
  343:                 additions, evictions = row.get('additions',[]), row.get('evictions',[])
  344:                 if any(re.search(r'SmokeTest|smoke-test',entry,re.IGNORECASE) for entry in additions):
  345:                     continue
  346:                 if row['file'] not in hot_categories:
  347:                     continue
  348:                 if row['file'] not in current_additions:
  349:                     # Check the whole registry/file invariant once per category, not once per fact.
  350:                     snapshot = memory._hot_snapshot(connection, hot_categories[row['file']])
  351:                     current_additions[row['file']] = set(snapshot['entries'])
  352:                 confirmed = [entry for entry in additions if entry in current_additions[row['file']]]
  353:                 # Retained audit rows cannot establish that an uncommitted fact was learned.
  354:                 if additions and not confirmed:
  355:                     continue
  356:                 additions = confirmed
  357:                 rows.append(json.dumps({'ts':row['ts'], 'file':row['file'], 'updated_by':row['updated_by'],
  358:                                         'additions':additions[:2], 'evictions':evictions[:1],
  359:                                         'additions_count':len(additions), 'evictions_count':len(evictions)}))
  360:         elif health:
  361:             rows.append(_health_status(row))
  362:     return '\n'.join(rows)
  363: 
  364: 
  365: def read(memory, scope: MemoryScope, path: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
  366:     rejected = {'ok':False, 'content':'', 'excluded':True}
  367:     with memory._transaction() as connection:
  368:         source, relative = _source_path(memory,scope,path)
  369:         if relative in CONTEXT_FILES and source.stat().st_size > 256 * 1024:
  370:             raise MemoryUnavailable('The native identity source exceeds the 256 KiB limit')
  371:         with source.open('r', encoding='utf-8', newline='') as stream:
  372:             content = stream.read()
  373:         if relative in CONTEXT_FILES and len(content.encode('utf-8')) > 256 * 1024:
  374:             raise MemoryUnavailable('The native identity source exceeds the 256 KiB limit')
  375:         timestamp = _source_time(source.stat())
  376:         projection = markdown_projection(memory, relative, content)
  377:         if projection is None:
  378:             return {**rejected, 'reason': 'Native identity admission rejected the source format'}
  379:         if relative in LOG_FILES:
  380:             return {'ok':True, 'content':_read_log(memory,connection,scope,relative,content,timestamp),
  381:                     'excluded':False, 'historical':True}
  382:         decoded = ''
  383:         if source.suffix in ('.json','.jsonl'):
  384:             values = ([json.loads(line) for line in content.splitlines() if line.strip()]
  385:                       if source.suffix == '.jsonl' else [json.loads(content)])
  386:             decoded = '\n'.join(text for value in values for text in _strings(value))
  387:         texts = list((projection, decoded) if decoded else (projection,))
  388:         checked = memory._native('validate_source_batch', contents=texts)['accepted']
  389:         if len(checked) != len(texts) or any(accepted is not True for accepted in checked):
  390:             return {**rejected, 'reason':'Native validation rejected this source text'}
  391:         filtered = _admit(memory, connection, scope, '\n'.join((content, decoded)) if decoded else content,
  392:                           relative, timestamp, projection='\n'.join((projection, decoded)) if decoded else projection)
  393:         if filtered['excluded']:
  394:             return {**rejected, 'reason':'This retained source predates a correction or contains a removed claim'}
  395:         return {'ok':True, 'content':content, 'excluded':False, 'historical':relative.startswith('LIFEOS/MEMORY/LEARNING/')}
  396: 
  397: 
  398: def filter_content(memory, scope: MemoryScope, content: str, timestamp: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
  399:     if (not CATEGORIES <= set(scope.read) or '*' not in scope.projects
  400:             or not isinstance(content,str) or not isinstance(timestamp,str)):
  401:         return {'content':'', 'excluded':True}
  402:     if memory._validate({'type':'idea','title':'Native retained context','content':content},content,'project'):
  403:         return {'content':'', 'excluded':True}
  404:     return memory.filter_history(scope,content,timestamp)

  147:                 self.assertFalse(memory.transaction.journal.exists())

    1: # Governed Atlas history retention

    3: Date: 2026-10-11. Adrian authorizes development item 2 after merging PR8.
