This development change adds no new schedule, source collector, model request, dependency, or native patch. The daily `.253` plugin remains the certified 0.2.0 release until a tested release update.
    6: 
    7: ## Retention and provenance
    8: 
    9: The default policy activates above 64 retained runs or 128 lifecycle events. It targets a recent window of 32 runs and 64 lifecycle events. It additionally preserves every source and edge observation run reference, each collector's newest run identifier, and each collector's maximum completion timestamp. Asset identifiers, observation freshness, and native sweep behavior remain intact.
   10: 
   11: The planner copies admitted database bytes into private in-memory SQLite state. It trims that copy before asking the actual native collector planner to reconcile current sources. No live file changes during planning. If the planned graph exceeds capacity, the planner tries a 16-run, 50-event window and then a window containing only protected runs. It never discards a protected run or current inventory to force a pass. An intrinsically oversized inventory or already unadmitted graph remains refused.
   12: 
   13: Before trimming reaches publication, the operation archives the exact prior database and snapshot bytes. Its manifest binds their SHA-256 values, the expected new graph and snapshot, source digests, current scope, retirement state, removal counts, and creation time. The archive identifier is the SHA-256 of that manifest without its identifier field. Current reads verify the complete manifest bytes and both preserved files.
   14: 
   15: The public snapshot adds archived counts to retained collector run and success counts. Retention does not reset those totals. Structural insight metrics continue to come from the actual current native graph. The sync result reports the archive identifier, removed counts, selected window, and actual capacity. The recent lifecycle window can shrink under capacity pressure. Exact older events remain in the archive.
   16: 
   17: ## Publication and recovery
   18: 
   19: Archive files use mode 0600 in private owner directories. Fixed digest paths refuse aliases, hard links, shared permissions, and existing archive files. The three archive files publish before the live database and snapshot. All five files share the existing owner transaction journal. Current owner authority is checked between writes. Final verification checks every output before committing the receipt.
   20: 
   21: An interrupted process restores every prior group member. A later archive or live graph edit blocks the complete recovery group. The journal remains available for review. Recovery can leave empty private archive directories; it does not remove unrelated directories.
   22: 
   23: Whole-guest backup coverage must include the complete external Atlas directory, including archives and pending governance journals. Native and profile-only backups still omit that external directory. The grouped filesystem restore test exercises archive preservation and current owner reads after restoration. It does not claim a new Proxmox Backup Server restore or a daily-server release deployment.
   24: 
   25: ## Current owner archive access
   26: 
   27: Use the identifier returned by an archiving sync:
   28: 
   29: ```sh
   30: hermes lifeos-atlas-history ARCHIVE_ID --table sync_run --offset 0
   31: hermes lifeos-atlas-history ARCHIVE_ID --table lifecycle_event --offset 0
   32: ```
   33: 
   34: The command reads at most 100 rows per page. Follow `next_offset` until it is null. It admits the selected local owner and model route without requesting inference. Read-only, disabled, revoked, malformed, and unrelated destinations fail admission. A current retirement policy can withhold old text without altering the private archive files. Archives do not enter automatic memory recall. This release retains archives and has no automatic archive deletion policy.
   35:

FILE /home/outsider/.cache/lifeos-atlas-20261010/native-limits-refresh/LifeOS/install/LIFEOS/ATLAS/Store.ts
    1: // ABOUTME: Stores the native Atlas asset graph and derives deterministic metrics.
    2: // ABOUTME: Exports bounded read transactions for current owner graph admission.
    3: /**
    4:  * Atlas Store — SQLite system of record for the LifeOS asset graph.
    5:  *
    6:  * Design (2026-07-21 cross-vendor audit):
    7:  * - bun:sqlite, WAL, STRICT tables. No daemon; Pulse reads a redacted snapshot, never the live DB.
    8:  * - Facts come only from collectors (source_observation per collector — never a singular source field).
    9:  * - Sweep invariants: only a successful, enumeration-complete FULL run may mark a collector's
   10:  *   observations unfresh; targeted runs are structurally incapable of expiring anything.
   11:  * - No secret values, ever. Credential-class assets carry references only.
   12:  *
   13:  * DB lives OUTSIDE both git repos (~/.local/state/lifeos/atlas/).
   14:  */
   15: 
   16: import { Database } from "bun:sqlite";
   17: import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, rmSync } from "node:fs";
   18: import { hasMemoryAccess } from "../TOOLS/lib/MemoryAccess";
   19: import { join } from "node:path";
   20: import { homedir, tmpdir } from "node:os";
   21: 
   22: export const ATLAS_DIR = process.env.ATLAS_DIR ?? join(homedir(), ".local/state/lifeos/atlas");
   23: export const DB_PATH = join(ATLAS_DIR, "atlas.db");
   24: export const SNAPSHOT_PATH = join(ATLAS_DIR, "snapshot.json");
   25: export const EVENTS_PATH = join(ATLAS_DIR, "events.jsonl");
   26: 
   27: /** Days without any fresh observation before an asset/edge transitions. */
   28: const STALE_AFTER_DAYS = 2;
   29: const EXPIRE_AFTER_DAYS = 14;
   30: 
   31: // ── Collector contract ────────────────────────────────────────────────
   32: 
   33: export interface AssetObs {
   34:   kind: string;
   35:   /** Canonical, provider-scoped key, e.g. "cloudflare:worker:mysite", "domain:example.com". */
   36:   key: string;
   37:   name: string;
   38:   attrs?: Record<string, unknown>;
   39: }
   40: 
   41: export interface EdgeObs {
   42:   kind: string;
   43:   srcKey: string;
   44:   dstKey: string;
   45:   /** Kinds used to auto-create shell assets when the endpoint isn't otherwise observed. */
   46:   srcKind?: string;
   47:   dstKind?: string;
   48:   attrs?: Record<string, unknown>;
   49: }
   50: 
   51: export interface CollectResult {
   52:   /** True ONLY when enumeration finished with no errors/truncation. Gates sweeping. */
   53:   complete: boolean;
   54:   assets: AssetObs[];
   55:   edges: EdgeObs[];
   56: }
   57: 
   58: export interface Collector {
   59:   name: string;
   60:   collect(scope?: string): Promise<CollectResult>;
   61: }
   62: 
   63: /**
   64:  * Edge kinds that propagate operational failure (used by blast-radius).
   65:  * HOLDS belongs here: an asset holding a credential breaks when that credential rotates,
   66:  * so `blast credential:X` correctly returns everything that needs the new value.
   67:  */
   68: export const OPERATIONAL_EDGE_KINDS = ["SERVES", "ROUTE", "CALLS", "DEPENDS_ON", "RUNS_ON", "OWNS", "DEPLOYED_FROM", "HOLDS"];
   69: 
   70: // ── Store ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   71: 
   72: const SCHEMA = `
   73: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sync_run (
   74:   id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
   75:   collector TEXT NOT NULL,
   76:   scope TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'full',
   77:   started_at TEXT NOT NULL,
   78:   finished_at TEXT,
   79:   status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'running',
   80:   complete INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
   81:   stats TEXT
   82: ) STRICT;
   83: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS asset (
   84:   id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
   85:   kind TEXT NOT NULL,
   86:   canonical_key TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
   87:   display_name TEXT NOT NULL,
   88:   status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
   89:   first_observed_at TEXT NOT NULL,
   90:   last_observed_at TEXT NOT NULL,
   91:   attrs TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}'
   92: ) STRICT;
   93: CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_asset_kind ON asset(kind);
   94: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS source_observation (
   95:   asset_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES asset(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
   96:   collector TEXT NOT NULL,
   97:   first_seen TEXT NOT NULL,
   98:   last_seen TEXT NOT NULL,
   99:   last_run_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
  100:   fresh INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  101:   attrs TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  102:   PRIMARY KEY (asset_id, collector)
  103: ) STRICT;
  104: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS edge (
  105:   id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  106:   kind TEXT NOT NULL,
  107:   src INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES asset(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  108:   dst INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES asset(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  109:   status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'active',
  110:   first_observed_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  111:   last_observed_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  112:   attrs TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
  113:   UNIQUE (kind, src, dst)
  114: ) STRICT;
  115: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS edge_observation (
  116:   edge_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES edge(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  117:   collector TEXT NOT NULL,
  118:   first_seen TEXT NOT NULL,
  119:   last_seen TEXT NOT NULL,
  120:   last_run_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
  121:   fresh INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  122:   PRIMARY KEY (edge_id, collector)
  123: ) STRICT;
  124: CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS lifecycle_event (
  125:   id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  126:   asset_id INTEGER,
  127:   event TEXT NOT NULL,
  128:   at TEXT NOT NULL,
  129:   detail TEXT
  130: ) STRICT;
  131: `;
  132: 
  133: export class Store {
  134:   db: Database;
  135: 
  136:   constructor(path: string = DB_PATH, opts: { readonly?: boolean } = {}) {
  137:     if (!opts.readonly && hasMemoryAccess()) throw new Error("Managed Atlas writes require governed synchronization");
  138:     mkdirSync(ATLAS_DIR, { recursive: true });
  139:     this.db = new Database(path, opts.readonly ? { readonly: true } : { create: true });
  140:     if (!opts.readonly) {
  141:       this.db.exec("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;");
  142:       this.db.exec("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 10000;");
  143:       this.db.exec("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;");
  144:       this.db.exec(SCHEMA);
  145:     }
  146:   }
  147: 
  148:   close() {
  149:     this.db.close();
  150:   }
  151: 
  152:   /** Apply one collector run in a single transaction. Sweeps only when eligible. */
  153:   applyRun(collector: string, scope: string, result: CollectResult): { runId: number; swept: boolean } {
  154:     const now = new Date().toISOString();
  155:     const status = result.complete ? "success" : "partial";
  156:     const insertRun = this.db.prepare(
  157:       "INSERT INTO sync_run (collector, scope, started_at, finished_at, status, complete, stats) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
  158:     );
  159:     const upsertAsset = this.db.prepare(`
  160:       INSERT INTO asset (kind, canonical_key, display_name, first_observed_at, last_observed_at, status, attrs)
  161:       VALUES ($kind, $key, $name, $now, $now, 'active', $attrs)
  162:       ON CONFLICT(canonical_key) DO UPDATE SET
  163:         display_name = $name, last_observed_at = $now, status = 'active',
  164:         attrs = CASE WHEN $attrs != '{}' THEN $attrs ELSE asset.attrs END
  165:       RETURNING id`);
  166:     const upsertObs = this.db.prepare(`
  167:       INSERT INTO source_observation (asset_id, collector, first_seen, last_seen, last_run_id, fresh, attrs)
  168:       VALUES ($aid, $collector, $now, $now, $run, 1, $attrs)
  169:       ON CONFLICT(asset_id, collector) DO UPDATE SET
  170:         last_seen = $now, last_run_id = $run, fresh = 1, attrs = $attrs`);
  171:     const upsertEdge = this.db.prepare(`
  172:       INSERT INTO edge (kind, src, dst, first_observed_at, last_observed_at, status, attrs)
  173:       VALUES ($kind, $src, $dst, $now, $now, 'active', $attrs)
  174:       ON CONFLICT(kind, src, dst) DO UPDATE SET
  175:         last_observed_at = $now, status = 'active', attrs = $attrs
  176:       RETURNING id`);
  177:     const upsertEdgeObs = this.db.prepare(`
  178:       INSERT INTO edge_observation (edge_id, collector, first_seen, last_seen, last_run_id, fresh)
  179:       VALUES ($eid, $collector, $now, $now, $run, 1)
  180:       ON CONFLICT(edge_id, collector) DO UPDATE SET
  181:         last_seen = $now, last_run_id = $run, fresh = 1`);
  182: 
  183:     let runId = 0;
  184:     let swept = false;
  185: 
  186:     const tx = this.db.transaction(() => {
  187:       runId = Number(
  188:         insertRun.run(collector, scope, now, now, status, result.complete ? 1 : 0,
  189:           JSON.stringify({ assets: result.assets.length, edges: result.edges.length })).lastInsertRowid,
  190:       );
  191: 
  192:       const idFor = new Map<string, number>();
  193:       const ensureAsset = (kind: string, key: string, name: string, attrs: Record<string, unknown> = {}): number => {
  194:         const cached = idFor.get(key);
  195:         if (cached) return cached;
  196:         const row = upsertAsset.get({ $kind: kind, $key: key, $name: name, $now: now, $attrs: JSON.stringify(attrs) }) as { id: number };
  197:         idFor.set(key, row.id);
  198:         return row.id;
  199:       };
  200: 
  201:       for (const a of result.assets) {
  202:         const aid = ensureAsset(a.kind, a.key, a.name, a.attrs ?? {});
  203:         upsertObs.run({ $aid: aid, $collector: collector, $now: now, $run: runId, $attrs: JSON.stringify(a.attrs ?? {}) });
  204:       }
  205:       for (const e of result.edges) {
  206:         const src = ensureAsset(e.srcKind ?? "unknown", e.srcKey, e.srcKey.split(":").pop() ?? e.srcKey);
  207:         const dst = ensureAsset(e.dstKind ?? "unknown", e.dstKey, e.dstKey.split(":").pop() ?? e.dstKey);
  208:         // A shell endpoint is still an observation by this collector.
  209:         upsertObs.run({ $aid: src, $collector: collector, $now: now, $run: runId, $attrs: "{}" });
  210:         upsertObs.run({ $aid: dst, $collector: collector, $now: now, $run: runId, $attrs: "{}" });
  211:         const row = upsertEdge.get({ $kind: e.kind, $src: src, $dst: dst, $now: now, $attrs: JSON.stringify(e.attrs ?? {}) }) as { id: number };
  212:         upsertEdgeObs.run({ $eid: row.id, $collector: collector, $now: now, $run: runId });
  213:       }
  214: 
  215:       // SWEEP GATE — the only path that can un-fresh observations.
  216:       if (result.complete && scope === "full") {
  217:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE source_observation SET fresh = 0 WHERE collector = ? AND last_run_id != ?").run(collector, runId);
  218:         this.db.prepare("UPDATE edge_observation SET fresh = 0 WHERE collector = ? AND last_run_id != ?").run(collector, runId);
  219:         swept = true;
  220:       }
  221: 
  222:       this.deriveStatuses(now);
  223:     });
  224:     tx();
  225:     return { runId, swept };
  226:   }
  227: 
  228:   /** Canonical lifecycle derives from ALL observations: active if ANY fresh; stale/expired by age. */
  229:   private deriveStatuses(now: string) {
  230:     const staleCutoff = new Date(Date.now() - STALE_AFTER_DAYS * 864e5).toISOString();
  231:     const expireCutoff = new Date(Date.now() - EXPIRE_AFTER_DAYS * 864e5).toISOString();
  232:     const transitions = this.db.prepare(`
  233:       SELECT a.id, a.status AS old, a.display_name,
  234:         CASE
  235:           WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM source_observation o WHERE o.asset_id = a.id AND o.fresh = 1) THEN 'active'
  236:           WHEN a.last_observed_at < ? THEN 'expired'
  237:           WHEN a.last_observed_at < ? THEN 'stale'
  238:           ELSE a.status
  239:         END AS new
  240:       FROM asset a`).all(expireCutoff, staleCutoff) as Array<{ id: number; old: string; display_name: string; new: string }>;
  241:     const setStatus = this.db.prepare("UPDATE asset SET status = ? WHERE id = ?");
  242:     const logEvent = this.db.prepare("INSERT INTO lifecycle_event (asset_id, event, at, detail) VALUES (?,?,?,?)");
  243:     for (const t of transitions) {
  244:       if (t.old !== t.new) {
  245:         setStatus.run(t.new, t.id);
  246:         logEvent.run(t.id, `${t.old}->${t.new}`, now, t.display_name);
  247:       }
  248:     }
  249:     this.db.exec(`
  250:       UPDATE edge SET status = CASE
  251:         WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM edge_observation o WHERE o.edge_id = edge.id AND o.fresh = 1) THEN 'active'
  252:         ELSE 'stale' END`);
  253:   }
  254: 
  255:   // ── Queries ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  256: 
  257:   assetByKey(key: string) {
  258:     return this.db.prepare("SELECT * FROM asset WHERE canonical_key = ?").get(key) as Record<string, unknown> | null;
  259:   }
  260: 
  261:   /** Everything reachable from `key` via outbound OWNS edges (what deleting it orphans/destroys). */
  262:   owns(key: string) {
  263:     return this.db.prepare(`
  264:       WITH RECURSIVE reach(id) AS (
  265:         SELECT id FROM asset WHERE canonical_key = ?
  266:         UNION
  267:         SELECT e.dst FROM edge e JOIN reach r ON e.src = r.id WHERE e.kind = 'OWNS' AND e.status = 'active'
  268:       )
  269:       SELECT a.kind, a.canonical_key, a.display_name, a.status FROM asset a
  270:       JOIN reach r ON a.id = r.id WHERE a.canonical_key != ?`).all(key, key) as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  271:   }
  272: 
  273:   /** Inbound blast radius: what operationally relies on `key`, directly or transitively. */
  274:   blast(key: string) {
  275:     const kinds = OPERATIONAL_EDGE_KINDS.map(() => "?").join(",");
  276:     return this.db.prepare(`
  277:       WITH RECURSIVE reach(id) AS (
  278:         SELECT id FROM asset WHERE canonical_key = ?
  279:         UNION
  280:         SELECT e.src FROM edge e JOIN reach r ON e.dst = r.id
  281:         WHERE e.kind IN (${kinds}) AND e.status = 'active'
  282:       )
  283:       SELECT a.kind, a.canonical_key, a.display_name, a.status FROM asset a
  284:       JOIN reach r ON a.id = r.id WHERE a.canonical_key != ?`)
  285:       .all(key, ...OPERATIONAL_EDGE_KINDS, key) as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  286:   }
  287: 
  288:   /**
  289:    * Credential exposure: which credentials `key` holds, directly or through what it owns.
  290:    * The incident-response direction — "this asset is compromised, what did it leak?" — as
  291:    * opposed to blast(), which answers "what breaks if I rotate this?". Traversal follows
  292:    * OWNS (a compromised host owns its services) and HOLDS (the thing that stores a secret).
  293:    */
  294:   exposed(key: string) {
  295:     return this.db.prepare(`
  296:       WITH RECURSIVE reach(id) AS (
  297:         SELECT id FROM asset WHERE canonical_key = ?
  298:         UNION
  299:         SELECT e.dst FROM edge e JOIN reach r ON e.src = r.id
  300:         WHERE e.kind IN ('OWNS','HOLDS') AND e.status = 'active'
  301:       )
  302:       SELECT a.kind, a.canonical_key, a.display_name, a.status, a.attrs FROM asset a
  303:       JOIN reach r ON a.id = r.id
  304:       WHERE a.canonical_key != ? AND a.kind = 'credential'`).all(key, key) as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  305:   }
  306: 
  307:   stale() {
  308:     return this.db.prepare(
  309:       "SELECT kind, canonical_key, display_name, status, last_observed_at FROM asset WHERE status != 'active' ORDER BY last_observed_at",
  310:     ).all() as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  311:   }
  312: 
  313:   /** Domains something actively SERVES but no security-plane target MONITORS (Bunker registration gap). */
  314:   unregistered() {
  315:     return this.db.prepare(`
  316:       SELECT a.kind, a.canonical_key, a.display_name,
  317:         (SELECT s.display_name FROM edge e2 JOIN asset s ON e2.src = s.id
  318:          WHERE e2.dst = a.id AND e2.kind = 'SERVES' AND e2.status = 'active' LIMIT 1) AS served_by
  319:       FROM asset a
  320:       WHERE a.kind = 'domain' AND a.status = 'active'
  321:         AND a.canonical_key NOT LIKE '%.workers.dev'
  322:         AND EXISTS (
  323:           SELECT 1 FROM edge e WHERE e.dst = a.id AND e.kind = 'SERVES' AND e.status = 'active')
  324:         AND NOT EXISTS (
  325:           SELECT 1 FROM edge e JOIN asset t ON e.src = t.id
  326:           WHERE e.dst = a.id AND e.kind = 'MONITORS' AND t.kind = 'target' AND e.status = 'active')
  327:       ORDER BY a.canonical_key`).all() as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  328:   }
  329: 
  330:   status() {
  331:     const collectors = this.db.prepare(`
  332:       SELECT collector, MAX(finished_at) AS last_run,
  333:         SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'success' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS successes, COUNT(*) AS runs
  334:       FROM sync_run GROUP BY collector ORDER BY collector`).all();
  335:     const assets = this.db.prepare(
  336:       "SELECT kind, status, COUNT(*) AS n FROM asset GROUP BY kind, status ORDER BY kind",
  337:     ).all();
  338:     const totals = this.db.prepare(
  339:       "SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM asset) AS assets, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM edge WHERE status='active') AS edges",
  340:     ).get();
  341:     return { collectors, assets, totals };
  342:   }
  343: 
  344:   /** Deterministic structural metrics — the factual half of the Insights tab (no inference). */
  345:   insights() {
  346:     const one = (sql: string, ...args: unknown[]) =>
  347:       (this.db.prepare(sql).get(...(args as never[])) as Record<string, number>) ?? {};
  348:     const all = (sql: string) => this.db.prepare(sql).all() as Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  349: 
  350:     return {
  351:       census: all("SELECT kind, COUNT(*) AS n FROM asset WHERE status='active' GROUP BY kind ORDER BY n DESC"),
  352:       edge_census: all("SELECT kind, COUNT(*) AS n FROM edge WHERE status='active' GROUP BY kind ORDER BY n DESC"),
  353:       // Operation: workers with NO invocation path at all — not served (custom domain
  354:       // or workers.dev), not routed, not called by another worker, no cron, no queue.
  355:       // This is the real "dead deploy" signal; a missing custom-domain alone is NOT.
  356:       unwired_workers: one(`
  357:         SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM asset a
  358:         WHERE a.kind='worker' AND a.status='active'
  359:           AND json_extract(a.attrs,'$.workers_dev') IS NOT 1
  360:           AND json_extract(a.attrs,'$.cron') IS NOT 1
  361:           AND json_extract(a.attrs,'$.queue_consumer') IS NOT 1
  362:           AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM edge e WHERE e.src=a.id AND e.kind IN ('SERVES','ROUTE') AND e.status='active')
  363:           AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM edge e WHERE e.dst=a.id AND e.kind='CALLS' AND e.status='active')`).n,
  364:       // How workers are actually wired — the honest breakdown.
  365:       worker_wiring: {
  366:         total: one("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM asset WHERE kind='worker' AND status='active'").n,
  367:         served: one("SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT src) AS n FROM edge e JOIN asset a ON e.src=a.id WHERE a.kind='worker' AND e.kind IN ('SERVES','ROUTE') AND e.status='active'").n,
  368:         workers_dev: one("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM asset WHERE kind='worker' AND status='active' AND json_extract(attrs,'$.workers_dev')=1").n,
  369:         cron: one("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM asset WHERE kind='worker' AND status='active' AND json_extract(attrs,'$.cron')=1").n,
  370:         service_binding_target: one("SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT dst) AS n FROM edge WHERE kind='CALLS' AND status='active'").n,
  371:       },
  372:       // Operation: live projects with no repo edge — provenance gap.
  373:       projects_no_repo: one(
  374:         "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM asset a WHERE a.kind='project' AND a.status='active' AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM edge e WHERE e.src=a.id AND e.kind='DEPLOYED_FROM')",
  375:       ).n,
  376:       // Interconnection: zones ranked by what they own — the blast-radius map.
  377:       blast_zones: all(
  378:         "SELECT s.display_name AS zone, COUNT(*) AS owns FROM edge e JOIN asset s ON e.src=s.id WHERE e.kind='OWNS' AND s.kind='domain' AND e.status='active' GROUP BY s.id ORDER BY owns DESC LIMIT 8",
  379:       ),
  380:       // Data-store sprawl.
  381:       data_stores: all(
  382:         "SELECT kind, COUNT(*) AS n FROM asset WHERE kind IN ('d1_database','r2_bucket','kv_namespace') AND status='active' GROUP BY kind",
  383:       ),
  384:       // Security: domains served but with no curated auth-boundary target (see unregistered() semantics).
  385:       auth_curation_gaps: this.unregistered().length,
  386:       // Security: multi-tenant/auth-bearing targets present, for context on what SHOULD be curated.
  387:       curated_targets: one("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM asset WHERE kind='target' AND status='active'").n,
  388:       stale: one("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM asset WHERE status!='active'").n,
  389:       totals: this.status().totals,
  390:     };
  391:   }
  392: 
  393:   /** Redacted snapshot for Pulse — credential-class assets are summarized, attrs stripped. */
  394:   exportSnapshot(): Record<string, unknown> {
  395:     const assets = (this.db.prepare(
  396:       "SELECT id, kind, canonical_key, display_name, status, first_observed_at, last_observed_at, attrs FROM asset",
  397:     ).all() as Array<Record<string, unknown>>).map((a) => ({
  398:       ...a,
  399:       attrs: a.kind === "credential" ? "{}" : a.attrs,
  400:     }));
  401:     const edges = this.db.prepare("SELECT kind, src, dst, status FROM edge").all();
  402:     const observations = this.db.prepare(
  403:       "SELECT asset_id, collector, fresh, last_seen FROM source_observation",
  404:     ).all();
  405:     const lifecycle = this.db.prepare(
  406:       "SELECT asset_id, event, at, detail FROM lifecycle_event ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 50",
  407:     ).all();
  408:     const unregistered = this.unregistered();
  409:     return { assets, edges, observations, lifecycle, unregistered, status: this.status(), generated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
  410:   }
  411: }
  412: 
  413: function graphState(store: Store): {graph: Record<string, unknown[]>; metrics: unknown} {
  414:   const graph: Record<string, unknown[]> = {};
  415:   for (const name of ["asset", "edge", "source_observation", "edge_observation", "lifecycle_event", "sync_run"]) {
  416:     const rows = store.db.prepare(`SELECT * FROM ${name} ORDER BY rowid LIMIT 2049`).all();
  417:     if (rows.length > 2048) throw new Error(`Atlas capacity: ${name} exceeds the 2048-row limit`);
  418:     graph[name] = rows;
  419:   }
  420:   return {graph, metrics: store.insights()};
  421: }
  422: 
  423: export function atlasGraphState(): {graph: Record<string, unknown[]>; metrics: unknown} {
  424:   const store = new Store(undefined, {readonly: true});
  425:   try {
  426:     store.db.exec("BEGIN");
  427:     return graphState(store);
  428:   } finally {
  429:     store.close();
  430:   }
  431: }
  432: 
  433: export function atlasSyncPlan(database: string | null,
  434:     runs: Array<{collector: string; scope: string; result: CollectResult}>): {
  435:       database: string; snapshot: Record<string, unknown>; graph: {graph: Record<string, unknown[]>; metrics: unknown}; runs: Array<{collector: string; runId: number; swept: boolean}>;
  436:     } {
  437:   if (hasMemoryAccess()) throw new Error("Atlas planning requires an internal owner operation");
  438:   const directory = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "lifeos-atlas-"));
  439:   const path = join(directory, "atlas.db");
  440:   try {
  441:     if (database !== null) writeFileSync(path, Buffer.from(database, "base64"), {mode: 0o600});
  442:     const store = new Store(path);
  443:     try {
  444:       const applied = runs.map(run => ({collector: run.collector, ...store.applyRun(run.collector, run.scope, run.result)}));
  445:       const graph = graphState(store);
  446:       const snapshot = store.exportSnapshot();
  447:       store.db.exec("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)");
  448:       store.db.exec("PRAGMA journal_mode=DELETE");
  449:       return {database: readFileSync(path).toString("base64"), snapshot, graph, runs: applied};
  450:     } finally { store.close(); }
  451:   } finally { rmSync(directory, {recursive: true, force: true}); }
  452: }

FILE /home/outsider/.cache/lifeos-atlas-20261010/native-limits-refresh/LifeOS/install/LIFEOS/ATLAS/collectors/Gear.ts
    1: // ABOUTME: Collects native Atlas assets from the declared owner Markdown source.
    2: // ABOUTME: Reads managed sources through current owner memory admission.
    3: /**
    4:  * Gear collector — physical estate from GEAR.md (## section = category, bold-led bullets = items).
    5:  */
    6: 
    7: import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
    8: import { memoryAccess, memoryObject } from "../../TOOLS/lib/MemoryAccess";
    9: import { join } from "node:path";
   10: import { homedir } from "node:os";
   11: import type { AssetObs, CollectResult, Collector } from "../Store";
   12: 
   13: const GEAR_PATH = join(homedir(), ".claude/LIFEOS/USER/GEAR.md");
   14: 
   15: export function collectGear(md: string | null): CollectResult {
   16:   // Absent source ⇒ degrade, never throw (ratchet gate 3). `atlas sync` runs
   17:   // every collector and turns the failure count into its exit code, so throwing
   18:   // because this machine simply lacks the source makes sync exit non-zero forever
   19:   // on most public installs. A source that EXISTS but is malformed still throws.
   20:   if (md === null) return {complete: false, assets: [], edges: []};
   21:     const assets: AssetObs[] = [];
   22:     let category = "uncategorized";
   23:     for (const line of md.split("\n")) {
   24:       const h = line.match(/^##\s+(.+)/);
   25:       if (h) {
   26:         category = h[1].replace(/[^\w\s/&-]/gu, "").trim();
   27:         continue;
   28:       }
   29:       // Items are table rows: | **Item** | Model / Details | Use |
   30:       const row = line.match(/^\|\s*\*\*([^*]+)\*\*\s*\|([^|]+)\|/);
   31:       if (row) {
   32:         const item = row[1].trim();
   33:         const model = row[2].trim();
   34:         if (!item || !model || /^-+$/.test(model)) continue;
   35:         assets.push({
   36:           kind: "device",
   37:           key: `gear:${`${item} ${model}`.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").slice(0, 80)}`,
   38:           name: model,
   39:           attrs: { category, role: item },
   40:         });
   41:       }
   42:     }
   43:     return { complete: true, assets, edges: [] };
   44: }
   45: 
   46: function collectResult(value: unknown): value is CollectResult {
   47:   return memoryObject(value) && typeof value.complete === "boolean" && Array.isArray(value.assets)
   48:     && value.assets.every(asset => memoryObject(asset) && typeof asset.kind === "string"
   49:       && typeof asset.key === "string" && typeof asset.name === "string" && (asset.attrs === undefined || memoryObject(asset.attrs)))
   50:     && Array.isArray(value.edges) && value.edges.every(edge => memoryObject(edge) && typeof edge.kind === "string"
   51:       && typeof edge.srcKey === "string" && typeof edge.dstKey === "string");
   52: }
   53: 
   54: export const gear: Collector = {
   55:   name: "gear",
   56:   async collect(): Promise<CollectResult> {
   57:     const admitted = memoryAccess("atlas_collect", {collector: "gear"}, memoryObject);
   58:     if (admitted !== undefined) {
   59:       if (admitted.ok !== true || !collectResult(admitted.result)) throw new Error("Atlas collector admission is unavailable");
   60:       return admitted.result;
   61:     }
   62:     return collectGear(existsSync(GEAR_PATH) ? readFileSync(GEAR_PATH, "utf8") : null);
   63:   },
   64: };

    1: // ABOUTME: Collects native Atlas assets from the declared owner Markdown source.
    2: // ABOUTME: Reads managed sources through current owner memory admission.
    3: /**
    4:  * Projects collector — parses PROJECTS.md's main table (deployed/live apps routing table).
    5:  * Observes projects and links them to the domains they serve and repos they deploy from.
    6:  */
    7: 
    8: import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
    9: import { memoryAccess, memoryObject } from "../../TOOLS/lib/MemoryAccess";
   10: import { join } from "node:path";
   11: import { homedir } from "node:os";
   12: import type { AssetObs, CollectResult, Collector, EdgeObs } from "../Store";
   13: 
   14: const PROJECTS_PATH = join(homedir(), ".claude/LIFEOS/USER/PROJECTS.md");
   15: 
   16: const strip = (s: string) => s.replace(/\*\*/g, "").replace(/[🟡🟢🎯🚨⚠️]/gu, "").trim();
   17: 
   18: export function collectProjects(md: string | null): CollectResult {
   19:   // Absent source ⇒ degrade, never throw (ratchet gate 3). `atlas sync` runs
   20:   // every collector and turns the failure count into its exit code, so throwing
   21:   // because this machine simply lacks the source makes sync exit non-zero forever
   22:   // on most public installs. A source that EXISTS but is malformed still throws.
   23:   if (md === null) return {complete: false, assets: [], edges: []};
   24:     const assets: AssetObs[] = [];
   25:     const edges: EdgeObs[] = [];
   26: 
   27:     // Main table only (stop at "## Routing Aliases").
   28:     const main = md.split(/^## Routing Aliases/m)[0];
   29:     const rows = main.split("\n").filter((l) => /^\|/.test(l)).slice(2); // drop header + separator
   30: 
   31:     for (const row of rows) {
   32:       const cells = row.split("|").map((c) => c.trim()).filter((_, i, a) => i > 0 && i < a.length - 1);
   33:       if (cells.length < 4) continue;
   34:       const name = strip(cells[0].split("(")[0]).replace(/\s+WIP.*$/, "").trim();
   35:       if (!name) continue;
   36:       const key = `project:${name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")}`;
   37:       assets.push({ kind: "project", key, name, attrs: { path: strip(cells[1]).slice(0, 200), deploy: strip(cells[3]).slice(0, 120) } });
   38: 
   39:       // URL column → served domains (skip localhost, ssh hosts, parenthetical notes).
   40:       const urlCell = cells[2] ?? "";
   41:       for (const m of urlCell.matchAll(/([a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*(?:\.[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*)+)/gi)) {
   42:         const host = m[1].toLowerCase();
   43:         if (host.includes("localhost") || host.endsWith(".lan") || host.startsWith("github.com")) continue;
   44:         if (host === "github.com" || host.endsWith(".workers.dev") === false && !host.includes(".")) continue;
   45:         edges.push({ kind: "SERVES", srcKey: key, dstKey: `domain:${host}`, srcKind: "project", dstKind: "domain" });
   46:       }
   47:       // github.com/owner/repo anywhere in the row → DEPLOYED_FROM.
   48:       for (const m of row.matchAll(/github\.com\/([\w.-]+)\/([\w.-]+)/g)) {
   49:         edges.push({
   50:           kind: "DEPLOYED_FROM",
   51:           srcKey: key,
   52:           dstKey: `github:repo:${m[1]}/${m[2].replace(/\.git$/, "")}`,
   53:           srcKind: "project",
   54:           dstKind: "repo",
   55:         });
   56:       }
   57:     }
   58:     return { complete: true, assets, edges };
   59: }
   60: 
   61: function collectResult(value: unknown): value is CollectResult {
   62:   return memoryObject(value) && typeof value.complete === "boolean" && Array.isArray(value.assets)
   63:     && value.assets.every(asset => memoryObject(asset) && typeof asset.kind === "string"
   64:       && typeof asset.key === "string" && typeof asset.name === "string" && (asset.attrs === undefined || memoryObject(asset.attrs)))
   65:     && Array.isArray(value.edges) && value.edges.every(edge => memoryObject(edge) && typeof edge.kind === "string"
   66:       && typeof edge.srcKey === "string" && typeof edge.dstKey === "string");
   67: }
   68: 
   69: export const projects: Collector = {
   70:   name: "projects",
   71:   async collect(): Promise<CollectResult> {
   72:     const admitted = memoryAccess("atlas_collect", {collector: "projects"}, memoryObject);
   73:     if (admitted !== undefined) {
   74:       if (admitted.ok !== true || !collectResult(admitted.result)) throw new Error("Atlas collector admission is unavailable");
   75:       return admitted.result;
   76:     }
   77:     return collectProjects(existsSync(PROJECTS_PATH) ? readFileSync(PROJECTS_PATH, "utf8") : null);
   78:   },
   79: };

   55: // ── Metrics + inference ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   56: 
   57: const METRICS_TIMEOUT_MS = 30_000;
   58: 
   59: async function metrics(): Promise<Record<string, unknown> | null> {
   60:   const proc = Bun.spawn(["bun", ATLAS_CLI, "insights"], { stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe" });
   61:   // Drain stdout AND stderr concurrently, under a kill timer (same class as
   62:   // modules/work.ts): stderr was piped but never read, so a chatty `atlas
   63:   // insights` failure fills the OS pipe buffer, the child blocks on the write,
   64:   // and with no timeout the request hung forever while wedged children piled
   65:   // up on every uncached poll.
   66:   // ported from public PR #1736, @elhoim
   67:   const timer = setTimeout(() => { try { proc.kill(); } catch { /* already gone */ } }, METRICS_TIMEOUT_MS);
   68:   const [out, err, code] = await Promise.all([
   69:     new Response(proc.stdout).text(),
   70:     new Response(proc.stderr).text(),
   71:     proc.exited,
   72:   ]);
   73:   clearTimeout(timer);
   74:   if (code !== 0 || !out.trim()) {
   75:     console.log(`[${MODULE_NAME}] metrics failed (exit ${code}): ${err.slice(0, 200)}`);
   76:     return null;
   77:   }
   78:   try {
   79:     return JSON.parse(out);
   80:   } catch {
   81:     return null;
   82:   }
   83: }
   84: 
   85: function metricHash(m: Record<string, unknown>): string {
   86:   return createHash("sha256").update(JSON.stringify(m)).digest("hex").slice(0, 16);
   87: }
   88: 
   89: const SYSTEM_PROMPT = `You are writing the Insights panel for Atlas, the LifeOS asset graph — the graph of everything the principal's system owns (Cloudflare workers, domains, DNS, repos, projects, data stores, security-scanner targets, services, devices). You are given the current deterministic structural metrics as JSON.
   90: 
   91: Write a sharp, specific analysis in plain Paul-Graham-plain language across EXACTLY these three sections:
   92: 
   93: ## Running the system
   94: Describe operational shape from the metrics. worker_wiring shows how workers are actually invoked — served (custom domain / workers.dev / route), cron, or service-binding target; most workers serve traffic one of these ways. unwired_workers is the count with NO invocation path at all — that is the only "dead deploy" signal, and it is usually small. Report worker_wiring honestly; do NOT call a worker unused just because it lacks a custom domain. Mention data-store counts as capacity, not waste.
   95: 
   96: ## Security
   97: What the graph reveals about coverage: auth_curation_gaps domains are served but have no curated auth-boundary target — note these are still hygiene-scanned hourly and that the gap matters for auth-bearing apps, not static sites. Give the ratio of curated targets to total domains. Flag concentration risk only if a metric supports it.
   98: 
   99: ## How things interconnect
  100: What the edge structure shows: which zones carry the most blast radius (blast_zones — deleting them orphans the most owned records), and where convergence/single-points-of-failure sit (e.g. RUNS_ON onto one machine, CALLS fan-in). Name the top zones and their owned counts.
  101: 
  102: Rules: 200-320 words. Every sentence tied to a number in the data. CRITICAL: never state a worker/asset is "unused", "dead", "orphaned", or "burning money" unless unwired_workers or a stale count backs it — absence of one edge type is not absence of use. No marketing register, no dramatization, no filler. Output ONLY markdown — no preamble, no closing notes.`;
  103: 
  104: async function inference(userJson: string, managed = false): Promise<string | null> {
  105:   const proc = Bun.spawn(
  106:     ["bun", INFERENCE, "--level", "high", "--timeout", "120000", SYSTEM_PROMPT, userJson],
  107:     { stdout: "pipe", stderr: "pipe" },
  108:   );
  109:   const [out, err, code] = await Promise.all([
  110:     new Response(proc.stdout).text(),
  111:     new Response(proc.stderr).text(),
  112:     proc.exited,
  113:   ]);
  114:   if (code !== 0 || !out.trim()) {
  115:     console.log(managed ? `[${MODULE_NAME}] inference unavailable` : `[${MODULE_NAME}] inference failed (exit ${code}): ${err.slice(0, 200)}`);
  116:     return null;
  117:   }
  118:   const paras = out.trim().split(/\n{2,}/);
  119:   while (paras.length > 1 && /^[*_(\s]*Note[:\s]/i.test(paras[paras.length - 1])) paras.pop();
  120:   return paras.join("\n\n").trim();
  121: }

  123: export function atlasInsightPlan(metrics: Record<string, unknown> | null): {metrics: Record<string, unknown> | null; hash: string | null} {
  124:   return {metrics, hash: metrics === null ? null : metricHash(metrics)};

  127: export async function buildAtlasInsights(): Promise<unknown> {
  128:   const prepared = memoryAccess("atlas_insight_prepare", {}, memoryObject);
  129:   if (!memoryObject(prepared) || prepared.ok !== true || typeof prepared.signature !== "string"
  130:       || !/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(prepared.signature) || !memoryObject(prepared.plan)
  131:       || !(prepared.plan.metrics === null || memoryObject(prepared.plan.metrics))
  132:       || !(prepared.plan.hash === null || typeof prepared.plan.hash === "string" && /^[0-9a-f]{16}$/.test(prepared.plan.hash))) {
  133:     throw new Error("Atlas generation requires admitted current owner metrics");
  134:   }
  135:   const check = (): void => {
  136:     const current = memoryAccess("atlas_insight_check", {signature: prepared.signature}, memoryObject);
  137:     if (!memoryObject(current) || current.ok !== true) throw new Error("Atlas generation authority or sources changed");
  138:   };
  139:   check();
  140:   if (prepared.plan.metrics === null) return {available: false, error: "run an atlas sync first"};
  141:   if (memoryObject(prepared.plan.metrics.totals) && prepared.plan.metrics.totals.assets === 0) return {available: true, empty: true, narrative: null};
  142:   const narrative = await inference(JSON.stringify(prepared.plan.metrics), true);
  143:   check();
  144:   if (narrative === null) return {available: false, error: "Atlas inference is unavailable"};
  145:   const value = {hash: prepared.plan.hash, narrative, generated_at: new Date().toISOString()};
  146:   const published = memoryAccess("atlas_insight_publish", {signature: prepared.signature, value}, memoryObject);
  147:   if (!memoryObject(published) || published.ok !== true || !memoryObject(published.value)) {
  148:     throw new Error("Atlas generation publication requires current owner authority");
  149:   }
  150:   return published.value;
  151: }

  155:   catch { console.error("Atlas insight unavailable"); process.exit(2); }
  156: }

  158: function readCache(): { hash: string; narrative: string; generated_at: string } | null {
  159:   if (!existsSync(INSIGHTS_CACHE)) return null;
  160:   try {
  161:     return JSON.parse(readFileSync(INSIGHTS_CACHE, "utf8"));
  162:   } catch {
  163:     return null;
  164:   }
  165: }
  166: 
  167: async function regenerate(m: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void> {
  168:   if (state.generating) return;
  169:   state.generating = true;
  170:   try {
  171:     const narrative = await inference(JSON.stringify(m));
  172:     if (narrative) {
  173:       mkdirSync(join(LIFEOS_DIR, "MEMORY", "STATE"), { recursive: true });
  174:       writeFileSync(
  175:         INSIGHTS_CACHE,
  176:         JSON.stringify({ hash: metricHash(m), narrative, generated_at: new Date().toISOString() }, null, 2),
  177:       );
  178:     }
  179:   } finally {
  180:     state.generating = false;
  181:   }
  182: }
  183: 
  184: // ── Request handling ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
  185: 
  186: export function renderAtlasSnapshot(snapshot: {content: string; modified_ms: number} | null): Response {
  187:   if (!snapshot) return Response.json({available: false, error: "no snapshot, run the Atlas sync command"});
  188:   const value: unknown = JSON.parse(snapshot.content);
  189:   if (!memoryObject(value)) throw new Error("The Atlas snapshot requires a JSON object");
  190:   return Response.json({available: true, snapshot_age_ms: Date.now() - snapshot.modified_ms, ...value});
  191: }
  192: 
  193: export function renderAtlasInsights(metrics: Record<string, unknown> | null, cacheContent: string | null): Response {
  194:   if (!metrics) return Response.json({available: false, error: "run an atlas sync first"});
  195:   const cache: unknown = cacheContent === null ? null : JSON.parse(cacheContent);
  196:   if (!(cache === null || memoryObject(cache) && typeof cache.hash === "string"
  197:       && typeof cache.narrative === "string" && typeof cache.generated_at === "string")) {
  198:     throw new Error("The Atlas narrative cache requires its declared fields");
  199:   }
  200:   const empty = memoryObject(metrics.totals) && metrics.totals.assets === 0;
  201:   return Response.json({available: true, metrics, narrative: empty ? null : cache?.narrative ?? null,
  202:     narrative_generated_at: empty ? null : cache?.generated_at ?? null, stale: !empty && (!cache || cache.hash !== metricHash(metrics)), generating: false});
  203: }
  204: 
  205: async function startManagedRegeneration(req: Request): Promise<Response> {
  206: const admitted = await memoryHTTPResponseAsync(req, "atlas_job");
  207: if (admitted.status !== 200) return admitted;
  208: const value: unknown = await admitted.json();
  209: if (!memoryObject(value) || Object.keys(value).join(",") !== "configuration_revision"
  210:     || typeof value.configuration_revision !== "string" || !/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(value.configuration_revision)
  211:     || req.signal.aborted) throw new Error("Atlas generation preparation is unavailable");
  212: if (state.generating) return Response.json({generating: true, alreadyRunning: true}, {status: 202, headers: admitted.headers});
  213: insightJob = startOwnerMemoryJob("atlas-insights", value.configuration_revision, () => {
  214:   state.generating = false;
  215:   insightJob = null;
  216: });
  217: state.generating = true;
  218: return Response.json({generating: true}, {status: 202, headers: admitted.headers});
  219: }
  220: 
  221: export async function handleRequest(req: Request, pathname: string): Promise<Response | null> {
  222:   if (hasManagedMemoryHTTP() && ["/api/atlas", "/api/atlas/insights", "/api/atlas/insights/regenerate"].includes(pathname)) {
  223:     const headers = {"cache-control": "no-store"};
  224:     if (SNAPSHOT_PATH !== join(homedir(), ".local/state/lifeos/atlas/snapshot.json")
  225:         || CLAUDE_DIR !== join(homedir(), ".claude")) {
  226:       return Response.json({error: "Atlas sources are unavailable"}, {status: 503, headers});
  227:     }
  228:     if (req.method !== (pathname === "/api/atlas/insights/regenerate" ? "POST" : "GET")) return Response.json({error: "Atlas snapshots require GET"}, {status: 405, headers});
  229:     const url = new URL(req.url);
  230:     if (url.search) return Response.json({error: "Atlas snapshots require a fixed route"}, {status: 400, headers});
  231:     const origin = req.headers.get("origin");
  232:     if (origin && origin !== url.origin) return Response.json({error: "Atlas snapshots require the current origin"}, {status: 403, headers});
  233:     try {
  234:       if (pathname === "/api/atlas/insights/regenerate") {
  235:         return await startManagedRegeneration(req);
  236:       }
  237:       let response = await memoryHTTPResponseAsync(req, "life", pathname);
  238:       if (response.status === 200 && pathname === "/api/atlas/insights") {
  239:         let value: unknown = await response.json();
  240:         if (!memoryObject(value)) throw new Error("Atlas insights require a declared response");
  241:         if (value.available === true && value.stale === true && !state.generating) {
  242:           const started = await startManagedRegeneration(req);
  243:           if (started.status !== 202 && started.status !== 503) return started;
  244:           // Admission can change while the owner job is prepared.
  245:           response = await memoryHTTPResponseAsync(req, "life", pathname);
  246:           if (response.status !== 200) return response;
  247:           value = await response.json();
  248:           if (!memoryObject(value)) throw new Error("Atlas insights require a declared response");
  249:         }
  250:         return Response.json(value.available === true ? {...value, generating: state.generating} : value,
  251:           {headers: response.headers});
  252:       }
  253:       return response;
  254:     }
  255:     catch { return Response.json({error: "Authenticated memory is unavailable"}, {status: 503, headers}); }
  256:   }
  257:   if (hasManagedMemoryHTTP() && pathname.startsWith("/api/atlas/") && ![
  258:       "/api/atlas/insights", "/api/atlas/insights/regenerate"].includes(pathname)) {
  259:     return Response.json({error: "Choose a declared Atlas route"}, {status: 404, headers: {"cache-control": "no-store"}});
  260:   }
  261: 
  262:   if (pathname === "/api/atlas/insights/regenerate" && req.method === "POST") {
  263:     const m = await metrics();
  264:     if (!m) return Response.json({ error: "metrics unavailable" }, { status: 503 });
  265:     regenerate(m).catch((e) => console.log(`[${MODULE_NAME}] regen: ${e}`));
  266:     return Response.json({ generating: true }, { status: 202 });
  267:   }
  268: 
  269:   if (pathname === "/api/atlas/insights") {
  270:     const m = await metrics();
  271:     if (!m) return Response.json({ available: false, error: "run an atlas sync first" });
  272:     const hash = metricHash(m);
  273:     const cache = readCache();
  274:     const stale = !cache || cache.hash !== hash;
  275:     // Fire-and-forget regeneration the moment the graph has moved on.
  276:     if (stale && !state.generating) regenerate(m).catch(() => {});
  277:     return Response.json({
  278:       available: true,
  279:       metrics: m,
  280:       narrative: cache?.narrative ?? null,
  281:       narrative_generated_at: cache?.generated_at ?? null,
  282:       stale,
  283:       generating: state.generating,
  284:     });
  285:   }
  286: 
  287:   if (pathname !== "/api/atlas") return null;
  288: 
  289:   const now = Date.now();
  290:   if (state.cached && now - state.cachedAt < CACHE_MS) {
  291:     return new Response(state.cached, { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
  292:   }
  293:   try {
  294:     if (!existsSync(SNAPSHOT_PATH)) {
  295:       return Response.json({ available: false, error: "no snapshot — run: bun ~/.claude/LIFEOS/ATLAS/Atlas.ts sync" });
  296:     }
  297:     const snapshot = JSON.parse(readFileSync(SNAPSHOT_PATH, "utf8")) as Record<string, unknown>;
  298:     const body = JSON.stringify({ available: true, snapshot_age_ms: now - statSync(SNAPSHOT_PATH).mtimeMs, ...snapshot });
  299:     state.cached = body;
  300:     state.cachedAt = now;
  301:     return new Response(body, { headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" } });
  302:   } catch (err) {
  303:     return Response.json({ available: false, error: String(err) });
  304:   }
  305: }
